# Architecture Traceability Index
Last Updated: 2026-10-28
Engine: Godot 4.7.2 (GDScript)
Source: `/architecture-review` full mode; requirement IDs registered in `docs/architecture/tr-registry.yaml`.

## Coverage Summary
- Total requirements: 1136 (794 from `tr-baseline.md`, 342 from this review's Acceptance Criteria completeness pass)
- Covered (Accepted ADR): 0 (0.0%). No ADR is Accepted yet.
- Covered (Proposed ADR): 50 (4.4%)
- Partial: 48 (4.2%). 10 are claimed by a Proposed ADR that covers only part of them; 38 are planned for ADR-0001/0002/0003, which exist but do not address them.
- Gaps: 1038 (91.4%). Their planned ADR has not been written.

Status key: 🟡 Covered (Proposed): a Proposed ADR addresses it (not a pass state until Accepted). ⚠️ Partial: an ADR exists but covers it only in part or not at all. ❌ Gap: no ADR yet. `Planned ADR` comes from `architecture.md` § Required ADRs.

## Full Matrix

### Command & Simulation Layer (`cmd`)
GDD: `design/gdd/command-simulation-layer.md` | 53 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-cmd-001 | The simulation advances only in fixed steps of 1/TICK_RATE (TICK_RATE = 60 Hz, set-once) simulated seconds; all durations are integer ticks and the kernel never reads wall-clock time or `delta`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-002 | A `TickDriver` (RefCounted, no scene tree) owns an integer accumulator in microseconds x TICK_RATE (TICK_UNITS = 1,000,000 per tick), is driven from exactly one PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS node's `_process`, and runs at most MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME (4) ticks per frame; the physics step is never the clock. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-003 | Lost time is dropped at end of frame and never fast-forwarded (LAG_FORGIVE_TICKS = 0); the accumulator is cleared on every transition into RUNNING and re-reads kernel state after each `step()`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-004 | Per-frame order is fixed by process priority: input adapter (0), TickDriver (1: boundary service, tick loop, forgiveness, result flush, paused service), presentation nodes (2), with a lint on process_priority values. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-005 | Kernel, driver, and every owner callable run on the main thread only; no Thread, WorkerThreadPool, or call_deferred into kernel code (lint-enforced). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-006 | Each tick executes phases P0 Begin, P1 Intake, P2 Dispatch, P3 Step, P4 Commit, P5 Notify, P6 Boundary services in order with no interleaving; subscribers see only post-P4 state. | Core | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-007 | Step hooks register (hook_id, owner, divisor, offset, after[]); the kernel topologically sorts them (ties by registration order then hook_id), a cycle or unknown id fails boot, and an `after` edge across non-coinciding hooks must declare `tolerates_stale`. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-008 | Every state change enters as a plain-data command with kernel-stamped `issuer_peer`, `origin` (PLAYER/SYSTEM), `seq`, `submission_id`; these are never payload-borne and no constructor without `issuer_peer` exists. | Core | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-009 | `submit()` is fire-and-forget (returns nothing, calls nothing); each command yields exactly one terminal result record (Applied/Rejected/Dropped/Faulted/Superseded) buffered and flushed at P5 and once per rendered frame in every kernel state; results carry reason codes, never display strings. | Core | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-010 | Command payloads pass a recursive Variant.Type whitelist (finite floats, depth <= 8, uniform dict key type, no Object/Callable/NodePath/RID) and `var_to_bytes(payload).size()` <= MAX_PAYLOAD (4 KiB) or are rejected MALFORMED. | Data | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-011 | Block, command-type, hook, diagnostics-channel, and binding-source registration is legal only in BOOTING and frozen after; duplicate IDs, missing mandatory fields (allowed_origins, actor_binding, coalesce, max_age_ticks, class, gameplay, repl_hint, persist), hook cycles, and ACTOR_SOURCE_INVALID fail boot in every build. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-012 | The kernel authority-checks every command at P2: origin allowed for type, and for `actor_binding = OWNED_BY_ISSUER` that (actor_source, actor) is in the binding table half_b of the issuer's PlayerId; single-player is host peer 1 -> PlayerId 1 with the same checks. | Core | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-013 | The kernel rebuilds binding-table half (b) (PlayerId -> {(source block_id, entity_id)}) itself as the last step of every LOADING pass from registered binding sources in ascending block_id order; half (b) is never saved and conflicts fault LOADING. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 + ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0016) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-014 | Each state block has a single owner and registers schema_version, class (SIM/PERCEPTION_OUT), persist and persist_scope (WORLD/PROFILE), scope (SHARED/PER_PLAYER by PlayerId), gameplay, interpolated, repl_hint, and snapshot/restore/default_state/post_restore/read_view callables; persistent per-player state is keyed by PlayerId, not peer_id. | Data | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-015 | Entity IDs are allocated by the owning block's own counter and are unique only within the block; cross-block references are keyed (block_id, entity_id) and the kernel allocates none. | Data | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-016 | Snapshot payloads are deep copies of canonical state in deterministic order with no derived caches; restore copies in, replaces the whole block, migrates from stored_version, and never retains a reference to input data. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-017 | Snapshots are taken only at a tick boundary (P6), synchronously in memory in SAVING (atomic within one boundary service, never spanning a frame), cover all persist WORLD blocks at the same T, and restore replaces WORLD blocks atomically while leaving PROFILE blocks untouched. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-018 | `kernel.clock` (T, world_seed) is the kernel's only registered block (SIM, persist, WORLD, never dirty); `restore_epoch` is owned and persisted by Save/Load (`slot_epoch.bin`), passed into `request_restore`/`request_new_game`, held read-only, exposed in the clock read view, and excluded from the snapshot header and all hashes. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-019 | RNG is never global: each system draws from a named stream (counter in its block) via the kernel's pure `rng_derive(world_seed, stream_name, counter)` (and `rng_derive_epoch` for threat draws) using SplitMix64 mix64, pinned by golden vectors. | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-020 | State hash `state_hash(PERSIST, T)` uses H64 = first 8 bytes of SHA-256 via HashingContext over canonical_encode bytes (Packed arrays hashed as raw bytes), with a published `block_hash(block_id)` read; hash prefix is T \|  \| world_seed. | Data | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-021 | Blocks are written only by owner mutators inside a kernel-issued WriteScope during P2 apply or P3 step; writes elsewhere are refused in every build and counted WRITE_OUTSIDE_SCOPE; apply is validate-then-apply with no rollback and a reported fault moves the kernel to FAULTED. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-022 | Every kernel-invoked owner callable receives a `ctx`, reports results only via ctx (ok/reject/put/fault) and returns only `ctx.complete()`'s identity-compared CompletionToken (never `-> void`); a missing token is fault(INCOMPLETE), and the kernel never relies on exceptions or `assert` for control flow. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-023 | Change notifications are emitted once per tick at P5 as (block_id, tick, change_keys) with no state payload (collapsing to ALL past MAX_DIRTY_KEYS = 256); subscribers fetch via read views and are classed PRESENTATION / INPUT_SURFACE / PROJECTOR / REPLICATION / DIAGNOSTIC; an object owning a block or hook never subscribes. | Core | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-024 | Presentation, PROJECTOR, and DIAGNOSTIC batches in a multi-tick frame are merged and delivered once after the frame's last tick (union of change_keys), while INPUT_SURFACE and REPLICATION get every tick's batch; P5 iterates a pre-P5 snapshot of the subscriber list and prunes freed subscribers. | Core | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-025 | PERCEPTION_OUT blocks are write-only to simulation: any sim-side read returns an empty view, increments PERCEPTION_READ_BLOCKED, and reports a violation; presentation holds no submit handle and sim never calls presentation. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-026 | Read views are getter-only facades or deep copies, never live containers; under STRICT_DEBUG a SIM_WRITE_TRIPWIRE hashes SIM blocks per dispatch batch and around P5 (TRIPWIRE_BUDGET_MS 1.0 ms, full sweep every 60 ticks). | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-027 | Commands apply in ascending (queued_tick, host_order) with apply_tick >= queued_tick + 1 while running; from an initial snapshot, same PLAYER command stream plus control-plane request log and same (save bytes, restore_epoch) yields identical canonical hash at tick N on the same build and platform. | Core | ADR-0002 + ADR-0005 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0005) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-028 | Control plane (`request_pause`, `request_resume`, `request_snapshot`, `request_restore`, `request_new_game`, `request_shutdown`) is a host-local API served at the next boundary in arrival order; `request_restore` may carry `expected_state_hash` and the kernel reports {T, computed_hash, match} without faulting on mismatch. | Core | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-029 | Kernel states BOOTING/LOADING/RUNNING/PAUSED/SAVING/FAULTED/SHUTTING_DOWN govern admission; LOADING and SAVING are atomic within one boundary service, submit in LOADING/SAVING/BOOTING is rejected NOT_ACCEPTING, and FAULTED never writes or autosaves over an existing save. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-030 | Pause is single-player only; while PAUSED only `allowed_when_paused` types (non-diegetic, writing only gameplay=false blocks named in `writes_blocks`) are dispatched via a paused service cycle (P1,P2,P4,P5,P6; no P3; T frozen), and all others are rejected PAUSED without queuing. | Core | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-031 | Queue bounds are boot assertions: MAX_CMDS_PER_TICK 128, QUEUE_CAPACITY 1024, PER_ISSUER_CAPACITY 256, `max_age_ticks(t) >= ceil(QUEUE_CAPACITY / MAX_CMDS_PER_TICK)`, and QUEUE_CAPACITY*(MAX_PAYLOAD+200B)+COMMAND_LOG_CAPACITY*(MAX_PAYLOAD+100B) <= COMMAND_MEMORY_BUDGET (16 MiB); overflow is QUEUE_FULL, never silent drop. | Performance | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-032 | Performance: a quiet tick performs 0 canonical encodes and 0 kernel container allocations, a tick dirtying k blocks performs k encodes, P3 <= 2.0 ms P99 (STEP_BUDGET_MS 4 ms alarm), frame sim <= 4.0 ms (1 tick) / 6.0 ms (4 ticks) P99, and a 32-block snapshot captures in <= 16.7 ms, measured on release export on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK. | Performance | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-033 | The kernel is scene-tree independent (headless-testable, `Kernel.step()` synchronous), with KernelProfile (STRICT_DEBUG / LENIENT_RELEASE) injected at construction and never read from `OS.is_debug_build()`; no networking code at MVP (host-only ingress is local input). | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-034 | Mode HOST ticks and applies authoritatively while MIRROR applies nothing, with an identical public API; blocks declare `repl_hint` (HOST_ONLY/SHARED_SUMMARY/OWNER_PRIVATE) now though unused until VS-B. | Networking | ADR-0002 + ADR-0032 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0032) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-035 | Blocks declare `restore_after`; a cycle or unknown id fails boot, `restore()` runs in DAG order, every `post_restore()` runs after every `restore()`, a `post_restore` failure faults LOADING without reverting, and registering more than MAX_BLOCKS (128) blocks fails boot. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-036 | Restore runs pre-checks before any `restore()` call and faults LOADING with pre-restore state intact on: unknown block_id (RESTORE_UNKNOWN_BLOCK), header TICK_RATE mismatch, restore_epoch >= 2^32, or a stored schema_version newer than code or older without migration; a missing persist block is set to `default_state()`. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 + ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-037 | A new game runs, inside LOADING and a WriteScope, every `default_state()`, then each owner's `init_world(world_seed)` in `restore_after` order, then `post_restore()`, then the binding rebuild; `init_world` never runs on a load from save. | Core | ADR-0016 | ADR-0003 (needs ADR-0016) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-038 | Snapshots round-trip losslessly through `var_to_bytes`/`bytes_to_var`, and any JSON interchange form writes every 64-bit value (T, u64 hashes, seeds) as a `hex64` string, never a JSON number. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-039 | Canonical TLV encoding is injective after normalisation: dictionary key order is irrelevant, -0.0 equals +0.0, all NaN patterns are equal, StringName equals String, and INT and FLOAT differ; `state_hash(PERSIST)` covers WORLD-scoped persist blocks only while `ALL_SIM` covers every SIM block. | Data | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-040 | Block hashes are cached and invalidated only by `mark_dirty`; every verification path (G3/G4 tests, snapshot hash, diagnostics diff) uses uncached encoding; `block_hash(id, uncached)` returns `ok=false` with BLOCK_HASH_IN_WRITE during a WriteScope or BLOCK_HASH_UNKNOWN for unknown or non-persist ids. | Data | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-041 | PERCEPTION_OUT blocks default to persist=false and are excluded from both `state_hash(PERSIST)` and `state_hash(ALL_SIM)`. | Data | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-042 | The pending queue supports coalescing: `LATEST_PER_ACTOR_PER_TICK` keys on (type, actor, issuer_peer), a superseded entry is tombstoned and compacted at P0, a batch never holds two commands of one key, a rejected newer intent supersedes nothing, and a coalescing type allowing both origins fails boot COALESCE_DUAL_ORIGIN. | Core | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-043 | Sequence numbers are kernel-stamped per (issuer_peer, origin) space with `host_order` global and monotonic; only a test or remote ingress supplies `seq` (DUPLICATE_SEQ on repeat); `correlation` is echoed in results but excluded from canonical bytes and hashes; T, seq and host_order are 64-bit and never wrap, asserted at boot. | Core | ADR-0002 + ADR-0005 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0005) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-044 | Admission evaluates terms in a fixed order and reports the first failing reason deterministically; remote issuers are rate-limited by a tick-based window (REMOTE_RATE_LIMIT per RATE_WINDOW_TICKS) that does not slide while PAUSED, the local issuer is never limited, and SYSTEM_RESERVE slots are reserved for SYSTEM commands. | Networking | ADR-0002 + ADR-0032 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0032) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-045 | Binding-table changes requested during tick T take effect at T+1 (every authority check in T uses the pre-change table), an unbound PlayerId's pending OWNED_BY_ISSUER commands are Rejected ACTOR_NOT_OWNED rather than dropped, and recreating a binding resets that issuer's seq counters while host_order continues. | Core | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-cmd-046 | Blocks flagged `interpolated` expose previous and current values for `tick_alpha` lerp; `commit_baseline` rolls only a derived cache outside snapshot and hash; resume, load and new-game call `snap_baseline` so previous equals current and tick_alpha reads 1.0 while PAUSED. | Core | ADR-0001 + ADR-0009 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0003 (needs ADR-0009) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-047 | Input-to-first-visible-response latency on a 60 Hz tick and 60 fps release export must have median `L_first` <= 33.3 ms, p95 <= 50 ms, median `L_full` <= 50 ms and p95 <= 66.7 ms, with the acknowledgement visible in the press frame (provisional pending RM-DESKTOP spike). | Performance | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-048 | The command log is a bounded ring of COMMAND_LOG_CAPACITY (1024) entries holding command, result and tick, evicting oldest first with exactly one entry per result_id; replay uses the command log plus the control-plane request log. | Data | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-cmd-049 | The DIAGNOSTIC class exposes read-only T, kernel state, queue depths, command log, resolved hook order and timings, rejection counters, lag counter and a state hash refreshed at DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS with hash_T and hash_algo; the Debug Overlay scene and DIAGNOSTIC entry point are excluded from release exports. | UI | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-050 | Test seams (`TestIngress.submit_raw`, `debug_set_counters`, stubbed diagnostics timer, `set_knob`) are excluded from release exports and verified by an export file-list symbol scan; runtime knob changes apply from the next tick. | Core | ADR-0010 + ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-051 | Kernel structure is lint-enforced in blocking gdUnit4 tests under `tests/unit/kernel/lint/`: a Kernel member allow-list, no per-verb command classes, no kernel-registered domain types, no networking symbols (MultiplayerAPI, ENet, rpc), and no presentation references from sim code. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-052 | Multiple Kernel instances coexist in one process with independent state, a block object bound to two kernels fails boot BLOCK_ALREADY_BOUND, and `Kernel.dispose()` is idempotent, empties the registries and leaves zero leaked RefCounted instances or orphan Nodes. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-cmd-053 | STRICT_DEBUG and LENIENT_RELEASE kernels fed the same command stream produce identical result records, final state and `state_hash(ALL_SIM)`; strict-only checks (command round-trip copy, SIM write tripwire) change only diagnostic counters. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |

### Life-Support Graph (`lsg`)
GDD: `design/gdd/life-support-graph.md` | 43 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-lsg-001 | The Graph is exactly one SIM state block `graph.state` (persist true, scope WORLD, SHARED, repl_hint SHARED_SUMMARY, schema_version 1, restore_after [layout.state]) registered during BOOTING. | Core | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-002 | Graph state is a struct-of-arrays `GraphState extends RefCounted` using PackedInt64/Int32/ByteArray only, with no Node, no Resource, no per-node objects, and underscore-prefixed fields written only by graph_mutators.gd, graph_propagate.gd, graph_restore.gd and graph_derived.gd (lint-enforced). | Core | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-003 | All stored, hashed and propagated quantities are integers (mGU, mC, dJ, mL, bp, permille, dkPa); zero floats on the propagation path, and designer floats are converted once at boot into immutable `GraphTuning`. | Core | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-004 | Extensive quantities are stored and intensive ones (P, p_o2, served_pm, descriptors) are derived on read; only hysteresis memory (band_o2/p/t, p_prev, SEAL_STRAIN bits) and warn trackers are persisted. | Save/Load | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-005 | Per-section conservation counters (vented_mgu, radiated_dj, spilled_cool_ml, produced_mgu, breathed_mgu, produced_dj, burned_uml, spilled_heat_mdj, discarded_*, withdrawn_*) keep gas, heat, coolant and fuel invariants exact integer equalities on a running ship, asserted by every test. | Core | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-006 | Every product on the propagation path stays below 2^62, verified by a boot assertion (D.13) that refuses a violating build; no sqrt/exp/pow/sin on the path. | Core | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-007 | Iteration is by ascending slot over dense append-only arrays (tombstones, no compaction in MVP); sim code never iterates a Dictionary; each loop uses one Jacobi pass from a double-buffered start-of-phase snapshot, with no iterative solver and no catch-up. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-008 | Graph registers three step hooks at divisor 6 with offsets 0 (power), 2 (atmo), 4 (failure), chained by `after:` with both chain edges declared `tolerates_stale: true`; one run = one step = 6 ticks, `step_no = floor(T/6)`, per-hook cursors persisted. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-009 | The step runs 12 fixed phases in order (power 0-3, atmo 4-7, failure 8-11); cascades queue in phase 10 and apply in phase 0 of the next run (one-hop latency), bounded by MAX_SPREAD_PER_STEP 32. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-010 | Topology refresh is one idempotent function run at the head of whichever Graph hook first runs after a structural mutator (M1-M5), zero cost while `topo_dirty` is clear; derived caches rebuild eagerly (`ensure_derived`) in structural mutators and `post_restore`. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-011 | Randomness (thaw-burst only at MVP) is `rng_derive(world_seed, "graph.fail", (step_no<<20) \| subject_id)`, a pure function of persisted state with no restore-epoch input, so a reload replays identical outcomes; subject_id < 2^20 asserted. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-012 | The thaw-burst roll window is persisted (`thaw_until_step`, THAW_ROLL_STEPS 100); a burst sets hp=min(hp, BURST_HP_TO) and closes the window; a restore on any tick neither re-rolls nor forgets it. | Save/Load | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-013 | The fairness gate: an endogenous transition to FAILED/DESTROYED or hull/seam LEAK->BREACH requires >= 100 consecutive steps (W_MIN_STEPS) of a sev>=1 descriptor, otherwise hp is floored at HP_FAIL+1 (BREACH_HP for breach); exogenous causes bypass the gate. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-014 | Non-player exogenous damage sources (M17 RUPTURE/GOUGE/STRESS, M18 EXOGENOUS) must be preceded by their own 10-30 s tell stated in the calling system's GDD (exported requirement). | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-015 | `alarm_eligible` is true only for alarm-capable modes (1-5,7-9,20-23; never 6,10,24-27) at sev >= ALARM_SEV_MIN with an open gate; the Graph publishes pure `alarm_sections()` (ascending, duplicate-free section_ids). | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-016 | The Graph publishes 19 mutators M1-M19 callable only inside a kernel `WriteScope` in P2/P3 with the scope token first; Class A has a paired pure `check_*` and an infallible apply, Class B (M14-M18) clamps, never rejects, and no-ops on stale ids; the Graph registers no commands. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-017 | M14 `set_load_request`, M15 `draw_air` and M17 `deposit_trace` queue into persisted bounded `pending_load` (<=64), `pending_draw` and `pending_trace` (<=16/run across callers), drained in phase 0 of the next run; `draw_grant(crew)` persists. | Save/Load | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-018 | `read_view` is pure and side-effect-free, publishes true values only (no perceived variant), never reads PERCEPTION_OUT, and the `state_hash` of graph.state is identical with and without Perception content. | Core | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-019 | Hot-path reads use typed scalar getters or a reusable view object, and `failures(filter)` returns slices of a preallocated columnar descriptor store built once in phase 11, allocating nothing per call. | Performance | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-020 | Dirty keys are int-encoded `(KIND<<32) \| id`, fire only on quantum bucket changes (T 1000 mC, P 10 dkPa, p_o2 5 dkPa, hp 250 bp, etc.), one C/<cid> per compartment per step; worst designed cascade is 38 keys at MVP and MAX_DIRTY_KEYS 256 must not fall below 128. | Core | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-021 | `canonical_bytes()` serialises persisted fields in fixed order as `[u32 tag][u32 len][bytes]` raw packed arrays with tombstones; `block_hash()` is cached H64 (first 8 bytes of SHA-256 via HashingContext), recomputed only after a write. | Save/Load | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-022 | Save interchange keeps all values < 2^53, uses packed int arrays, carries raw canonical bytes as base64 (`Marshalls.raw_to_base64`) in any JSON form, and never uses `var_to_bytes` or a Resource for saves. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-023 | Restore is two-phase (decode+validate into scratch state, then swap); only schema version 1 is supported; every failure is a named `GRAPH_RESTORE_*` reason with live state untouched; `post_restore` rebuilds derived caches and seeds last-emitted buckets (one ALL then silence). | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-024 | Structural invariant violations raise `GraphFault.raise(code, subject)` (never GDScript assert) causing kernel FAULTED; numeric violations clamp in release, increment `graph_saturations[field]`, and tests assert it is all zeros. | Core | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-025 | The Graph's hook spike share is P99 <= 0.5 ms and max <= 1.0 ms summed over hooks on one hook tick, on a Godot 4.7.2 release export on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK, with zero heap allocations per phase in steady state and presentation read side <= 0.1 ms P99/frame. | Performance | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-026 | Work per run is bounded by construction: one pass per loop, MAX_SPREAD_PER_STEP 32, MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP 16, capacity MAX_GRAPH_NODES 4096 / MAX_GRAPH_EDGES 8192, MAX_SEAMS_PER_SECTION 8; dormant sections cost zero. | Performance | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-027 | Welds only append edges: `can_join(plan)` (pure, closed reason set) validates, `weld_join` appends pre-validated sub-edges all disconnected (area 0, valves closed, breakers open), never renumbers ids, and cannot fail; ids are append-only and never reused. | Core | ADR-0012 + ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-028 | `cut_detach` is atomic in one P2 apply and only along seams (SEALED caps ports, RAGGED adds A_CUT 24 hull-stub holes and open_end ports); `discard_section` tombstones slots and moves mass to `discarded_*` so invariants close. | Core | ADR-0012 + ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-029 | Dormant sections are skipped by every phase, persisted and hashed unchanged, cannot be welded; M19 on load advances all absolute step stamps by the dormant duration so the gate and decay clocks count only run steps; no fast-forward. | Core | ADR-0012 + ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-030 | Type ids (loop, node kind, port type, edge kind, mode, state) are explicit, stable, unique-checked at boot, append-only and never reused; Item DB loads first, then Layout, then `graph.state` registers last. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-031 | The graph.state hash and rng_derive draws for fixed scripts (F-MVP-HEALTHY, weld/cut, thaw rolls) must be bit-identical on Windows, Linux and macOS, enforced by committed golden hash files compared in CI. | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-032 | Equalisation flux uses the exact integer gradient of start-of-phase amounts, never reverses direction, keeps the maximum gradient non-increasing, and is bounded by the D.12 stability sum, which can_join enforces with STABILITY. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-033 | Boot refuses invalid content or tuning with named GRAPH_BOOT_* reasons (power cycle, tuning relation violations, type-id duplicates, product overflow), and a content lint reports node/edge caps within 10%, D.12 sums, equilibrium temperatures and unforced frozen liquid pipes. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-034 | Every structural mutator (M1, weld_join, cut_detach) emits exactly one P5 dirty-key batch of its declared keys; can_join and read_view emit none; and a GraphFault mid-mutator emits no P5 notification and Drops the tick's commands with FAULTED. | Core | ADR-0003 + ADR-0021 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-lsg-035 | M17/M14/M15 deposits drain in phase 0 of the same tick when made by a P2 command or a hook ordered before graph.propagate.power, and at the next hook tick when made by a hook ordered after it, always in arrival order within the cap. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-036 | Snapshot of the MVP ship must take at most 0.5 ms and produce at most 32 KB, restore plus post_restore at most 2 ms (kernel integration world 3 ms and 10 ms), with canonical bytes byte-identical after restore, measured on the release export. | Performance | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-037 | Native HashingContext SHA-256 over roughly 150 KB of canonical bytes must take at most 1 ms (spike S0-d) so cached H64 block hashing is affordable; otherwise the hashing design is revisited. | Performance | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-038 | The persisted-field set is verified two ways in CI: reflection over every GraphState member against a checked-in DERIVED allowlist, and zero-and-diff of each derived cache confirming the next step's state_hash is unchanged. | Save/Load | ADR-0011 + ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-039 | Diagnostics counters (graph_saturations, graph_stale_calls, graph_gate_holds, hash_invocations and similar) are never part of the canonical byte layout or any state_hash, so zeroing them never changes the hash. | Save/Load | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-040 | A schema migration from a retained golden v1 save to v2 round-trips and its post-migration hash equals the committed golden v2 hash; golden saves are retained in tests as migration fixtures. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-041 | The SHARED_SUMMARY output of summary() is at most 1 KB, carries quantised intensive values within one quantum of truth, and excludes t_rem, pending queues and RNG inputs. | Networking | ADR-0032 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-042 | Consumers of Graph descriptors (Failure and Trail, Instruments, Adaptive Audio, Perception Filter) must not suppress a consequence-channel descriptor with sev >= 1, must play alarm classes only on alarm_eligible, and only the Filter calls true_value() outside diagnostics, enforced by CI lint. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-lsg-043 | Constants owned by the registry or kernel (MAX_DIRTY_KEYS, TICK_RATE, HYPOXIA_PP, DECOMP_P, COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC, HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC) are read by name and never redeclared as literals in Graph or Suit sources, with a lint for redeclaration. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Item & Module Database (`itemdb`)
GDD: `design/gdd/item-and-module-database.md` | 40 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-itemdb-001 | The catalogue is a static immutable `ItemCatalog` loaded once in BOOTING that registers no kernel state block, step hook, command or mutator. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-002 | `ItemCatalog` is constructed as `ItemCatalog.new(data, graph_tables)` with injected inputs, is scene-tree free, and uses no static, singleton or autoload state (many catalogues can coexist in one process). | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-003 | Each def is a typed `RefCounted` `ItemDef` written once by the loader (never a `Resource`, never a bare Dictionary), with array fields exposed as read-only copies. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-004 | Every IDB-28 query is pure and read-only, callable from `validate`, P3 and presentation, and returns no Dictionary iteration order. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-005 | `defs_of_class(class)` returns defs in ascending `type_id`, the only order sim code may iterate. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-006 | `def_id` (lowercase snake_case, <= `ID_MAX_LEN` 40, regex `^(mod\|tool\|cons\|part\|curio\|junk)_...`) is the only item key that reaches a save, snapshot, `SalvageRecord`, hash or replicated record; `type_id` (append-only u16) stays in memory. | Save/Load | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-007 | Boot refuses with `ITEM_BOOT_TYPE_ID` when type_ids collide, fall outside class range, are retired, or disagree with the committed `assets/data/items/ids.lock.json` ledger. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-008 | Item data lives as integer-only JSON under `assets/data/items/` (modules, tools, consumables, parts, curios, meta, ids.lock); designer floats convert once at boot via `int(round(x*scale))`; no `res://` path on any saved field. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-009 | Boot order is after Graph type tables and `GraphTuning` and before any block restore, with a closed fatal failure list (`ITEM_BOOT_TYPE_ID/FIELD/PORT/REPAIR/MIRROR/ALIAS`) and lifecycle UNLOADED, PARSED, VALIDATED, FROZEN, REFUSED. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-010 | Module port declarations are validated against the Graph's port-type ids (LS-4) and the pure `connectable` at data load; the Item DB defines no node kind, port-type id or loop id. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-011 | `ItemInstance` is a six-field value schema (`def_id`, `count`, `condition_bp`, `cap_bp`, `patched_at_step`, `charge`) embedded by every holder, with no instance id, and the Item DB stores/allocates/moves none. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-012 | `ItemInstance` uses a canonical positional encoding with mandatory canonical zeros for non-applicable fields so holder blocks feeding `state_hash` have one encoding per state (no float, no `res://` path). | Save/Load | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-013 | `validate_instance(instance, graph_step_no)` returns a reason code and never clamps; invariants checked: 1 <= condition_bp <= cap_bp, patched_at_step 0 iff cap_bp = HP_FULL else 1..step_no, module at condition_bp 0 refused at restore. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-014 | `validate_instance` compares against the Graph's 10 Hz `step_no`, never the 60 Hz kernel tick. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-015 | `salvage_yield(rec)` is deterministic with no randomness, computed from the return of the applied M6 `SalvageRecord{def_id, hp, state, hp_cap, patched_at_step}` so same-tick double-cuts cannot duplicate. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-016 | Salvage then reinstall preserves hp, hp_cap and patched_at_step (patch cap and decay clock ride with the module) while stored quantities (stock/stock_ml) are discarded and reinstall at 0; only a spent repair part lifts a cap. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-017 | `repair_part_for(subject_kind, kind_id, loop)` is total over the five `subject_kind` values and boot fails with `ITEM_BOOT_REPAIR` otherwise; part consumption is charged at the first productive channel step, with the command/timing owned by Interaction. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-018 | Defs carry only true integer-gram `mass_g` and a `weight_class` (F-1 by `WEIGHT_MEDIUM_G` 5000 / `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G` 25000); mass never changes speed or noise; no price/rarity/recipe/loot fields exist. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-019 | Every def has a true `mass_g`; junk defs differ from their lure by >= `WEIGHT_TELL_MIN_PM` 300 permille and in a different `weight_class` (vertical slice), with lure data held outside this folder. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-020 | `catalogue_version` is a single integer bumped on def removal, rename or field-shape change (F-9) and offered to Save/Load header; `catalogue_hash` is diagnostic only and never gates a load. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-021 | Renames need `renamed_from[]` on the new def honoured only at restore via `resolve_alias`; retired defs restore through `replaced_by` or fail, and ids are never reused. | Save/Load | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-022 | Glowstick parameters (`GLOWSTICK_LIFE_S` 150, radius 2 tiles, wane 400/250 permille) are data-driven; life counts from the throw tick, and a stowed glowstick is inert; the curve is evaluated by Emitter State (`WANE_LINEAR_25`). | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-023 | Non-allocating queries (`def`, `has`, `weight_class`, `repair_part_for`, `compatible_defs`) leak no Objects and allocate no instance id across 10,000 calls (OBJECT_COUNT delta zero). | Performance | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-024 | Catalogue load plus freeze creates exactly 25 Objects (23 `ItemDef`, the `NO_DEF` sentinel, one `ItemCatalog`) and returns to baseline on release; wall-clock load P99 <= 14.9 ms desktop / 30.0 ms Deck is pending spike measurement. | Performance | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-025 | Instance location states (INSTALLED=Graph, LOOSE_WORLD=Interaction `loose[]`, CARRIED=Controller id + Suit instance) are each performed by the named owner; the Item DB performs no move. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-026 | A def encodes no "dead"/powered-off variant; derelict dead modules are the same `def_id` authored at hp <= HP_FAIL, and HULL_SEGMENT/edges/valves/openings/seams have no def. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-027 | Salvage is the only item faucet (authored placement, `salvage_yield`, Suit starting kit); no shop, currency, recipe, crafting or refill exists. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-028 | Dialect is a sprite/skin field with no gameplay stat; every MVP def is `dialect: foundry` with no stat override. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-029 | A canonical positional ItemInstance encoding (`var_to_bytes(inst.to_array(), false)`) must be at most 96 bytes per instance, a twelve-instance holder block with an 8-field header at most 1,024 bytes (25 percent of MAX_PAYLOAD 4096), and every integer at most 2^53. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-030 | The exported build must include `assets/data/items/*.json` despite default export filters, and the loader must parse via `JSON.new().parse()` with error line and message, treat JSON integers as floats checked for integrality, and refuse integers above 2^53. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-031 | Module fields split into copy-at-install (`req_w`, `req_max`, port caps, copied into the Graph node at M5 and persisted) and read-live (`waste_permille`, `out_w_rated` and similar, read through `def_id` each use, never copied or persisted); `repair_part_id` is also read live. | Core | ADR-0011 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-032 | A save must restore unchanged after catalogue tuning edits (mass, `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G`, `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX`, `PATCH_CAP_BP`): the Item DB neither asserts, clamps, deletes nor migrates, and `catalogue_version` stays unchanged. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 + ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-033 | Restoring a store whose saved stock exceeds a lowered live `stock_cap` must succeed by clamping to the cap, adding the excess to the section's `discarded_*` counter, closing conservation, and emitting a diagnostic naming node, old stock and cap. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 + ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-034 | The `emits` record (`shape_id`, `base_pm`, `hue_class`) must validate at load against Emitter State's shape table and the registry `hue_class_token_map`, and node-lantern output derives deterministically from node state, hp and run level. | Data | ADR-0017 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-035 | A CI lint suite in `tools/ci/item_db_lints` (with its own unit tests) must enforce catalogue and content rules: forbidden-key and stale-token scans, weight windows, folder contents, dead-twin naming, placement def existence, part supply, start-kit consumers, with BLOCKING versus ADVISORY severity per rule. | Core | ADR-0010 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-036 | Design-time lints and formula checks for fluid port leaks, seam floors, power caps, power budget and the O2 margin ladder must call the Graph's pure functions (`cap_seam`, D.4, D.5) with Graph-sourced node and edge numbers, never retyped copies. | Data | ADR-0011 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-037 | Lookups of unknown ids must return the shared `NO_DEF` sentinel (or `NONE`, 0 for unknown repair kinds) and never throw; `resolve_alias` outside the restore path returns `NO_DEF`; `salvage_yield` of ABSENT or SALVAGED returns an empty list. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-038 | ItemInstance merge is defined: only instances identical in every field except `count` merge (counts summed), and a merge exceeding `stack_max` is refused with the surplus returned. | Data | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-039 | No UI scene or string table may bind `mass_g` or `instance_mass_g` or contain mass or weight words (`kg`, `weight`, `mass`, `heavy`, `lbs`); weight reaches the player only through closed-list consequence cues, verified by an automatic scan. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-itemdb-040 | On salvage and reinstall, switch and setpoint fields (`throttle_pm`, `setpoint_pp`) take the Graph's fresh defaults and `state` and `forced_mode` are re-derived from hp, so reinstall never raises hp or clears an hp-caused failure. | Core | ADR-0021 + ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline & Camera (`pxl`)
GDD: `design/gdd/pixel-perfect-render-pipeline-camera.md` | 37 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-pxl-001 | The world is a 640x360 base-px logical canvas drawn at integer scale k = floor(min(W/640, H/360)) from window pixel size (never DPI; content_scale_factor 1.0), with minimum window 1280x720 so k >= 2, and no world node is ever drawn at a non-integer scale. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-002 | World content renders in its own SubViewport inside a SubViewportContainer (layer 0); HUD (layer 2), chart table/menus (layer 3), transit-hold quad (layer 1) and debug overlay (layer 4, dev only) are CanvasLayers above it that receive no world light, glow, ambient, occlusion, Filter pass or camera jolt. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-003 | A single pipeline-root node is the only writer of SubViewport.size, size_2d_override, camera limits rect and the only caller of HudLayout; any window/mode/size change fires one RESCALING frame that re-derives k, H, logical size, letterbox, limits and world_to_hud. | Core | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-004 | Nearest filtering must be set in three places (project default_texture_filter=0, world SubViewport canvas_item_default_texture_filter=0, SubViewportContainer.texture_filter=TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST=1) and mipmaps are off on drawn sheets; SubViewport.size and TileSet.tile_size (48x48) are set explicitly. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-005 | Presentation code places every node at integer base-px via pos = floor(prev + (curr-prev)*tick_alpha) in the current body frame (one floor function everywhere, incl. camera and beam chest point); snap_2d_transforms/vertices_to_pixel are a safety net only; no per-node scale animation, Camera2D.zoom stays (1,1). | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-006 | Y-sorted layer order is the key (sort_y, class_rank, entity_id) with class_rank 0 wall face < 1 wall-mounted < 2 doors/props/machinery < 3 actors/horrors, entity_id a globally unique persistent int64 from the publisher's registered namespace (never x, never creation order); children kept in (class_rank, entity_id) order via move_child at spawn; sort origins are integer base px. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-007 | A door/doorway with opening area > 0 sorts by the north edge of its footprint (sort_y = y_0), a closed door (area = 0) sorts as a wall (y_0 + 24). | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-008 | z-band order is the contract (Floor 0, Floor marks +1, Y-sort +10, Wall caps +20, Dust +30; floors/caps/dust never Y-sorted; dependents lint band names not numbers); all bands stay inside Light2D range_z; wall footprint 24 / face 72 / cap 24, TILE_PX 48, caps-above safe iff no flush item face > 72. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-009 | If the Y-sorted layer exceeds ~300 draws, a world-anchored per-row z-band fallback (BAND_H 24, window z 10..899, band_0 from union rect of all drawn bodies) is built only if it lowers both draw calls and CPU at the spike; otherwise withdrawn in favour of per-field draw caps. | Performance | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-010 | The camera is a pure CameraSolver (RefCounted solve(input,state,params)->pose, float64 per-axis state, no scene-tree dependency, headless-testable) hosted by a top-level presentation node that writes the world viewport canvas_transform from pose_q in the pipeline root's frame_pre_draw handler (fallback _process lowest priority); Camera2D.limit_* stays at defaults, solver is the only clamp, no Camera2D descends from a PhysicsBody2D. | Core | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-011 | The camera responds only to player-owned inputs (avatar position, local raw beam aim, zoom_in/zoom_out, current vehicle) and never to world state (Attention, breach, horrors, sound, graph, sanity); identical input trace must yield identical camera trace in calm, tell and breach; the Perception Filter may not write any camera or viewport property. | Core | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-012 | Follow is first-order exponential (tau_follow 0.18 s, tau_eff = tau*min(1,s), s = field_h/360, dt clamped to DT_MAX 0.05 s) with per-axis SETTLE_EPS snap, pose quantised after smoothing to Q=1 base px; pose agrees across 30/60/144 fps to within 1 base px (not bit-identical); D.6b offset-quantised follow is the fallback if avatar-to-screen offset changes > 2/s at 60/90/144 fps. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-013 | Look-ahead reads the local raw aim (not the coalesced intent), is bounded by L_MAX 72 px (scaled by s), eased with tau_lookahead 0.40 s and L_SLEW_MAX 144 px/s, run-weighted by committed loco_state only, and is 0 under reduced motion; build invariants LAG_CUT > v_max*tau and INNER_BOX_FRAC*field_h > v_max*tau_eff + L_MAX_eff hold at every reachable ladder field. | Core | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-014 | The aim-pose loop is open: mouse_to_world_aim(cursor,p) = f_q + (cursor - picture_centre)/k_z using the published look-ahead-free follow pose f_q with no L, inner-box clamp or jolt; cursor is converted to physical window px via inverse get_final_transform; mouse_to_world_view is separately published and must never feed beam aim (lint). | Input | ADR-0008 + ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-015 | Camera state is never persisted; solver resets (CUT) on every LOADING->RUNNING and pod/transit body transfer, starts with L=0 easing in; weld/detach BODY_TRANSFER with frame_delta_px is a rebase (shift f and history, keep lag and L); owners of moving read views set previous := current in post_restore. | Save/Load | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-016 | Zoom is a persistent edge-triggered integer ladder k_z in [1, k_base+2] implemented by changing world viewport logical size (never Camera2D.zoom), each step a one-frame cut with solver length params rescaled; at most one accepted step per frame and >= ZOOM_STEP_MIN_INTERVAL 0.15 s apart (surplus discarded); steps failing the static ZOOM_MIN_K draw/P99 table are withheld; HUD unaffected; zoom not reset by in-session restore. | Input | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-017 | Camera limits are the loaded body's int base-px bounding rect set by a PROJECTOR (kernel CR-10 class) on weld/detach and consumed by the solver alone (inner box first, limits win); camera is CLAMPED when an axis is clamped; no cinematic takeover, free-look, shared co-op camera or shake beyond JOLT_MAX <= 2 (default 0, P1 physical events only, applied inside world viewport, never as container offset). | Core | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-018 | World canvas is the current body's frame with interior at origin; ship rotation never rendered inside, all human-built bodies render at rotation 0 and detached sections translate without rotation; exterior/transit parallax layers are driven by the camera host with whole-base-px offsets (-floor(pose_q*scroll_scale)); follow target is the local seat's controlled entity (kernel CR-7), no shared camera. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-019 | world_to_hud(pos) is the single body-space-to-HUD projection (accounts for k, H, letterbox, zoom, unjolted pose, quantised to HUD grid) and no system re-derives it; HUD elements anchor to a HUD-root Control sized hud_rect/H pinned to the k_base picture rect. | UI | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-020 | Every visual system is a kernel PRESENTATION subscriber: reads last-committed state via read views, treats notifications as dirty marks and draws in the render callback, writes only CanvasItem/Material/Light2D/particle/audio properties, never a block/command/physics body/camera or viewport property, interpolates with tick_alpha (clamped at 1, no extrapolation), never causes or blocks a tick; Debug Overlay alone is DIAGNOSTIC. | Core | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-021 | The pipeline owns present_time (render-rate clock, advances while RUNNING/SAVING/LOADING-with-hold, frozen in PAUSED, never scaled by reduced motion); layer-0 animation uses only present_time, layers 3-4 run on engine process time, horror rig pose stays sim-stepped; SceneTree.paused is not kernel pause. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-pxl-022 | The sim stays 60 Hz and render rate never changes the tick: identical state_hash after a scripted 10-s replay at max_fps 30/60/144/0; the tick is driven from the kernel's `_process` TickDriver and the physics step is never the clock (kernel CR-1a), so no physics_jitter_fix or max_physics_steps_per_frame coupling exists (ADR-0001, Proposed); tick-before-render ordering is the process-priority order input adapter, then driver, then presentation. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-pxl-023 | Exactly one full-screen hook: a ShaderMaterial slot on the SubViewportContainer assigned/nulled only by the pipeline root, below every CanvasLayer, colour-writing only (no displacement/blur/resolution change), flash-clamped; at MVP filter_route = PER_ELEMENT and the slot is NOT_USED (null). | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-024 | HUD integer scale H independent of k and zoom with H_default = max(k,3) (HUD_MIN_SCALE 3), offered up to 2x only where bottom_row_fit holds, so two integer scales coexist in one window from MVP (render-wiring ADR; leading candidate i: root stretch disabled, script sets SubViewport size, container scale and CanvasLayer.scale); reading-face text rasterised at final screen px size, never under integer-scaled layers. | UI | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-025 | RENDER_MODE in {A,B} is a single build flag (A default: world viewport at logical size, B: window size with size_2d_override_stretch); get_final_transform().get_scale() must equal (k,k) for the world and (H,H) for the HUD at every window in PR-1's table; renderer is Forward+; A and B must be identical in coordinate space, sort contract, camera contract and HUD. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-026 | Aspect: default keep, expand (post-spike) uses logical size (2*floor(W/2k), 2*floor(H/2k)) capped at 960x540 at default zoom; manual k below Auto forces keep; letterbox bars are true black (clear colour (0,0,0,1)) outside the world SubViewport and untouched by Filter or jolt. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-027 | Flash safety: per-light, 3x3-region and frame-mean flash clamps (F = ceil(n_transitions/2) <= FLASH_MAX_PER_S 3) are computed analytically from modifier state before drawing (no GPU readback in the clamp path; readback scan is dev/verification only); reduce flashing defaults ON at first launch; no full-frame flash and no full-frame gamma/brightness anywhere. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-028 | Startup guard rails: project.godot pins stretch block, default_texture_filter, both snap_2d keys, hdr_2d, use_debanding=false, physics_interpolation=false, clear colour black, window override 1280x720, HDR output off; a debug-build pipeline-root assertion reads back effective values (fails loudly), a headless gdUnit4 test parses project.godot, and a debug tree-walk flags fractional positions, rotation under human-built groups, Camera2D.zoom != (1,1), HUD Control.scale != (1,1), and Camera2D under PhysicsBody2D. | Core | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-029 | Pipeline states BOOTING/PRESENTING/RESCALING/TRANSIT_HOLD/PAUSED_FROZEN/HIDDEN (+DEBUG_OVERLAY modifier) with camera state as three independent fields (follow FOLLOWING/CLAMPED, zoom default/ZOOMED, hold live/HELD/CUT); PAUSED_FROZEN holds an undimmed last frame, a RESCALING while updates are disabled forces UPDATE_ONCE, TRANSIT_HOLD plate is #000000 with no text. | Core | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-030 | Performance budget (pending spike on RM-DESKTOP i5-9400F/GTX 1050 Ti and RM-DECK, release export): pipeline _process <= 0.5 ms P99 (solver <= 0.05 ms), draw calls D_world <= 350 and D_root <= 62 with sum <= 1000, total frame P99 <= 14.9 ms desktop / 30.0 ms Deck, RESCALING re-derivation <= 4 ms in one frame, world render targets <= 3 MB (A) / 22 MB (B). | Performance | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-031 | Every time-dependent pipeline and camera rule (zoom rate limit, T_PAN, flash windows, latency stamps) reads an injected monotonic Clock.now_us() passed to the pipeline root, never wall time or the render delta, so interval logic is deterministic and testable under a fixture clock. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-032 | Pipeline-root input mapping never compares device ids to 0 (lint-enforced) and converts cursor positions only through the pipeline's own physical-window-pixel mapping, so device identity follows the Player Controller's 4.7 device-ID scheme. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-033 | Settings data (stored in the profile, not the save slot) exposes only enumerated render options: world scale Auto plus ints 2 and 3, HUD scale 3 to 6, frame cap {30,60,120,144,240,uncapped}, V-sync {enabled, mailbox, off} with no adaptive, reading-face scale 80-200 percent; no gamma, brightness, contrast, sharpening or filter option exists. | Data | ADR-0008 + ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-034 | The reduced-motion and reduce-flashing toggles alter presentation only: with either or both on, kernel tick count, state_hash of sim blocks and derived render state stay identical to the toggle-off run, and no toggle writes a sim block. | Core | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-035 | Debug Overlay scene, fractional-transform tree-walk script and flash-scan tool are excluded from release exports by file, and the DIAGNOSTIC surface exists only in dev builds. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-036 | Pipeline diagnostics (diag.* counters and cut_reason values) are owned and name-frozen by the pipeline under the Debug Overlay channel-registration convention, so criteria, the overlay and tests read stable keys. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pxl-037 | Pixel-level verification (frame diffs, checkerboard, draw-call counts, flash scan) needs a windowed CI runner with a real rasteriser; the headless runner may only cover pure functions, scene-tree walks, project.godot parsing and lints. | Rendering | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Emitter State & Light-Exposure Query (`emitter`)
GDD: `design/gdd/emitter-state-and-light-exposure-query.md` | 46 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-emitter-001 | Emitter State is exactly one persisted SIM block `emitter.state` (persist true, scope WORLD/SHARED, repl_hint SHARED_SUMMARY, schema_version 1, supported_versions [1], added_in_format 1, restore_after [layout.state, graph.state, body.state, controller.state]) registered at BOOTING; boot lints refuse a block lacking supported_versions or added_in_format. | Save/Load | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-emitter-002 | The module registers exactly one step hook `emitter.place` (divisor 1, offset 0, after [body.integrate, graph.propagate.failure]) doing all per-tick work in fixed order W0..W7; no second hook; in MIRROR mode the hook never runs. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-003 | The module under src/sim/emitter/ is scene-tree free, integer-only, and never references Light2D, LightOccluder2D, Node or PhysicsServer2D, nor obtains a PERCEPTION_OUT view; it runs headless with zero scene nodes (enforced by lint). | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-004 | Emitter records are created, changed and destroyed only through published mutators E1..E10 inside a kernel WriteScope by their declared callers (Class A infallible, Class B clamp/never reject); Lighting, Attention, Horror and presentation never write emitters; mutators outside a WriteScope assert in debug and reject in release. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-005 | Emitter records have u32 ids allocated from a canonical counter inside the block, never reused, strictly ascending; all iteration is in ascending emitter id / subject ref, never Dictionary order. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-006 | The emitter kind set is closed (BEAM, FIXTURE incl. EMERGENCY/DOCK_STATUS subkinds, NODE_LANTERN, DEPLOYABLE/GLOWSTICK, BEACON, FAULT_GLOW); a seventh kind is a schema bump; no record has phantom/perceived/decoy/hallucinated fields. | Data | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-007 | Canonical (snapshotted, hashed) state is limited to the id counter, canonical record fields (incl. switch_on, arm_tick, last_toggle_tick, life/evict ticks, FAULT_GLOW placed_tick/glow_key/sev) and subject registrations including each subject's `lit` hysteresis bit; body_id, origin/aim/reach/output_pm, pair rows, lux values and read-view snapshots are derived and never hashed. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-008 | `post_restore` rebuilds all derived state in ascending id (including the whole static layer, so no subject is pending at the first validate), keeps the subject `lit` bit, re-derives body_id from Layout/Body, and a snapshot restored at any tick yields identical answers from T+1 on (kernel G4 save-equivalence). | Save/Load | ADR-0016 + ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-009 | The kernel binding source `beam_bindings() -> [(player_id, beam_id)]` (ascending player_id, pure read) is registered at BOOTING so the LOADING rebuild binds seat beams after every post_restore; `toggle_beam` uses actor_binding OWNED_BY_ISSUER with actor_source `emitter.state`. | Core | ADR-0016 + ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-010 | `toggle_beam` (origin PLAYER, coalesce NONE, max_age_ticks 120) validates in order NO_SUCH_EMITTER, NOT_ACTIONABLE, TOO_SOON, CHARGE_EMPTY; the beam changes only on committed state and the Presenter never predicts it. | Input | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-011 | A sim-side commit-spacing guard rejects `toggle_beam` (TOO_SOON) and `set_switch` (COOLDOWN, all origins) when a realised transition is within SWITCH_MIN_SPACING_TICKS (12) of canonical `last_toggle_tick`; an OFF during ARMING is exempt; E10 is exempt. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-012 | `set_switch` (PLAYER toggle / SYSTEM {emitter_id,on}) and `set_beacon` (SYSTEM only) validate against 'structurally present' only (NOT_LIVE, NOT_SWITCHABLE, NOT_BEACON, NO_SUCH_EMITTER); PLAYER-origin validate never reads node state, served_pm or health (no oracle). | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-013 | Fixture ON calls Graph M14 `set_load_request(node_ref, req_w)` inside the apply, the lamp is ARMING (output 0) until arm_tick = T_a + ((GRAPH_STEP_TICKS - T_a mod GRAPH_STEP_TICKS) mod GRAPH_STEP_TICKS) (0..5 ticks), and OFF is immediate with M14 (node_ref, 0). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-014 | Lamp output is `switch_on and armed and live ? floor(base_pm * served_pm / 1000) : 0`, with served_pm/node state read live each tick; a not-live (FAILED/DESTROYED/SALVAGED/ABSENT/dormant) lamp keeps switch_on and relights on repair without a command. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-015 | Node lantern output uses the Graph-published `node(id).run_pm` clamped to 0..1000 (never recomputed from out_w, never served_pm alone); lantern on-states come from Item DB `lantern_on_states`. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-016 | Beam geometry: apex/reach from `controller.state.chest_sim`, axis from committed aim_angle (AIM_UNITS 4096), occlusion segment starts at the foot origin (pos_curr); shape table (EM-8, 11 rows incl. conditional BEAM_SPILL) is data with integer half_angle_units and COS2_Q28; rendered reach must equal table reach +/-1 px for hard rows (build lint). | Physics | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-017 | Cone membership uses a build-time-generated integer Q14 direction table (mirror-exact), Q28 squared-cosine test with D>0 guard, exact integer floor sqrt, no float/trig on the sim path, and all products bounded under 2^62 (boot asserts I-MIRROR, I-OVERFLOW). | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-018 | Occlusion is an integer segment-vs-rectangle test over Layout opaque footprints (unit-square cells, doubled coordinates, closed boxes, conservative) behind an `OccluderSource` seam (`rects_in(aabb)`, `version(section_id)`); no physics backend, no direct-space-state query; actors/horrors never occlude; door state read from Layout projection of Graph door_state with no Emitter door flag. | Physics | ADR-0017 + ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-019 | Illumination is a saturating integer sum `lux_pm = min(1000, sum c)` over SOFT/HARD curve classes with no ambient term; `act_lux_pm` sums only gates_actions emitters (beacon excluded); `lit(ref)` is hysteresis on act_lux_pm with LIT_ON_PM 150 / LIT_OFF_PM 120 and no dwell; emitter publishes r_lit_px and r_off_px. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-020 | Subjects register via E6/E7/E8 with a single anchor; an unregistered ref answers lux 0 / lit false in release (no existence oracle); registration over MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS (192) or MAX_MOVING_SUBJECTS (16) returns false and reads dark, with release telemetry counters. | Data | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-021 | Frames: stored anchors are section-local, composed with section_offset into the body frame; emitter and subject are compared only when body_id matches; body_id is derived from Layout `body_of` (Body `body_of_actor` for beam/ACTOR), never stored, re-read in W0 when `static_version` changes (weld/cut). | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-022 | Static-layer contributions are cached as per-pair rows (emitter,subject)->{d,f_pm,c} with unsaturated raw sums, updated by deltas (output, reach, create/remove, subject move, occluder change, cold, frame change) synchronously by W4; dynamic layer (BEAM, BEACON) recomputed per tick; never by subscription (CR-10). | Performance | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-023 | Exposure work is bounded by deterministic counted work units (budget B = 10,000 units, caps MAX_EMITTERS 96, MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE 8, MAX_DEPLOYABLES_EVICTING 4, MAX_FAULT_GLOW 10), never wall-clock; creation at MAX_EMITTERS is refused in priority order (BEAM 255, BEACON 200, FIXTURE 120, DEPLOYABLE 100, FAULT_GLOW 80, NODE_LANTERN 60). | Performance | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-024 | Performance budgets on release export (RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK): steady-state `emitter.place` + exposure P99 <= 0.4 ms (EXPOSURE_BUDGET_MS); rare scenarios (door travel, blackout, weld/cut) max <= 1.0 ms (EXPOSURE_TICK_CEILING_MS); cold rebuild/restore <= 8 ms (COLD_REBUILD_CEILING_MS). | Performance | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-025 | Read-view reads are pure table lookups served from a per-tick snapshot built in W7 (packed/typed columns, sorted by id; no-retain rule, no allocation), at most one tick stale unless the reader declares after emitter.place; never callable from notification handlers that decide state. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-026 | Dirty keys are emitted per emitter only (`E/<id>` = (KIND<<32) \| id) on create/remove/switch/shape/output-bucket (OUTPUT_DIRTY_QUANTUM_PM 50)/reach step; continuous pose and subject lux/lit never dirty; Lighting polls the 17-field read view plus `beam_spill(beam_id)` at render rate. | Rendering | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-027 | FAULT_GLOW emitters are derived each Graph (failure-hook) tick by reconciling Graph `failures(filter)` against existing records using Failure & Trail glow-table rows and Layout `anchor_geometry` head point; lowest-sev eviction only for records older than W_MIN_WARNING_S; protection beats the cap. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-028 | Deployables (glowsticks) spawn only at landing via E3 with life remaining, hold full reach until GLOWSTICK_WANE_FROM_PM then wane linearly to the 24 px floor (sim state, not presentation); cap eviction is a visible evict-wane (D.9b), never an instant removal; evict_tick and reach_at_evict persist. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-029 | The beacon axis is a pure function of kernel tick T and authored phase/dir (`(phase + dir*floor(T*AIM_UNITS/BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS)) mod 4096`, BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS 128) with no per-tick angle state, and gates_actions is false. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-030 | Determinism: no global RNG, no floats stored/hashed/replicated or on the query path; `state_hash` covers the canonical set only; same seed + command stream gives identical state_hash and identical answers across machines (lint AC-2 over src/sim/emitter/). | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-031 | Every boot invariant (I-EDGE, I-HYST, I-WEDGE, I-CAPS a-e, I-WANE, I-REACH, I-DOCK, I-MIRROR, I-OVERFLOW, I-SPACING) is a function returning a fault code, never `assert()`; the composition root refuses to start on any non-OK code identically in Release (I-SPACING is warn-only), proven by a Release-export `--quit-after` smoke. | Core | ADR-0007 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-032 | The emitter runs headless against injected seams only (FakeClock, Layout, Graph, Body, Controller, Suit, Diagnostics, CommandBus, WorkCounter); the work counter is compiled in solely under the EM_WORK_COUNTER test flag and never in the benchmarked build, and must equal the D.14 formula exactly. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-033 | Debug assertions and all defect or budget conditions (overflow, create refused, invalid transition, rebuild over ceiling, anchor-clearance fail, fault-glow overcap) report through a Diagnostics seam as named counters that exist and increment in Release templates; playtest counters (lit-beam ticks, toggles, TOO_SOON) are sim-incremented with no gameplay effect. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-034 | Source under src/sim/emitter/ uses no bare integer `/` or `%`; a possibly negative operand always goes through checked helpers `floor_div` (non-negative operands) or `posmod_i`, enforced by a grep lint with committed bad/good fixture files per banned token. | Core | ADR-0005 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-035 | Every submitted emitter command reaches exactly one terminal result; a same-state SYSTEM `set_switch`/`set_beacon` is Applied with no state change, no dirty key and no `last_toggle_tick` write; a mutator called outside a WriteScope is rejected with no state change in Release. | Core | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-emitter-036 | Downstream hooks order after emitter.place: Attention declares after graph.propagate.power and emitter.place, Horror after Attention and emitter.place, and suit.vitals reads the beam `lit` as of the previous tick; Suit's `force_off` in suit.vitals zeroes the beam output in the same tick's place; a lit lamp counts once as light, not also as `lamps_lit_w`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0022 + ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-037 | Mutators fill every derived record field synchronously, so the read view is complete from P4 of the creating tick, while the subject table first reflects spawns, E7 unregisters and E8 anchor moves at the first emitter.place at or after that tick (at most one tick later). | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-038 | emitter.place and all mutators do nothing while the kernel is RESTORING or FAULTED; an unsupported `schema_version` on restore is a reported failure; the block exposes `canonical_bytes()` byte-identical across round trips and `block_hash()` once kernel amendment K-1 lands. | Save/Load | ADR-0003 + ADR-0005 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-emitter-039 | Self-powered DOCK_STATUS glows (node_ref 0) are switched only by Web Map's E10 `set_dock_status` apply, with no ARMING, no M14 call, no cooldown and no `last_toggle_tick`; E1 at `init_world` creates authored-on lamps ARMING from clock 0 and reads no Web Map state (`pod_at`). | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-040 | Weld and cut transactions call no Emitter mutator; the emitter adapts through Layout `body_of`/`static_version` and recomputes affected rows on the same tick, so no subject is ever pending or stale on a rebuild tick, and an over-ceiling tick still completes its updates and counts `emitter_rebuild_over_ceiling`. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-041 | Simulation results (state_hash, lux, lit, top_emitter, section_emission) are independent of camera, zoom rung and visible rect; off-screen subjects and lamps are fully simulated and counted, and the emitter source references no camera, zoom or viewport symbol (lint). | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-042 | `section_emission(section)` is a precomputed per-section array published in the W7 snapshot (sum of floor(output_pm * reach_px / 1000) over that section's emitters, zero-output and off-screen included, shape-blind, foot spill excluded), read in O(1) with no per-emitter iteration. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-043 | The Item DB `emits` block is validated at data load by a lint that maps each def to exactly one emitter kind and field set, refusing unknown kinds, hue tokens or out-of-range reach. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-044 | Emitter and subject anchors must clear every occluder by ANCHOR_CLEARANCE_PX (1) under a data-load lint naming EMITTER_ANCHOR_CLEARANCE; a violating run-time anchor reads lux 0 / lit false (emitters light nothing but still count in section_emission), with a per-record counter. | Data | ADR-0007 + ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-045 | Emitter state transitions are a closed table; any command or event requesting an unlisted transition (e.g. WANING to OFF) leaves state unchanged and increments `emitter_invalid_transition` labelled with from and to states, never silently altering a record. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-emitter-046 | Exposure evaluation must use broadphase and radius-survivor rejection rather than a linear subject scan: at 174 subjects a linear scan costs 12,969 units against the B = 10,000 budget, and cold build or restore (151,430 units) is the only whole-table pass allowed. | Performance | ADR-0017 + ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |

### 2D Lighting & Occlusion (`light`)
GDD: `design/gdd/2d-lighting-and-occlusion.md` | 43 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-light-001 | Lighting is a PRESENTATION subscriber that reads only last-committed SIM views (`emitter.state`, `shapes()`, Layout `OccluderSource`) and PERCEPTION_OUT lists (decoys and module-body glows only); notification handlers only mark dirty and all drawing happens in the render callback. | Core | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-002 | Lighting must own no simulation fact (no reach, output, `lit`, block, hook) and never write back to an emitter or report a cull to the simulation; the simulation never reads Lighting. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-003 | Lighting writes only CanvasItem, Material, Light2D, LightOccluder2D and CanvasModulate properties inside layer 0 of the world viewport owned by the Pixel-Perfect Pipeline. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-004 | Lighting persists nothing and registers no state block, step hook or `after:` entry; after restore (`post_restore`) it re-derives all lights/occluders from `emitter.state` and the Layout `version` counter, discarding governor windows, dwell timers, gutter epochs and caches. | Save/Load | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-005 | After restore, lit emitters, positions, radii and L0 energy must equal the pre-save picture; envelopes, gutter phases, fades and governor history may restart. | Save/Load | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-006 | Lighting and the Avatar Presenter own disjoint Light2D member sets: Lighting writes all value members (`enabled`, `energy`, `color`, `texture`, `texture_scale`, `offset`, shadow/range members, occluder_light_mask); the Presenter writes only `position`/`rotation` of the local beam, with Presenter-before-Lighting frame order. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-007 | Lighting wakes on dirty keys `E/<emitter id>` (`(KIND<<32) \| id`) and polls pose at render rate; geometry-bearing quantities (`r_lit_px`, `r_off_px`, `texture_scale`, energy) are computed from exact committed `output_pm`/`reach_px`/`r_lit_px`, never from the quantised dirty bucket; work runs at per-tick, per-frame and `FULL_RECONCILE_TICKS` (60) cadences with write-on-change. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-008 | Kernel clock (`tick`, `tick_alpha`) drives anything the sim also computes (beacon aim, poses, glowstick wane); `present_time` (frozen while PAUSED) drives cosmetic phase only (gutter, warm-up, fades, governor window); the tick counter is never used for cosmetic phase. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-light-009 | Exactly one light node is drawn per in-view emitter (plus BEAM_SPILL per bound seat beam), shaped from the 11-row `shapes()` table. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-010 | HARD cones must be drawn with the hard edge at `reach_px` +/-1 px along the aim axis, with `texture_scale = reach_px / measured edge radius` from an import-time sidecar, at every zoom rung and under both render passes. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-011 | SOFT pools must be drawn to the latch-release radius `r_off` (published `r_off_px`) +/-1 px and never smaller than `r_lit_px`, so prompts appear exactly where the light visibly ends. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-012 | Drawn energy = `LIGHT_ENERGY_PER_PM x KIND_GAIN[kind] x output_pm`, then modifier chain, then governor; it must never exceed the first term (a lamp never renders brighter than the power served). | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-013 | At most `SHADOW_SLOTS_MAX` (6) shadow-casting lights; slots 1-4 are beam seats, 5 is the beacon, 6 by fixed eligibility rank; vacant seat slots are lent (`SHADOW_LEND_SPARE`, `LEND_SLOTS_MAX` 3) to wall-near lights. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-014 | The occluder builder (PROJECTOR-class duty) builds LightOccluder2D from Layout `OccluderSource` rects with `version(section_id)` change detection (unconditional rebuild on restore), bounded by `OCCLUDER_POLYS_VIEW_MAX` 120, `OCCLUDER_VERTS_MAX` 12, `OCCLUDER_SYNC_BUDGET_US` 400. | Rendering | ADR-0014 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-015 | Shadow origin must match the simulation's segment origin (foot, via `body.state` pos_curr / `occ_origin`) versus chest apex, with a spike-decided mechanism (preference B, A, D; C rejected). | Rendering | ADR-0018 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-016 | Non-shadow lights are capped at `NONSHADOW_LIGHTS_MAX` 24 (lanterns a subset, `LANTERN_LIGHTS_MAX` 8); over-budget lights are demoted deterministically (priority, distance, emitter id) to an emissive stand-in drawn to the same edge, never dropped; emitters at output_pm 0 are outside the budget. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-017 | A non-shadow light must not draw where the simulation's segment test calls a compartment dark; containment (mechanism C0 via lent slots, C1 fallback) bounds pools by OCCLUDES_LIGHT rects, with `PD1_EXCEPTION_MAX_PX` 24. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-018 | Light LOD tiers are pure functions of the committed field and window (`rho_field`, `rho_px`), never of measured frame time, so renders are reproducible; zoom rungs are never removed, only quality tiered. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-019 | Zoom is implemented via world viewport logical size, never `Camera2D.zoom`; all Light2D length members are in base px, independent of `k_z` under both passes; Lighting has no zoom handler. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-020 | Modifier contract is an ordered multiplicative permille chain (L0 base, L1 recipes, L2 Attention tell, L3 Horror disturbance, then governor last), fed by state that Attention/Horror publish and Lighting reads; submitters never call Lighting and no layer may change reach, `lit`, colour of the real edge. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-021 | Decoys are unlit additive/mix overlays on a dedicated mask bit, never shadow-casting, never lights or emitters, bounded by `decoy_budget` (`DECOY_MAX_CH` 0.85); no real light may read PERCEPTION_OUT for enabled/energy/color/scale/offset/pose (isolation lint). | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-022 | Flash governor limits flashes (>=10% relative luminance pair) to `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 per rolling 1 s per light, per region and on the frame mean, computed from modelled decisions, not GPU readback. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-023 | `reduce_flashing` (default on for a fresh profile) makes deployables steady and arcs held glow while the beacon keeps its sweep; HR-15 low-vision raise is applied renderer-side per client. | UI | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-024 | Light-mask bit allocation (20 bits) covers per-body bits (`BODIES_LIVE_MAX` 4), colour/stand-in/decoy bits and z-bands; cross-body light degrades via `OTHER_BODY` stand-in. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-025 | A single shared HullSurface ShaderMaterial carries the floor `out = max(albedo x ambient_eff, floor)` and total ShaderMaterial shaders must be at most five; ambient is delivered per section via indirection (ring/home table). | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-026 | Emission glow uses the unshaded additive Emissive layer with `GLOW_HDR_THRESHOLD` (placeholder 1.3, must stay inside the D.8 interval); depends on `use_hdr_2d` and a world-viewport glow Environment (pre-tonemap glow since 4.6). | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-027 | Light textures are greyscale 8-bit PNG, linear filter, with authored hard-edge bands and sidecar-measured edge radius; texture memory budget 5.8 MiB (pass A) / 19.4-25.9 MiB (pass B). | Data | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-028 | Light-system CPU cost must stay at or below `LIGHT_CPU_BUDGET_US` (1000 us P99 steady, 3000 us event frame) with node pools pre-allocated (`LIGHT_NODE_POOL` 53, `OCCLUDER_NODE_POOL` 240, `STANDIN_POOL` 90), measured on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
