| TR-light-029 | Startup assertions must fail boot with a named error for: invalid glow threshold interval, missing `use_hdr_2d` or glow Environment, NEAREST light-texture filter, more than five ShaderMaterial shaders, bad texture layout. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-030 | Darkness is never a gameplay cost or meter: no light meter, vignette, fog mask, darkness debuff or gamma/brightness slider is permitted. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-031 | Lighting is split into a pure, engine-free plan step and an apply step, so the plan can be run headless against a stub with an injectable clock and a recorded frame-time trace, and an incremental pass must equal a full reconcile (including the `ALL` collapse above `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256). | Core | ADR-0028 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-032 | Cosmetic randomness (gutter gaps and lengths, flicker) is a pure hash function of (emitter id, epoch) with no global generator and no `rng_derive` stream, so the sequence is reproducible after restore without being persisted. | Core | ADR-0005 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-033 | The Emitter State commit path must itself enforce flash spacing (`SWITCH_MIN_SPACING_TICKS` 12) and count a defect when committed switches would breach the 3 flashes per second limit, because a command-level spacing guard alone is proven insufficient under arming-latency jitter. | Core | ADR-0017 + ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-light-034 | Ambient is computed per section from `mean_fixture_pm` over Graph-served fixtures only: self-powered fixtures (EMERGENCY lamp, dock status glow) are excluded from the mean, an empty section reads 20 permille, and the slew is bounded by `AMBIENT_SLEW_FRAMES`. | Data | ADR-0028 + ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-035 | Door occluders are derived continuously from Layout door `area`/`area_max` (leaf width `width_px` x (1 - area/area_max), no open-threshold constant), only Layout `OCCLUDES_LIGHT` rectangles produce occluders (never actors or horrors), and occluders must not be implemented as a TileSet layer. | Core | ADR-0014 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-036 | Every drawable class must darken correctly at zero lights: the HullSurface `character` variant and LIGHT_ONLY sprites go near-black or undrawn, and a default-material sprite is forbidden because it would render full albedo in a dark room; the carrier choice is fixed by the combine probe. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-037 | Repeated LOADING, build, body swap, restore and LOADING cycles (100 in the test) must leave node, orphan-node and render-texture counts equal to cycle 1, and the light and occluder pools are rebuilt without leaking. | Performance | ADR-0028 + ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-038 | All shader variants in the warm-up matrix are compiled during LOADING or TRANSIT_HOLD behind the hold quad so no variant compiles on first use in play; the Shader Baker effect is measured and recorded. | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-039 | GPU ms and VRAM ceilings (including the `hdr_2d` target at 8 bytes per pixel, the shadow atlas and glow buffers, per target and per pass) are recorded from RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK measurement into the `project.yaml` performance block before epics are created. | Performance | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-040 | Unverified engine capabilities (light-texture `offset`, per-item light cap, 20 canvas mask bits, `Light2D` under `size_2d_override`, `CanvasModulate` with unshaded items, engine light and shadow limits) are probed in the lighting spike and read back at startup; `LIGHT_NODE_POOL` above the read-back limit fails boot. | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-041 | Content lints run per push and fail the build: non-shadow placements must pass the containment (C3) lint with spill at most 24 px, no view chunk may be overlapped by more than `LIGHTS_PER_ITEM_MAX` 12 lights, no compartment may share a view with more than 4 fault glows, and three lights over one emissive stamp fail the glow interval. | Data | ADR-0007 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-042 | Containment mechanism is selected by config key `lighting.containment_mechanism` (`C0` default, `C1` fallback); any other value fails the run, and residual leak bounds are logged per build through `diag.contain_leak` counters that gate the build. | Data | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-light-043 | Lighting exposes named `DIAGNOSTIC` counters (`contain_leak`, `decoy_overflow`, `occluder_overflow`, `cap_exceeded_by_protected`, `max_item_overlap`, `demotions`, `k_z`) read by the debug overlay, and every pooled light node carries an `emitter_id` meta resolving to exactly one read-view row; all diagnostic draws are excluded from release builds. | Core | ADR-0031 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Player Controller & Input (`pc`)
GDD: `design/gdd/player-controller-input.md` | 46 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-pc-001 | The system is split into three parts: Input Adapter (presentation, submits PLAYER-origin commands as peer 1, writes no block), Controller Sim (owns `controller.state`), Avatar Presenter (PRESENTATION, never submits); a lint verifies the Adapter writes no block. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-002 | `controller.state` is registered as SIM, `persist=true`, `persist_scope=WORLD`, `scope=PER_PLAYER` keyed by PlayerId, `repl_hint=SHARED_SUMMARY`, holding intent, gait, committed aim, facing, loco_state, carry/channel refs, channel_face, chest point and hand point. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-pc-003 | Ship Body owns ship-local integer position (`pos`, `sub`, `odometer`, `LocomotionStep`); Controller Sim owns intent/gait/aim/facing/loco_state/carry/channel (PC-14, pending TD ratification). | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-004 | The Adapter samples gameplay intents in its own `_process` (before the TickDriver, by process priority, not in the physics callback) so an intent submitted in frame N is applied in P2 and integrated in P3 of the same tick (one tick latency, never two). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-pc-005 | Hook order is `suit.vitals` -> `controller.step` (divisor 1) -> `layout.doors` -> `body.integrate` -> `controller.noise` and `emitter.place`; Attention (divisor 6) integrates pushed noise; the order is asserted from the hook registry. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-pc-006 | `set_move_intent {octant 0..7\|8=NONE, run}` and `set_aim {aim_angle int 1/4096 turn, live}` are intents with `coalesce=LATEST_PER_ACTOR_PER_TICK`, `max_age_ticks=30`; all other commands use `coalesce=NONE`, `max_age_ticks=120`; all use `actor_binding=OWNED_BY_ISSUER` with `actor_source`. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-007 | The Adapter sends `set_move_intent` only on change (8 octants, `MOVE_DEADZONE`=0.25, `OCTANT_HYSTERESIS_DEG`=5), submits it before any command in the same frame, and sends `set_aim` at most once per `AIM_SEND_INTERVAL`=3 ticks (<=26 commands/s total). | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-008 | No input buffering: `ACTION_BUFFER_TICKS`=0; a press during a channel is submitted and answered honestly (`BUSY`/`MOVING`), nothing is queued. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-009 | The Adapter keeps `last_acked` per intent and resends its current value once, bypassing the change filter, on the five resync triggers (STALE reject, leaving PAUSED, LOADING->RUNNING, focus/gamepad return, body TRANSFER); focus or gamepad loss sends `set_move_intent {NONE}` and `cancel_channel`. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-010 | Exactly one input context is active (`gameplay`, `chart_table`, `inspect_card`, `chat`); the context x action matrix governs which actions are live, and STAGGERED/DOWNED/DEAD/IN_POD force a return to `gameplay`; `chart_table` entry and `chat` entry cancel channels, `inspect_card` does not. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-011 | Game code reads named rebindable actions (StringName), never raw keys or `device == 0`; devices are identified by event type; per-context conflict checking applies. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-012 | The Adapter publishes a presentation-local MenuInput event stream (NAV_DOWN/UP, ACCEPT, BACK, CLOSE, FOCUS_IN/OUT, DEVICE_CHANGED, POINTER) sampled on render frames with echo suppression, deadzone hysteresis, cardinal-axis rule and latch-until-released. | UI | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-013 | Movement is hand-rolled integer kinematics (`LocomotionStep`, pure RefCounted, 1/256-px fixed point, axis-separated 1-px AABB sweeps, diagonal 181/256) with no physics body and no CharacterBody2D; avatar position is a pure function of command stream and layout (deterministic for replay G3/G4). | Physics | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-014 | Speeds are whole base px per tick: `v_walk`=2, `v_run`=3, `v_carry`=1; `v_max`=`v_run` must satisfy build invariants I1 (`LAG_CUT` > v_max*tau) and I2 (`v_run*TICK_RATE*FOLLOW_TAU + L_MAX <= 0.30*field_h`); the loader refuses data violating any of the 19 invariants, naming exactly the violated set. | Data | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-015 | Tick-expressed knobs (units `ticks` or `px/tick`) are invalidated by a `TICK_RATE` change, detected data-driven from the unit column. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-016 | Facing is recomputed every tick from committed state with a 2-quanta (1/128 turn) hysteresis and the six-level priority order (frozen / channel_face / running / aim-live / aim-idle moving / hold); a MIRROR client never recomputes facing or channel_face from aim. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-017 | The Controller Sim publishes a SIM read view (`octant`, `run`, `gait_effective`, `aim_angle`, `aim_live`, `facing`, `loco_state`, `carry_ref`, `salvage_carry`, `channel_ref`, chest point, hand point); Attention and Horror read only `loco_state` and never other Controller state. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-018 | Four Class B mutators (`set_channel_ref(actor, owner_system, channel_id, face_octant)`, `clear_channel_ref`, `set_carry_ref`, `clear_carry_ref`) are the only cross-block write path; they take the shared `WriteScope`, report via `scope.fault`, reject `face_octant` outside 0..8, and callers propagate the status. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-019 | Every kernel-invoked Controller callable (`validate`, `apply`, `controller.step`, `controller.noise`, `snapshot`, `restore`, `post_restore`, `read_view`) receives `ctx` and ends in `return ctx.complete()`. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-pc-020 | `snapshot()` normalises transient fields (`octant=NONE`, `run=false`, `channel_ref/channel_face=none`, moving loco_states written as IDLE) while `restore()` is a whole-block replace, so `snapshot(restore(snapshot()))` is canonical-byte identical; `controller.state` is not in `REBASE_BLOCKS` and restore verification is EXACT; integer vectors persist as ints or packed arrays, never `Vector2i`. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-021 | The persisted payload contains no Adapter-local state (`last_acked`, hold timers, run latch, device, context, pre-play tokens). | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-022 | DEAD hand-off: the death stub makes exactly one `request_restore`, honoured only after Save/Load's epoch staging reports `EPOCH_DURABLE`; `loco_state` stays DEAD with no further transition and the seat accepts no commands. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-023 | `give_up {actor}` (PLAYER origin, `coalesce=NONE`, `max_age_ticks=120`) is valid only in DOWNED solo (else `NOT_DOWNED`) and sets `death_tick := min(death_tick, T)`; `stagger {actor}` is SYSTEM-origin only, rejected `NOT_STAGGERABLE` in DOWNED/DEAD/IN_POD, and does not extend the lock. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-024 | Command authority: a command with `issuer_peer` not owning the actor is `Rejected(ACTOR_NOT_OWNED)` at P2 with no state change; no shared or assisted control exists; the Adapter attaches an opaque integer `correlation` and keys pre-play tokens by it; pre-play is body/sound only, never the effect or beam light. | Networking | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-pc-025 | The Adapter never reads Sanity, Attention or PERCEPTION_OUT views (input mapping, deadzones, aim source are HR-6 anchors); the Controller's Suit read is `locomotion_cap` only; Sanity may never alter bindings, aim or movement. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-026 | The Avatar Presenter writes only `frame`, `flip_h`, integer `position`, overlay `visible` and the Light2D `position`/`rotation`; never scale, rotation, skew, modulate or tweens on them; position is `floor(lerp(prev, curr, tick_alpha))` with zero fractional part; it contains no HOST/MIRROR branch. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-027 | Controller and body sources contain no forbidden time/RNG calls (`Time.get_ticks_*`, `get_process_delta_time`, `randi`, `randf`, `RandomNumberGenerator`) except allow-listed render-time timers in Adapter/Presenter. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-028 | Under co-op, `controller.state` and `body.state` replicate as `SHARED_SUMMARY`; the replicated `aim_angle` quantum may not exceed `FACING_HYSTERESIS` (64 units) or boot faults with `REPL_QUANTUM_TOO_COARSE{aim_angle}`. | Networking | ADR-0032 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-029 | Per-component CPU sub-budgets (advisory, RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release export): Adapter <=0.1 ms/frame, `LocomotionStep` <=0.05 ms/actor/tick (2,000 rects), `controller.step` <=0.02 ms/actor/tick, Presenter <=0.1 ms/avatar/frame, diagnostics <=0.01 ms/tick. | Performance | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-030 | Dev-only artifacts (`PC_*` diagnostics, `debug_overlay_*` actions, input logger) are absent from release exports, except Ship Body's `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP`; the ten `debug_overlay_*` actions are registered only under the dev feature tag and never appear in Settings or rebinding. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-031 | Raw mouse aim is computed once per frame from the render pipeline's published follow position `f_q` and screen-to-picture mapping (never `pose_q`), rounded once to 1/4096-turn units with `posmod`, so camera look-ahead cannot feed back into aim; the Adapter warps the OS cursor on zoom edges and aim-source handover to keep the aim unchanged. | Input | ADR-0019 + ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-032 | All `Input.*`, `InputMap`, `InputEvent`, `_input` and `_unhandled_input` use lives only in the Adapter's registered files (MenuInput publisher included); the Adapter hands cursor position and zoom edges to the pipeline root, which makes no Input read of its own, and a lint enforces this. | Input | ADR-0019 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-033 | Key bindings use `physical_keycode` so non-QWERTY layouts keep WASD positions, multiple wheel events in one rendered frame coalesce to at most one `zoom_in`/`zoom_out` edge, and the OS cursor is hidden and confined in `gameplay` and released in `chart_table`. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-034 | Every channel-owner hook registers `after: [controller.step]`; an interrupt (move intent, cap change, stagger, target invalidation, body transfer, restore) reaches the owner only as the Controller clearing `channel_ref` through state, never as a command result, and the owner aborts before accruing progress on that tick. | Core | ADR-0020 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-pc-035 | `controller.step` detects a change of `body_of_actor` reported by Ship Body (pod hop, section detach) and on that tick clears `channel_ref`, `channel_face` and `channel_body`, sets `pos_prev == pos_curr` and resets `sub`; a weld that leaves the actor's body id unchanged keeps the channel. | Core | ADR-0019 + ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-036 | `controller.noise` pushes exactly one `push_stimulus(NOISE, actor, class)` per tick with class chosen by `loco_state` (WALK, RUN, CARRY) and none when Body's derived `moved` is zero, in non-locomotion states or from `controller.step`; BURDEN is pushed every tick while `salvage_carry` is set, at a data-driven `U_BURDEN`. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-037 | The registered `controller.state` schema, its published read view and its snapshot payload must agree field by field (incl. `cap_seen`, `ramp_ticks`, `walk_ticks`, `last_octant`, `decel_k`, `stagger_end_tick`, `death_tick`, `channel_body`); `cap_seen` persists so a restore never re-fires the cap falling-edge stagger. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-pc-038 | Render-time confirm-bar and tap-window timers advance by frame delta clamped to `CONFIRM_DT_MAX_S`, are frozen (effective delta zero) while the kernel is PAUSED, and resume where they stopped; idle animation frames advance from sim tick `T`, not wall time. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-pc-039 | Controller and Presenter outputs (sim blocks, `set_move_intent`/`set_aim` submission logs, Presenter frame/position/light logs) are byte-identical across render cadences of 30, 60, 120 and 144 fps, across calm/tell/breach/sanity-stage fixtures, and with `aim_indicator` on or off; a recorded input-trace replay format is required. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 + ADR-0009 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-pc-040 | The Presenter writes only beam pose (`Light2D` position and rotation); 2D Lighting writes `enabled`, `energy`, `texture` and `color` from `emitter.state`, and the Presenter's pose write must precede Lighting's apply in the same render callback; a beam toggle press never changes light state before committed state does. | Rendering | ADR-0009 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |

| TR-pc-042 | The Adapter chooses the command for `interact` solely from Interaction's per-object declaration (`command_type`, mode TAP/HOLD_CONFIRM/CHANNEL) with no verb branch keyed on object class; candidates need reach, arc and Emitter exposure above threshold, and `ACTOR_SELF` verbs are submitted with own PlayerId as target through an `act_gate` exempt row. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-pc-043 | The Adapter publishes per-seat `confirm_view(seat)` and `glyph_view(seat, action)` read views that HUD plates (including the give-up plate) are driven by alone; the Adapter publishes no `input_ignored_view`, and ignored or rejected presses cause no HUD change or camera jolt. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Interaction System (`interact`)
GDD: `design/gdd/interaction-system.md` | 43 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-interact-001 | Interaction registers one command type per verb (begin_patch, begin_cut, begin_cap, begin_crank, cancel_channel, set_breaker, set_valve, top_up, set_opening, pickup, drop, throw_glowstick, field_check, begin_harm, begin_revive), each with its own validate, reason subset, max_age_ticks 120, coalesce NONE, actor_binding OWNED_BY_ISSUER with actor_source body.state, allowed_origins {PLAYER} and not allowed_when_paused; `interact` is an input action, never a command type. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-002 | Command payloads are primitives only (a registered subject ref, a PlayerId, or aim_angle for throw_glowstick and the closed check_kind {SCAN}); a payload never carries a believed state, direction, verb choice or kind. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-003 | Only set_opening admits SYSTEM origin (failure-cascade door close); all other Interaction commands are PLAYER origin. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-004 | The verb declaration table is data, one row per (object class, tool requirement) under assets/data/interaction/verbs/, and takes no sanity, Attention, rhythm, breach or device input (data-load lint); adding a verb is one data row, one handler and one registration line with an empty Adapter diff. | Data | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-005 | select_verb is a pure function of (object classes, tool in hand, eligible predicate) and never of hp, state, forced mode or served power. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-006 | Every non-exempt PLAYER command validates act_gate(o) = in_reach(o) AND lit(o) against the exposure table committed at tick T-1; exemptions are exactly drop, toggle_beam, the inspect card of an already-lit instrument (plus actor verbs throw_glowstick and the ACTOR_SELF self-patch); an unregistered ref answers exactly as an unlit one. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-007 | A reason depending on module body state (hp, state, forced mode, served power) is never returned at press time: the command is Applied, the full channel runs, and only then resolves to an outcome (NOTHING_TO_REPAIR, NOT_REPAIRABLE, STILL_HOT); immediate reasons are limited to geometry, light, the actor's own stock/state and the consequence channel. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-008 | A channel is one record per seat in interaction.state, created by the verb's apply in P2 together with the Controller's set_channel_ref and advanced only by hook interaction.step, accruing from e=1 to D, with D mod GRAPH_STEP_TICKS(6) == 0 for every *_CHANNEL_TICKS (lint) and seats processed in ascending PlayerId. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-009 | interaction.step runs `after: emitter.place` (reads tick T's exposure table) and re-checks lit and in_reach every LIT_RECHECK_TICKS (legal values 1,2,3,6; GRAPH_STEP_TICKS mod LIT_RECHECK_TICKS == 0) so no step is credited on an unlit or out-of-reach block end. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-010 | Every Interaction predicate and formula is integer-only, uses no RNG and no wall-clock, reads body.state.pos and the Emitter State table, and no physics query (Godot Physics 2D) is permitted anywhere in the system; throws use an integer direction table with no runtime trigonometry. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-011 | Interaction holds no scene-tree reference and never draws, subscribes to results or calls presentation; it exposes only the read views focus_view and channel_view. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-012 | One in-progress job per (subject_ref, verb class) with classes REPAIR, SALVAGE, CAP, CRANK, CHECK, WELD, ACTOR_ACT; a second begin gets immediate BUSY, nothing queues, and a FULL step on a subject already at hp = hp_cap = HP_FULL resolves PRECONDITION(NOTHING_TO_REPAIR) consuming no part. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-013 | cancel_channel cancels whatever controller.state.channel_ref names as (owner_system, channel_id); all channel records live in interaction.state regardless of owner, and owner-registered verbs (Assembly begin_weld/begin_seam_cut) create theirs via Class B open_channel(seat, owner_system, verb_id, target_ref, duration_ticks). | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-014 | interaction.state is a SIM block, persist true, persist_scope WORLD, scope SHARED with per-seat records keyed by PlayerId (never transport peer id), repl_hint SHARED_SUMMARY, schema_version 1, restore_after [layout.state, controller.state, graph.state, body.state, emitter.state]. | Save/Load | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-interact-015 | The block persists the partial-progress ledger (MAX_PARTIAL_ENTRIES 16, PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS 600), FULL-job part records with grace entries (MAX_GRACE_ENTRIES 8, FULL_JOB_GRACE_S 60), throws[], loose[] (item instances), stubs[], last_salvage_T and id counters, plus per-seat last_result/prev_result. | Save/Load | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-016 | post_restore drops all in-progress channels (ABORT(RESTORE)), door attempts and last_result, resolves a RESERVED FULL-job part from the persisted blocks_done/t_last_block (released if 0, consumed if >=1), keeps ledgers/throws/loose/stubs, and re-registers all subjects idempotently before the first emitter.place. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-017 | Each loose[].inst is decoded in post_restore with Item DB from_array(a, graph_step_no); a wrong length, element type or failed validate_instance fails the restore with the closed reason INTERACTION_RESTORE_LOOSE_INSTANCE, leaving live state untouched and never clamping. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-018 | Interaction never touches carry_ref or suit.state.carried_id on restore, and writes interaction.state only through its own mutators, calling the Controller-published set_channel_ref/clear_channel_ref/set_carry_ref/clear_carry_ref (CR-9). | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-019 | focus_view(seat) and channel_view(seat) are pure, integer, enum/key-only (no strings, no node refs) read views computed after the tick commits and keyed by PlayerId; progress_pm and duration_class derive from declared duration and the player's own elapsed ticks, never from hp. | UI | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-020 | channel_view.state is restricted to IDLE, CONFIRMED, PROGRESS; terminal results (DONE, ABORT, OUTCOME) are read only from last_result/prev_result (depth 2), edge-detected via monotone transition_seq/result_seq; the Assembly DONE handoff is a separate one-tick field. | UI | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-021 | The HUD reads exactly four sources (focus_view, channel_view, Controller's input_view, local result-event record with no reason) and never interaction.state, node()/edge() or any PERCEPTION_OUT block; hold-to-confirm progress is Adapter-local, never in a simulation view. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-022 | Subjects register with Emitter State (E6) only while inside the registration window W (rooms within SUBJECT_WINDOW_HOPS of a bound body's room, one-hop hysteresis), recomputed at most once per tick in room-id order and on restore/instantiate/discard/dormancy; sync_section maintains the candidate table and dormant sections must unregister. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-023 | Subject budget: INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET 120 + PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE 24 <= 144 (75% of MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS 192), lint-checked per window W_int(p) at every authored body position; runtime overflow is a content defect counted IA_SUBJECT_OVERFLOW; drop/begin_cut refuse OBSTRUCTED when subject_headroom() is insufficient. | Performance | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-024 | TOOL-class verbs call Attention push_stimulus(NOISE, source_ref, class) once per accrued channel tick (none on a non-accrued tick) and LOUD one-shots at cut completion, seam-cut DONE, vent apply, throw landing and berth apply, with relations 2<=U_TOOL<=6, U_LOUD>=30*U_TOOL and PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS <= 2*TAU_ATT_TICKS. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-025 | Class A Graph mutators M6-M9 and M11-M13 (and Class B M16 for top_up) are called only through their check_* validate counterparts, with physical result codes; module-body truth is read only inside the hook via node()/edge(). | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-026 | Door set_opening validate uses Layout leaf_would_overlap and Ship Body foot_boxes(body_id) (unfiltered) for OBSTRUCTED/CLOSING_BLOCKED; PLAYER close pending times out at CLOSING_BLOCKED_TIMEOUT_TICKS, SYSTEM close has no timeout; TAP_REARM_TICKS >= door travel time. | Physics | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-027 | Throw landing and drop placement use Layout integer geometry (clip_to_footprints, floor_tile_at, loose_slots) and spawn_deployable (E3); THROW_RANGE_TILES > GLOWSTICK_RADIUS_TILES and THROW_ARC_TICKS < ticks(GLOWSTICK_LIFE_S). | Physics | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-028 | The friendly-harm act is gated by Sanity's actor_sanity_below_harm_threshold eligible predicate, calls Suit apply_suit_damage with HARM_BP_PER_STEP per block against HARM_TOTAL_BP, and is live only with stub actors at MVP (begin_harm/begin_revive Vertical Slice B); HORROR subjects take no verb. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-029 | Pause stops the tick: no diegetic verb is admitted (PAUSED, never queued), channels do not advance and ledger TTLs do not age; stagger clears the channel, downed clears channel and carry, pod_hop aborts ABORT(BODY). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-interact-030 | A gameplay value (all tuning knobs) lives in data under assets/data/interaction/ as tick counts (never hardcoded; changing TICK_RATE invalidates them), and Interaction refuses stats, reason strings on screen, damage numbers beyond HARM_TOTAL_BP, cooldowns and rewards. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-031 | The fifteen Interaction command types are registered at boot with coalesce NONE, actor_binding OWNED_BY_ISSUER, actor_source body.state, max_age_ticks 120 and not allowed_when_paused; none is named interact or use_item, and the Interaction public surface is exactly sync_section, place_loose and open_channel plus focus_view, channel_view and last_salvage_T (boot-registry test). | Core | ADR-0002 + ADR-0020 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-interact-032 | interaction.step is registered with divisor 1, offset 0 and after [controller.step, emitter.place] using verbatim hook ids; it runs Class A mutators M6, M7, M11, M12 in P3 inside a kernel WriteScope, M13 only in P2 from set_opening, and the Graph reads a Class A change at the next multiple of 6 (lag 1 to 6 ticks). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0003 + ADR-0020 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-interact-033 | Every write to interaction.state packed arrays marks its dirty key, and snapshot-restore-snapshot with all tables populated is byte-identical; replaying a command log twice yields identical state_hash on every tick. | Save/Load | ADR-0004 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-034 | The FULL-repair part uses the Suit reserve_stowed / release_stowed / consume_reserved contract with a commit rule independent of module truth: released if cancelled before the first credited block, consumed after it; stock, cue_load and reserved bucket are identical across healthy and damaged runs at every tick, and no RESERVED record exists without a running channel. | Core | ADR-0027 + ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-035 | An item instance moves losslessly between Suit (release_carried, replace_tool), salvage yield, seeded placement (fresh_instance) and loose[] via place_loose(inst, section_id, pos_px), equal element for element to the Item DB item_instance_to_array form; loose positions are section-local and unchanged by weld or detach. | Data | ADR-0027 + ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-036 | World build order is build_section, Graph M1, Emitter E1, then Interaction sync_section; discarding a section unregisters all its subjects and drops its loose[], and a save with a throw in flight lands the stick on the same absolute tick with the same life_ticks, spawning the deployable at land_tick so the first lit table is land_tick + 1. | Core | ADR-0026 + ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-037 | SYSTEM set_opening retry for a blocked door is capped: a foot box held in a leaf for 300 ticks yields at most 2 SYSTEM commands per door and a 10-door cascade at most 20 in 300 ticks, with identical replay hashes; a door attempt window is 30 ticks and attempts[] and rearm[] are not persisted. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-038 | The Interaction hook meets a release-export budget on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK under a worst-case load (4 seats, full ledgers, 16 throws, 12 attempts, a window recompute every 30 ticks, 10 SYSTEM door resubmits per tick): mean at most 0.10 ms, p99 at most 0.50 ms, max below STEP_BUDGET_MS, zero MAX_PROBES_PER_TICK use (lit() is a table lookup). | Performance | ADR-0012 + ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-039 | No PERCEPTION_OUT block is reachable from any simulation validate, apply or hook, and no Perception Filter code path calls register_subject or submits a command; validate is verified with a Graph spy that fails on any node(), edge(), opening() or switch-position read. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-040 | Interaction exposes IA_* diagnostic counters (IA_REJECT, IA_CHANNEL_ABORT, IA_RESERVE_COMMIT, IA_SUBJECT_OVERFLOW, and others; IA_PHANTOM_PROBE development builds only) registered under the diag_panel_contract naming convention, each incrementing once per triggering edge, none readable by the HUD, none present in release builds. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-041 | A field_check SCAN calls Sanity record_falsification zero times on every tick of the channel including the begin tick, exactly once on the terminal tick, and zero on abort, cancel or restore; interaction.state stores no result, and the call site lives only in interaction.step's completion branch. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-042 | Simulation outcomes are independent of presentation settings: changing zoom step or HUD scale mid-channel leaves state_hash equal to a control run, REACH_PX is evaluated in base pixels, and keyboard-mouse, gamepad and toggle-versus-hold policies yield identical command streams and channel_view series. | Input | ADR-0019 + ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-interact-043 | Two commands applied on one tick (two throws, two seats on one subject) are processed in seq order with the second validate seeing the first apply; a press during a channel or a rejected press queues nothing (ACTION_BUFFER_TICKS 0), and a cancel_channel wins over a same-tick abort cause. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |

### Failure & Trail System (`ftrail`)
GDD: `design/gdd/failure-and-trail-system.md` | 44 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-ftrail-001 | The system registers no state block, no step hook and no command; the only members persisting across a Graph step are pooled scene-node refs, the last read_view poll cache and `diag_latch`; the whole trail set rebuilds bit-identically from one `ALL` plus `present_time`. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-002 | Every trail visual property is a pure function of read_view fields (`failures()`, `compartment()`, `node()`, `edge()`), Layout geometry and `present_time`; no timer, fade, Tween, spawn stamp or smoothing accumulator exists. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-003 | All trail nodes carry the TRUTH_PX PerceptionTag grade, read `SIM` only (never `PERCEPTION_OUT`), never call `true_value()`, and render bit-identically with the Perception Filter pass on or off. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-004 | Every drawn element corresponds to a Graph descriptor (key `(subject_kind, subject_id, mode, medium, anchor)`); no descriptor means no element, and no ambient or decorative mist/decal not backed by a fault may be drawn. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-005 | All draw decisions (state, size_idx, chain, extent, reveal threshold, density, cover16, dull_step, glow_step, hue, band, anim frame, in-field test) are computed by one pure headless `RefCounted` resolver `resolve(descriptor, ctx) -> TrailSpec` with a `prepare_feed(descriptors, ctx)` pre-pass; the scene layer is a thin applier that decides nothing. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-006 | The resolver's inputs are injectable seams (descriptor feed, geometry provider, present_time, settings, knob table in a data file, world_seed, logger sink, `hash_fn` defaulting to kernel `rng_derive`) and it references no autoload, clock or random function. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-007 | Notification handler only stores/unions key sets and returns; reads and draws happen in the next render callback; a union exceeding `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256 or containing `ALL` is treated as `ALL`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-008 | read_view is polled once per Graph step (detected by changed `step_no`, `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6) into a per-step poll cache; extent, dullness and animation phase are read from the cache every frame because age fields and continuous flow/T dirty no key. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-009 | On `ALL` (restore, derelict instantiation, >256 keys) the system performs one full re-read reconciled against the node pool within the same render frame, with logging thresholds (not cut-offs) `TRAIL_RESTORE_BUDGET_MS` 8 ms and `TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS` 4 ms. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-010 | An idle render frame (no new step_no, no keys, no anim_frame change, no camera-field change) performs zero scene writes and zero resolver calls; pixel-neutral reducers (changed-property-only writes, retained draw lists, per-room batching) precede any degradation. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-011 | Trail extent is `min(edge length, GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev] x grow_steps, [FREEZE only] frost_reach)` floored to integer base px, never below `MIN_EXTENT_PX`; `grow_steps` (frozen at isolation) and `mode_steps` come from the Graph's GA-T onset triple (`mode_since_step`, `mode_key`, `isolated_at_step`); all arithmetic integer. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-012 | `GROW_PX_PER_STEP` must be integer, monotone non-decreasing, with [1] >= 1 and `V_WALK` 12 px/step < [4] <= `V_RUN` 18 px/step (placeholder 2/3/6/15). | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-013 | Size is a function of `sev` only (s 48 px at sev 1-2, m 96 at 3, l 144 at 4, sev 0 nothing; PUDDLE follows volume tier) via distinct drawings, never sprite scaling, with chain taper by `TAPER_STAMPS`. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-014 | Pixel integrity: nearest sampling, integer `floor` positions, no scale animation, rotation 0, binary alpha (0/255) on every sprite including mist, palette lock, hairlines/seams as sprites (never runtime line draws), minimum feature 2x2 px (1 px hairline composite excepted). | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-015 | Static marks are stamps from three-size sheets chained as cap/run/tip/corner/terminal with the last piece revealed by integer threshold; density is a world-anchored 2x2-px dither (no partial alpha) so every client draws identical pixels. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-016 | Moving media are pooled stepped sprite frames at `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` 12 indexed from `present_time` (never wall clock or tick); GPUParticles2D permitted only for BREACH/AIRFLOW venting after spike S1 proves floor-to-grid positions and binary alpha. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-017 | Draw bands: floor trails z+1 never Y-sorted; wall trails in the single Y-sorted wall layer z+10 at host-face sort origin; moving media in dust/particles z+30; no trail uses z 0 or +20, and all marks sit under the hull-floor material. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-018 | Trails are lit albedo marks with emissive layer only on scorch/arc/ember; the system writes no Light2D, registers no emitter or exposure, and supplies a descriptor-to-glow data table (`GLOW_BASE_PM[key]`, `SEV_GLOW_PM[0..4]`) consumed by Emitter State. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-019 | No on-screen consequence-channel element (descriptor with medium != NONE, sev >= 1) is ever culled: the in-field test uses the polyline bounding box padded by one head cell, no distance or darkness culling is permitted, and cost pressure degrades moving media only (frame count, density, anim fps, far-hold), never stamps. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-020 | Budgets hold at every zoom ladder step k in [1, k_auto+2] and at 1080p/1440p/2160p: `D_FT` <= 38 draws (60 if Filter mask renders trails twice), `stamps_world` <= `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` 704 (log-only, node pool pre-sized to it), `TRAIL_OVERDRAW_MAX` 4; conditional on spike S2 (per-instance data route to CanvasItem shaders). | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-021 | Per-instance reveal threshold, dull step, coverage rank and clock must reach the shader via a route proven by spike S2 (per-trail `_draw`, MultiMesh custom data or Sprite2D pool; fallback packs into instance COLOR/`_msk` G), and S2's result is recorded in an ADR before the first render story. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-022 | Variation (stamp variant, drip phase, fan flip) derives only from `rng_derive(world_seed, "trail.vis", (subject_id<<20) \| stamp_index)` so all clients and restores draw identical trails; engine global random functions and RNG instances are lint-forbidden. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-023 | Trail visuals are deterministic across restore: nothing persists in this system (extent/dullness derive from the Graph-persisted onset fields), so a restored world appears already grown with no start-up animation. | Save/Load | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-024 | A gas leak (`1 LEAK/GAS`, `8 SEVERED/GAS`) at `intensity_pm` 0 and `27 PATCH_WEEP/GAS` draw zero pixels; the HISS acoustic channel is the tell and is not suppressed. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-025 | Trails are never drawn on a machine body sprite, gauge, inspect card or crewmate; scorch/shimmer/glow anchor to the descriptor's fitting or port and floor/wall marks (module-body channel is corruptible, trail channel is not). | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-026 | LIVE vs STILL is `intensity_pm` > 0 (or per-row LIVE predicate on node()/edge() true values for ids 2 and 3); rows 4/7/9/27 are DRAWN_STATIC; dullness (D.5 ramp of `mode_steps`) applies to static stamps only, live descriptors are capped at `DULL_STEPS`-2 (rows 7, 27 held there; 4, 9 uncapped), with `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` >= 7500. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-027 | Non-dominant descriptors per subject (dominance by highest sev, ties to lowest mode) draw head stamp only at `MIN_EXTENT_PX` (except the seam weep, which uses `since_steps`); dominance is computed statelessly in the feed pre-pass and a MIRROR client derives it from summary `(mode, sev)` pairs. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-028 | Arc/ember flicker uses one shared world phase at `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` <= `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 with regional amplitude scaling by world-fixed arc count; "reduce flashing" (default ON) holds glow at `ARC_HELD_GLOW_PM` with four static spark-tip pixels; reduced motion holds each moving medium on frame 0 and never suppresses a trail. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-029 | The two accessibility toggles are read-only through the `SettingsStore` seam (CT-18/CT-19) and the system owns no setting. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-030 | Anchor geometry (`anchor_geometry`, `room_surface`, corridor clearance) is taken from Layout (LY-8, LY-23, polyline axis-aligned, wall part first) in base px; a corridor narrower than the head cell is a Layout content error, never a runtime clip/clamp; the 144 px clearance is a free interval normal to the run. | Core | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-031 | Diagnostics are edge-triggered: each `(log_id, subject_id)` latches in `diag_latch` on the rising edge, writes one line, re-arms when cleared, and no draw/reconcile/selection path reads the latch. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-032 | Co-op MIRROR (VS-B, not MVP) consumes `SHARED_SUMMARY` descriptors `(id, mode, sev, alarm_eligible, intensity bucket, since_steps, mode_steps, grow_steps)` to produce identical presentation to HOST; MVP path is exercised via the descriptor-feed seam against a mocked summary. | Networking | ADR-0032 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-033 | Tests: resolver unit tests run headless under tests/unit/failure_and_trail/ against data-file fixtures (fx_cryo, fx_tell, fx_row_oracle, fx_steady, fx_mvp_worst, fx_design_max, fx_seam); rendered criteria run on a windowed `ft-render` job on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-034 | Per-frame-class CPU budgets on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release exports, nearest-rank p99 over at least 100 samples per class: idle 0.25 ms, pan 0.5 ms, animation flip 0.75 ms (0.25 ms if S2 makes the frame index shader-driven), step frame 1.5 ms, measured under a scripted V_WALK run of at least 600 Graph steps. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-035 | All materials and shader variants of the table of record are instantiated by a load-time warm-up draw and the stamp node pool is pre-sized before the first descriptor resolves, so the first leak of a session costs at most 1.5x the median frame time (every one of 10 cold launches) and logs no compile event. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-036 | Every Graph descriptor id has a row in the table of record, enforced by a build-time lint; at run time an unknown id draws nothing and a record missing mode_steps or grow_steps draws the head stamp only, each writing one diagnostic per (log_id, subject_id) rising edge. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-037 | The knob and constant tables are validated at data load or by lint, which refuse and never clamp: knob invariants, derived read-only values such as ARC_FLICKER_PER_S, A_HOLE_MAX and hole-threshold desync, and every registry constant cited by the GDD must equal design/registry/entities.yaml. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-038 | The Graph must preserve a seeded onset triple (mode_since_step with matching mode_key) through phase 9, advance both onsets across a dormant interval (M19), and restart them on re-weld; the fixture loader rejects a mode_since_step seeded without mode_key. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-039 | When the kernel is PAUSED, extent, animation frame index and all trail animation stay unchanged because every animated quantity derives from present_time, which must hold still while paused. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-ftrail-040 | All dirty keys of one Graph step (for example N/ with B/, or a LEAK despawn with its PATCH_WEEP spawn) are applied in a single render callback, so no frame shows new size with old reach or both or neither of a handover pair. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-041 | Emitter State derives FAULT_GLOW records from the trail glow table on the Graph tick of the descriptor and never later, capped at MAX_FAULT_GLOW 10 keeping highest sev with ties by ascending descriptor id; the trail emissive layer adds nothing to the exposure query. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-042 | The system creates no audio node and waits on no audio; a trail's acoustic class is anchored at the same Layout-derived source position as its trail. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-043 | No trail material may read the screen texture or displace UVs, keeping trail pixels independent of the Perception Filter and any screen-space pass. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-ftrail-044 | Mapping a 64-bit rng_derive result to variant, phase and flip indices must yield non-negative indices for every bit pattern, including all-ones and the sign bit alone, using the signed 64-bit semantics of GDScript. | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Suit & Equipment (`suit`)
GDD: `design/gdd/suit-and-equipment.md` | 43 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-suit-001 | Suit registers exactly one state block `suit.state` (class SIM, persist true, persist_scope WORLD, scope PER_PLAYER keyed by PlayerId, schema_version 1, restore_after []) and exactly one step hook `suit.vitals`. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-suit-002 | `suit.vitals` runs at divisor 6, offset 2, `after: [graph.propagate.atmo]` only (never after `emitter.place`), and the chain `suit.vitals` -> `controller.step` -> `body.integrate` -> `emitter.place` holds; boot fails on an unknown `after` id or a cycle. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0027 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-suit-003 | Per-seat data is keyed only by PlayerId (int) and item `def_id`; no peer_id, node path or engine object appears in the block. | Data | ADR-0027 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0027) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-suit-004 | Each hook run processes bound seats in ascending PlayerId in the fixed order of SE-39 (skip unbound/DEAD, resolve room + pressure window, reconcile ledger, breath source, leak debit, cell drain, thermal, SCORCH/arc, ladders, derive outputs and dirty keys). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0027 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-suit-005 | Suit uses no RNG, no wall clock and no `delta`; all block fields are int32-safe integers or plain-data dictionaries (no floats), so drain, ladder and cue thresholds are deterministic and replayable. | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-suit-006 | Suit's hook, mutators and block contain no `Node`; the system is scene-tree-free. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |

| TR-suit-010 | Restore failures use the closed reason set (`SUIT_RESTORE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`, `_FIELD_RANGE`, `_STOWED_OVERFLOW`, `_UNKNOWN_DEF`, `_BEAM_SHAPE`, `_CARRY_MISMATCH`, `_SEAT_UNBOUND`, `_DATA_REV_UNKNOWN`, `_INSTANCE_FORM`). | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |

Dormant at MVP. | Core | ADR-0027 | — | ❌ Gap |

| TR-suit-023 | The five readings (O2 numeral, reserve, integrity, warmth, charge) plus the TRUTH-class bit `suit.alarm` reach the HUD only via `true_value(key, player_id)` wrapped by Perception Filter `perceived`; body cues are a pure function of band enums and `suit.state` and are never faked, suppressed or tinted by Sanity or Attention. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |

| TR-suit-033 | Boundary rules are enforced by CI lints that parse source and data: no band-constant literals, no retired tokens, Controller reads only `locomotion_cap` and `stowed_heavy`, no Sanity, Attention or PERCEPTION_OUT symbol in Suit, HUD reads readings only via `true_value`, `withdraw_store` only inside V7 apply, and non-negative dividends. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
