### Ship Section & Layout Data Model (`section`)
GDD: `design/gdd/ship-section-and-layout-data-model.md` | 43 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-section-001 | Layout registers exactly one kernel state block `layout.state` (class SIM, persist true, persist_scope WORLD, scope SHARED, schema_version 1, `restore_after: []`, one hook `layout.doors`) with callbacks snapshot/restore/default_state/post_restore/read_view. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-section-002 | The Layout data module is scene-tree free and integer-only: a headless test must assert it references no `Node` and no float exists in any Layout value. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-003 | Templates (rooms, tiles, rect table, doors, sockets, hardpoints, slots, anchors) are immutable data loaded in BOOTING from a committed `assets/data/sections/manifest.json` in sorted `template_id` order (never a DirAccess scan), never appear in a snapshot, and every JSON number is int-cast and bound-checked. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-004 | `layout.state` stores instances by reference to templates (bodies table, 13-int section-instance rows, sparse non-FREE socket table, `welds[]`, counters `next_section_id`/`next_body_id`/`next_room_id`); derived caches (broadphase grid, leaf rects, version counters, `opening_leaves`, `graph_ref` maps) are never persisted. | Save/Load | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-005 | Ids (`section_id`, `body_id`, `room_id`) are allocated by Layout from persisted counters in template order, never reused or renumbered; a discarded section remains a tombstone row; `graph_ref_base {node_base, edge_base, comp_base}` is persisted per instance. | Save/Load | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-006 | Snapshot encoding uses dense parallel packed arrays in ascending id order (no int-keyed Dictionary, no Vector2i/Rect2/Resource), 64-bit values (`welded_at_T`, `cut_at_T`) as two 32-bit halves, and `snapshot()` returns deep copies. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-007 | `restore()` swaps whole tables from scratch only on success, rejects unsupported stored_version, unknown `template_id`, structural template change, or geometry_hash change on an instance with history; `rot_q != 0` is rejected (`LAYOUT_ROT_UNSUPPORTED`). | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-008 | Static queries (room_at, V, exposed_tiles, section_offset, body_of, socket_plan, static rects) are valid immediately after `restore()`; leaf-dependent queries are valid only after Layout's `post_restore()` (derive_doors pass) and report template-default closed leaves with a `stale_leaf` diagnostic before it. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-009 | `post_restore()` performs cross-block verification only (graph_ref_base resolves, def_ids resolve, weld seam_ids resolve in Graph) and rebuilds `opening_leaves`, bumping version/leaf_version for sections with open leaves. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-010 | Geometry is expressed as half-open integer rects `[x0,x1)x[y0,y1)` in base px with a flags byte (SOLID, OCCLUDES_LIGHT, DOOR_LEAF, PROP, MACHINE, HULL) on a 24-px grid (TILE_PX 48, WALL_FOOTPRINT_DEPTH 24); px-to-tile conversion uses floor division, and transforms are integer translation only (no rotation, mirror or zoom value). | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-011 | One rect set serves collision (Body `solid_in`), light occlusion (Emitter/Lighting `rects_in`) and drawing, with no "perceived" or phantom variant of any query; the avatar has no engine physics body and no engine tile collision or navigation mesh is created. | Physics | ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-012 | `layout.doors` hook (divisor 1, `after: []`) runs in P3, re-derives each door/hatch leaf rect from the Graph's `opening(edge_id) -> {area, area_max, door_state}` with `leaf_w = width_px - floor(width_px*area/area_max)`, and bumps leaf_version/version once per changed section; `body.integrate` is `after: [controller.step, layout.doors]`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-013 | Layout never stores, mirrors or writes `area` or `door_state`; every template-door/hatch `area` write must occur in P2 via command (never M13 from a P3 hook), and the door leaf sprite extent must equal the leaf rect (no tween). | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-014 | `leaf_would_overlap(opening_ref=Graph edge_id, area, foot_boxes[])` and `leaf_obstruction` are pure queries evaluating every leaf of the opening (both sections of a mated hatch); foot boxes are caller-supplied arguments and Layout reads nothing from `body.state`. | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-015 | Hot-path `rects_in(aabb, out)` / `solid_in(body_id, aabb, out)` fill a caller-owned QueryBuffer (PackedInt64Array keys `section_id*RECT_KEY_STRIDE+idx`, PackedInt32Array occupied spans `(x0,y0,x1-1,y1-1)`, PackedByteArray flags) in ascending key order and allocate nothing in steady state (0 bytes growth over 9,000 calls). | Performance | ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-016 | A per-body derived uniform-grid broadphase (BROADPHASE_CELL_PX 48, never persisted, rebuilt at build/attach/detach/restore) answers `solid_in` at P99 <= 0.05 ms per actor with a 2,000-rect body (aggregate 8 actors <= 0.6 ms; Emitter 288x288 query <= 0.3 ms) on RM-DESKTOP/RM-DECK release export. | Performance | ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-017 | Per-section `version`, `static_version`, `leaf_version` are monotone, non-persisted, reset to 1 on restore, and bumped for every section whose rect set can change (both sections of a weld/cut including the unmoved host, and every moved section); `changed_region(section_id) -> ALL \| REGION \| FRAME` is valid only for the current tick. | Core | ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-018 | Sockets are pre-declared attachment sites with states FREE -> WELDED(seam_id) -> CAPPED or CUT_OPEN, which never return to FREE in MVP; sockets change state only inside LM2/LM3 (no `install_cap_plate` or `set_socket_state` mutator). | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-019 | `can_attach` applies a fixed first-failure-wins reason order (SOCKET_UNKNOWN, SOCKET_NOT_FREE, SAME_BODY, FACING_MISMATCH, WIDTH_MISMATCH, CLASS_MISMATCH, LANE_MISMATCH, GEOM_OVERLAP) with GEOM_OVERLAP tested over static footprints and tile cells, memoised on (socket_a, socket_b, static_version_a, static_version_b), <= 2 ms uncached. | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-020 | Layout has exactly four Class A (validate-then-apply, infallible apply) mutators: LM1 `build_section`, LM2 `attach_section`, LM3 `detach_section`, LM4 `discard_section_geometry`; no Class B mutator; each has a pure `can_*` validate; LM2/LM3 run inside Assembly's single atomic apply after the Graph's `weld_join`/`cut_detach`. | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-021 | Weld offsets are `offset_b = offset_a + pos_a - pos_b` on the survivor body (lower `body_id`), argument-order independent, with `welds[]` canonicalised so reversed calls produce a byte-identical block; every built section starts on its own body so the weld relation is a forest and a cut splits exactly two components; detach allocates the new `body_id` from Layout's counter and re-bases to the min-id section. | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-022 | Dirty tracking is explicit inside each mutator (not via packed-array property setters, due to the Godot 4.7 packed-array element-write change); dirty keys are ints `(KIND<<32) \| id` (SEC 1, BODY 2, SEAM 3), batched to the PROJECTOR subscriber, collapsing to ALL past MAX_DIRTY_KEYS 256. | Core | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-023 | World construction runs only at `init_world` inside one WriteScope in the fixed order Layout `build_section` -> Emitter E1 `instantiate_section_emitters` -> Interaction `sync_section` -> PROJECTOR notification; Layout calls no Emitter or Interaction mutator; MVP world is four sections on four bodies (453 tiles). | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-024 | The PROJECTOR is a separate subscriber that builds engine-side derived objects (TileMapLayer/sprites) from `layout.state`, never writes blocks, never creates Light2D/LightOccluder2D, builds 453 tiles at BUILD_TILES_PER_FRAME 256 in <= 3 frames, and assigns each sprite a Layout-namespaced int64 `entity_id = (LAYOUT_ID_NS<<56) \| (section_id<<24) \| (kind_ordinal<<16) \| template_local_idx` for the Pipeline Y-sort key. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-025 | The PROJECTOR scene shape (no-reparent option B vs per-body reparent option A) must be decided by spike SP-YSORT-1 and recorded in ADR-LAY-03 before the PROJECTOR story starts; weld frame costs at most one frame over the P99 ceiling (16.6 ms desktop, 33.3 ms Deck). | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-026 | Determinism: `layout_hash` is the kernel `block_hash` of `layout.state` (derived caches and door state excluded), all iteration is over dense arrays in ascending id order, Layout draws no RNG, and `welded_at_T` is the only stored time value (seam age = `T - welded_at_T >= SEAM_OLD_AFTER_TICKS` 36,000, derived). | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-027 | Layout owns room identity, tile sets, `V`, `exposed_tiles`; both `V` and `exposed_tiles` never change while a room exists; the Graph caches them as derived fields rebuilt in its `post_restore` (`graph.state restore_after: [layout.state]`). | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-028 | Layout owns no loose-item table (Interaction's `interaction.state` holds it), no `visited` bit (WORLD-scoped block rolls back), no pose/velocity, no atmosphere; item positions are `(section_id, section-local px)`. | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-029 | A structural lint (ERROR/WARN/INFO ids such as RECT_OVERLAP, GRID_24, ROOM_SHAPE, V_RANGE, CONTAINMENT_LEAK, HATCH_ALIGN, HARDPOINT_NO_COMPATIBLE, CAPACITY_*) runs at template load and rejects out-of-range or malformed templates; content lint is Derelict Authoring's. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-030 | Capacity ceilings are enforced by `can_build`/`can_detach` (MAX_FOOTPRINT_RECTS_PER_BODY 2000, MAX_DOORS_LOADED 48, MAX_DOORS_PER_SECTION 24, MAX_SECTIONS/BODIES, `next_section_id <= 2^20-1`); snapshot size estimate raises a one-shot diagnostic at LAYOUT_BLOCK_SOFT_BYTES 65,536 and stays under MAX_BLOCK_BYTES 32 MiB. | Performance | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-031 | `layout_hash()` is memoised and recomputed only after LM1-LM4 or restore; a tick containing only door changes performs no `layout.state` encode or `block_hash` call, and `geometry_hash` and `visual_hash` are computed once per template load. | Performance | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-032 | Template `geometry_hash` and `visual_hash` persisted through `PackedInt32Array` are compared after `& 0xFFFFFFFF` normalisation so values at or above 2^31 never spuriously fail restore; `mutation_count` is never persisted, and the only hash is the kernel H64 through `HashingContext` (a lint bans the built-in `hash()` and hand-rolled byte-accumulator loops). | Save/Load | ADR-0005 + ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-033 | Every Layout-owned tuning knob is loaded from the default-config file with no literal in module source; each is validated at its range minimum and maximum, and an out-of-range value is rejected loudly with the knob's name and never clamped. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-034 | When `attach_section` applies in P2, the new hatch LEAF cache is seeded closed, so reads between the P2 apply and the P3 `layout.doors` hook see a solid span, raise no `stale_leaf`, and do not bump `leaf_version` unless the P3 area read differs. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0013 + ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-035 | When `layout.doors` reads an unknown `opening(edge_id)`, the leaf stays closed at the template default, `area` is never invented, `leaf_version` is not bumped, and `opening_unknown` is raised once per edge; an `area` above `area_max` is clamped with a diagnostic, and the same fault found in `post_restore` is `LAYOUT_RESTORE_DANGLING`. | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-036 | A failing `can_*` changes no state, marks no dirty key and consumes no id; reason sets are closed per validator (including `CAPACITY` for build and detach), surface as `VALIDATION_FAILED(layout.<REASON>)`, never include door-close tokens, and a fault inside an apply sends the kernel to `FAULTED`. | Core | ADR-0003 + ADR-0021 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-section-037 | `next_body_id` and `next_room_id` are capped at 2^31-1 (the int32 limit of their packed columns), `can_build` and `can_detach` return `CAPACITY` before consuming an id or calling the Graph, and no counter ever wraps. | Save/Load | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-038 | LM2 returns `{surviving_body_id, absorbed_body_id, moved_sections[], frame_shift_px}` and LM3's record carries `rects[]` equal to `detach_preview(seam_id).rects[]`, so Body rebases from the record; `detach_section` places the cap plate without reading any actor or foot-box state, and an actor-in-cap rejection belongs to Body and Assembly. | Core | ADR-0015 + ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-039 | LY-28 read views return records with fixed field names and deterministic ordering: `anchor_geometry` and `room_surface` match the Failure and Trail contract, `door_passage` equals the D.5 table, `room_adjacency` is ordered `(room_a, via_id)`, `wall_face_rows` is at most 16, and every read is idempotent and identical after restore. | Data | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-040 | The BOOTING template loader and the offline authoring tool run one lint implementation with an identical set of lint ids and severities, matching the single authoritative LY-30 table; any ERROR rejects the whole template and aborts boot. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-041 | In a Godot 4.7.2 release export the manifest-listed template and world JSON paths are included by export filters, load in sorted `template_id` order, and yield `geometry_hash` and `visual_hash` equal to the editor run; no directory scan is used. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-042 | `layout.doors` with MAX_DOORS_LOADED (48) animating doors costs at most 0.2 ms on a release export (5% of STEP_BUDGET_MS 4), bumps `leaf_version` at most once per section per tick, and never bumps `static_version`. | Performance | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-section-043 | Engine behaviours Layout depends on are probed by spike SP-ENG-1 on a 4.7.2 release export before dependent ADRs are accepted: JSON number types, packed-array element-write setter semantics, `var_to_bytes` determinism, negative integer `/` and `%`, SHA-256 and `>>` behaviour, and `PackedInt32Array` round-trips of values at or above 2^31. | Core | ADR-0010 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Ship Body & Interior Physics (`shipbody`)
GDD: `design/gdd/ship-body-and-interior-physics.md` | 50 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-shipbody-001 | No Godot physics server or physics node (RigidBody2D, CharacterBody2D, Area2D, PhysicsServer2D, move_and_slide, test_move, engine tile collision, nav mesh) is used on any simulation path; a lint over the whole Body and Layout directories finds zero matches. | Physics | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-002 | All Body-frame state (actor position, foot box, sweep) is integer-only; the Body and Layout directories promote GDScript typing warnings (UNTYPED_DECLARATION, INFERRED_DECLARATION, UNSAFE_*, NARROWING_CONVERSION, INTEGER_DIVISION, INFERENCE_ON_VARIANT) to errors, with exactly one annotated integer `/` site inside `trunc_div`. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-003 | Each welded set is exactly one body keyed by Layout's `body_id`; Body stores only pose (`world_x`, `world_y`), velocity (`vel_x`, `vel_y`), reserved `heading_q` (always 0) and actor rows, and never allocates, retires or edits `body_id`, membership, offsets, rectangles, sockets or hatch states. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-004 | Nothing keyed on a body-frame coordinate reads `world_*` or `vel_*`; a body with non-zero `vel` run 600 ticks yields a byte-identical `canonical_encode(payload["actors"])` to a body at rest. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-005 | `body.state` is registered as one SHARED SIM block: persist true, scope WORLD/SHARED, schema_version 1, supported_versions [1], `restore_after: [layout.state]`, exactly one hook (`body.integrate`) and exactly one PROJECTOR subscriber (`body.projector`). | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-shipbody-006 | The persisted schema v1 is per-actor `{actor_id, kind, seat, body_id, pos_curr, sub, last_octant, odometer}`, sparse per-body `{body_id, world_x, world_y, vel_x, vel_y, heading_q}`, and scalar `next_actor_id`; `pos_prev`, `blocked` and all diagnostics are derived and never persisted or hashed. | Save/Load | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-007 | `odometer` is a 64-bit integer carried in the snapshot as an exact integer (2^53+7 round-trips) and is never stored in a Vector2i or as a JSON number. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-008 | `snapshot()` returns a plain Dictionary with exactly five keys (`next_actor_id`, `n_bodies`, `n_actors`, `bodies` n*6 ints, `actors` n*10 ints; plain Arrays, rows sorted by id, no floats, no `pos_prev`), and canonical bytes are the kernel's D.12 TLV encoding of it; Body ships no byte writer and never uses `var_to_bytes` for hashed bytes. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-009 | `restore(data, version)` validates in order (type, count, per-row fields, order and canonicity, Layout liveness), rejects the whole block with a named BDY_RESTORE_* reason on the first failure leaving live state untouched, never clamps, and `snapshot(restore(snapshot()))` is byte-identical for `body.state`. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-010 | `post_restore` sets `pos_prev := pos_curr`, preserves `sub`, runs the trapped-actor probe, and does not rebuild the kernel seat binding; a foot box overlapping a SOLID rectangle after restore is counted, never a load failure. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-011 | `body.integrate` runs at divisor 1, offset 0, `after: [controller.step, layout.doors]` (partial order), and `emitter.place` runs after it; every hook id named must be registered by its owner or boot fails. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-shipbody-012 | Within `body.integrate`, bodies integrate pose first in ascending `body_id` (`world += vel`, with `world_prev := world` set beforehand), then actors step in ascending `actor_id`; a stationary actor issues no Layout query. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-013 | The hook is total: data defects (orphan body, invalid octant, v_eff outside 0..3, unbound SEAT, world+vel beyond +/-WORLD_COORD_LIMIT) are absorbed deterministically and counted (BDY_ORPHAN_ACTOR, BDY_OCTANT_INVALID, BDY_VEFF_INVALID, BDY_SEAT_UNBOUND, BDY_WORLD_LIMIT) and never FAULT. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-014 | The hook is allocation-free per tick: id-sorted struct-of-arrays (PackedInt32Array / PackedInt64Array), a caller-owned scratch record, and `solid_in_into` filling a preallocated PackedInt32Array; `step_into` and the value-returning `step` return identical results. | Performance | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-015 | `LocomotionStep` is a pure scene-tree-free RefCounted function `step(state, intent, collide)` usable headlessly and by Horror Entities; it uses `trunc` for the sub-pixel residue (1/256 fixed point, UNIT 256/181) and FLOOR division only for px-to-tile conversion. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-016 | Body reads only `octant` and `v_eff` from `controller.state[seat]` and derives no gait, speed, facing or loco_state; the sub-pixel residue resets only when the move octant differs from Body's own persisted `last_octant`. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-017 | The foot box is `[pos.x-8, pos.x+8) x [pos.y, pos.y+8)` (FOOT_BOX_W 16, FOOT_BOX_H 8, half-open, origin at top-centre), and collision is against Layout's `solid_in(body_id, aabb)` rectangles exactly (24 px wall footprint), with no inflated margin and no second rectangle set. | Physics | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-018 | One `solid_in` query per moving actor per tick (swept box at most 19x11 cells) is swept analytically; the analytic clamp is the shipped baseline and the literal 1-px stepper is the test oracle, proven result-identical by property test. | Physics | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-019 | An actor whose foot box starts inside SOLID rectangles may only take sub-steps that do not increase per-rectangle overlap and whose destination foot origin lies in a section of its own body; the body is never pushed, shoved, snapped or displaced, and actors do not collide with one another. | Physics | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-020 | Every mutator BM1-BM7 is Class A, called only from Assembly's or Transit's single `apply` after the Layout mutator, check-then-write (a faulting call writes nothing), returns OK or a negative FAULT_CODE (BM1 returns actor_id >= 1 or a negative code), uses no `assert()`, and reports defects through an injectable `plan_mismatch_sink` counting BDY_PLAN_MISMATCH. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-021 | Each mutator has a pure `can_*` precondition read (`can_place`, `can_remove`, `can_rebase_attach`, `can_rebase_detach(old_body_id, frame_shift_px)`, `can_transfer`, `can_set_velocity`, `can_set_pose`) that the caller's `validate` invokes before any mutator runs. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-022 | Dirty keys use Layout's encoding `(KIND << 32) \| id` (BODY 2, ACT 4) and every mutator marks dirty keys explicitly, because 4.7 packed-array element writes no longer call the property setter; per-block per-tick keys stay under MAX_DIRTY_KEYS 256. | Core | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-023 | BM3 (weld) shifts only actors on the absorbed body by `+frame_shift_px` (frame_shift_px on Layout's 24-px grid), keeps `sub` and `last_octant`, deletes the absorbed pose row, and for every moved actor `(actor_section, actor_local_px)` is unchanged before and after. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-024 | BM4 (cut) assigns actors by the section containing their foot origin evaluated against reconstructed pre-cut geometry (`offset_pre = section_offset + frame_shift_px` for moved sections), shifts `C_new` actors by `-frame_shift_px` with `sub` zeroed, creates the new pose row, and refuses with `-BDY_REBASE_ACTOR_IN_CAP` if `actors_overlapping(old_body_id, cap_rects)` is non-empty; an out-of-range new pose row saturates and returns OK. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-025 | Weld legality takes no pose input (Layout `can_attach` plus Graph `can_join` only); `docked(a, b, frame_shift_px)` is a pure read that gates nothing inside Body, and a PLAYER-origin weld is offered only when `docked` holds (Assembly and Transit must repeat this). | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-026 | World pose and velocity are integers in whole base px (` \| world \| <= 2^22`, ` \| vel \| <= 16` per tick, `heading_q` must be 0); lowering either bound or MAX_ACTORS after saves exist requires a schema_version bump with migration, and `DETACH_DRIFT_PX_PER_TICK` is locked at 0 (non-zero fails a boot lint). | Data | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-027 | Ship motion applies no force, push, tilt, knockback or drift to any actor; docking and separation are published only as P5 events `BODY_DOCK_CONTACT`, `BODY_HULL_SEPARATION` and `BODY_TRANSFER{weld \| detach \| pod}` (payload `{tick, actor_id, seat, old_body, new_body, frame_delta_px}`) carrying no simulation state. | Core | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-028 | `actor_section(actor_id)` equals Layout's `section_at(body_of(actor), pos_curr)`, derived on demand and never stored or cached across a mutator; `actor_local_px = pos_curr - section_offset`; a foot origin in no section is a defect-only state counted as BDY_TRANSFER_NO_SECTION. | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-029 | `body.state` is SIM and never PERCEPTION_OUT; its read view (`pos_curr`, `pos_prev`, `body_id`, `kind`, `seat`, `foot_box`, `bodies[*].{world, vel, world_prev}`) may be read by PRESENTATION subscribers, with no per-player variant, and `blocked` is readable only by PRESENTATION and DIAGNOSTIC subscribers (never by a SIM hook; lint). | Core | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-030 | Body publishes `seat_bindings() -> [(seat, actor_id)]` (ascending seat, SEAT rows only, derived per call) as a binding source registered at BOOTING; the kernel's LOADING step (CR-7, KRN-BIND-01) rebuilds PlayerId bindings from it, and AC-BDY-6 / AC-BDY-138 are Body's epic closure gate against the real kernel and Save/Load. | Core | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-031 | Determinism: no RNG, wall clock, float state or unordered-container iteration in the block or hook, and Body is correct for 1..MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME (4) `body.integrate` runs per rendered frame. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-032 | `body.projector` is the only Body script that may name Node2D/Camera2D/Viewport; it owns one per-body root Node2D (translation only, rotation 0), one `y_sort_enabled` actor/props layer per body root, the loaded body's `Camera2D.limit_*`, and reparents Presenter nodes on `BODY_TRANSFER` via `attach_presenter(actor_id, node)` without using global-transform-preserving `Node.reparent`. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-033 | The canvas offset of a non-avatar body is one floored integer per body per frame, `floor(off_prev + (off_curr - off_prev) * tick_alpha)` with both offsets taken relative to the current avatar body, and `physics_interpolation` is off for all presentation (`tick_alpha` is the one interpolator). | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-034 | Hook cost is budgeted as a whole (pose + all actors + commit + diagnostics), pending spike S1 on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK in a release export: P99 <= 90% of the frame ceiling, per-actor sweep logged above SWEEP_COST_LOG_MS 0.05 ms, 8 actors cited at 0.4 ms of the 2 ms P3 target. | Performance | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-035 | Body owns the diagnostics `PC_FOOTPRINT_OVERLAP`, `BDY_TRAPPED_ACTOR`, `BDY_REBASE_ACTOR_IN_CAP` and the other BDY_* defect counters, which live in the hook's diagnostics channel (never in the block or hashed set) and must read 0 at the end of every clean-build CI scenario. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-036 | Layout's `solid_in(body_id, aabb)` must return unique rectangles in ascending key order, and the sweep must tolerate empty, duplicate or unordered results with identical output plus a debug-only `diag.solid_in_unordered` signal; the rectangle set read in `body.integrate` is the post-cut, post-leaf set of that tick. | Physics | ADR-0014 + ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-037 | The coupling `BROADPHASE_CELL_PX >= FOOT_BOX_W + 3` is a boot-checked constraint so that a swept foot box touches at most 4 broadphase cells (2 per axis); a configuration violating it must fail the bound test. | Data | ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-038 | Integer-semantics assumptions (`posmod`, GDScript integer-division truncation, float32 exactness of canvas offsets to 2^23, the `Performance.OBJECT_COUNT` monitor, directory-scoped warning levels) are unverified on 4.7.2 and gated by spike S2; a failure replaces the expression with a sign-split form or lowers `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT`. | Core | ADR-0010 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-039 | Hatch-cap safety is enforced by Assembly: a player-origin cut is rejected and a system-origin cut deferred while `actors_overlapping(cap_rects)` is non-empty, with no cut path bypassing that check; a forced direct detach followed by a refused BM4 ends the kernel in FAULTED with the actor unmoved. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-040 | After every mutator call, including each that returned a FAULT_CODE or saturated, the block is consistent: `restore(snapshot())` accepts it, actor ids are unique and below `next_actor_id`, and every `body_id` in either table is live in Layout. | Save/Load | ADR-0015 + ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-041 | `body.state` is excluded from Save/Load's `REBASE_BLOCKS` registry because its round trip is idempotent. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-042 | A SIM hook may read Body's `moved` or `pos_prev` only if registered `after: [body.integrate]`; a lint rejects any other SIM reader, so the Controller's `controller.noise` sees the same tick's `moved`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-shipbody-043 | Neighbour read contracts: Suit calls Layout `room_at(section_id, actor_local_px)` with section-local pixels and treats a NONE or plate-cell result as exterior exposure; Interaction reads `foot_boxes(body_id)` unfiltered and ascending including unbound seats; Emitter and Lighting anchor on floored `pos_curr`. | Core | ADR-0015 + ADR-0027 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-044 | Body state is independent of render zoom: identical streams at different ladder steps give identical `body.state` bytes, and `zoom`, `k_auto` and `field_h` appear only in the Presenter or projector directory. | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-045 | The projector's weld frame completes within a working threshold of 8 ms P99 over 100 repeated welds on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release exports, with no frame in the window exceeding the machine ceiling (16.6 ms and 33.3 ms) and no orphaned body root nodes. | Performance | ADR-0034 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-046 | CI performance gates for the hook are deterministic counts from instrumented `collide` and `section_at` wrappers (at most one `solid_in` query per moving actor, bounded rectangle tests, bounded `section_at` calls); wall-clock P99 is a nightly benchmark failing at +25% over the S1 baseline per machine. | Performance | ADR-0015 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-047 | In MIRROR mode, host snapshots carry `pos_curr`, `body_id`, `seat`, `kind`, `odometer`, `last_octant` and `sub` but not `blocked`, `moved`, `actor_section` or diagnostics, and `body.integrate` does not run on MIRROR (deferred to Vertical Slice). | Networking | ADR-0032 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-048 | Transfers (BM3, BM4, BM5) change only `body_id`, `pos_curr`, `pos_prev` and `sub` of an actor row, and leave `controller.state` bytes (carry_ref, DEAD state) identical; a same-tick Controller write after a pod hop must not displace the arrival cell. | Core | ADR-0015 + ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-049 | The Pipeline treats `BODY_TRANSFER{weld \| detach}` carrying `frame_delta_px` as a camera rebase (shifting follow state and history), not a cut, so the avatar's screen delta on a weld or cut tick is 0 px unless new-body camera limits clamp, in which case it is a cut, never a pan. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-shipbody-050 | Restore-time motion continuity requires the Controller Adapter's input resync to land in P2 of the first post-restore tick; a later resync resets `sub` once (at most 255/256 px loss), and restore with no key held resets `sub` as a stop does. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Modular Ship Assembly (`assembly`)
GDD: `design/gdd/modular-ship-assembly.md` | 48 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-assembly-001 | Assembly registers exactly five command types (`begin_weld`, `begin_seam_cut`, `weld`, `seam_cut`, `install_module`) and no others (`remove_module` stays unregistered). | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-002 | `weld` and `seam_cut` are SYSTEM-origin only (allowed_origins = SYSTEM) with payload exactly `{actor, target, cause}` (no `q`, plan or mode), and their `max_age_ticks` equals `PENDING_TTL_TICKS` (8-119, default 12). | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-003 | A weld/cut is two-phase: `begin_*` opens an Interaction channel and mutates no ship state; `assembly.step` hands off on DONE and submits the completion; the completion applies in P2 of the next tick (e.g. begin 437039 -> apply 437400 for D=360). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-004 | The same predicate body and reason order is evaluated at three sites (begin validate, handoff pre-check, completion validate), and the completion `validate` is the only authoritative site; declared skips are only those in T2a (e.g. `BUSY`, `NOT_DOCKED`, `CUT_STRANDS`). | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-005 | Every completion `apply` is atomic and no-rollback: it calls published mutators in fixed order (weld: Graph M2 -> Layout LM2 -> Body BM3 -> Interaction `sync_section` x2 -> `assembly.state`; cut: M3 -> LM3 -> BM4 -> `sync_section` x2 -> state), emits one P5 notification batch, and Body mutators never precede their Layout mutator (`BDY_PLAN_MISMATCH`). | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-006 | Assembly writes exactly one block (`assembly.state`); every other state change goes through a mutator published by the owning system (Graph, Layout, Body, Suit, Controller, Interaction). | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-007 | Every Class A mutator Assembly calls returns a status (`OK` or `FAULT_CODE`); `apply` checks it after every call, calls `ctx.fault(code, detail)` and returns `ctx.complete()` on first non-OK, and never relies on throw/assert (GDScript has none; assert is stripped in release). | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-008 | All kernel-invoked Assembly callables (`validate`, `apply`, `assembly.step`, `snapshot`, `restore`, `post_restore`, `default_state`, `read_view`) take the kernel `ctx` and have `return ctx.complete()` as their only return statement. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-assembly-009 | A read-only cross-block verifier checks INV-1..INV-7 and INV-10: all eight hard in `post_restore` (restore fails to FAULTED on violation; INV-6 `BODIES_LIVE_MAX` half warn-only), and INV-1/2/4/5/7 after each weld/cut apply in release builds. | Core | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-010 | `assembly.step` is a hook (divisor 1, offset 0, after `interaction.step`) that reads only `assembly.state` and `channel_view`, iterates seats in ascending PlayerId, submits at most one SYSTEM command per seat per tick, and uses no wall-clock, RNG or dictionary-order dependence. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-011 | `assembly.state` is a SIM block (`persist true`, scope WORLD, SHARED keyed by PlayerId, `SHARED_SUMMARY`, schema_version 1, `restore_after [layout.state, graph.state, interaction.state, body.state]`) holding per-seat `pending` and `last_result` plus the reserved CASCADE deferral record, and no channel progress or world history. | Save/Load | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-assembly-012 | `snapshot()` writes every seat's `pending` as NONE (restore-idempotent, byte-identical re-snapshot) and `last_result` is runtime-only (outside snapshot, hashes and Save/Load payload); a save taken in the `pending` window (up to ~13 ticks) loses the in-flight completion, which is never replayed. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-013 | 64-bit tick values (`T`, `submit_T`, `welded_at_T`) are stored as two 32-bit ints or strings and never as JSON numbers; the block uses no packed-array fields and dirty keys are marked explicitly in every mutator (Godot 4.7 packed-array element setter change). | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-014 | Assembly publishes the pure read `last_shape_change_T()` (max of Layout `welds[].welded_at_T/cut_at_T`) for Save/Load to poll, event names `ASSEMBLY_WELD` / `ASSEMBLY_CUT`, and never calls Save/Load. | Save/Load | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-015 | Assembly publishes pure read `ragged_breach_open()` (keep-side venting or uncapped ports within `RAGGED_WITHHOLD_MAX_TICKS` 2400 of a RAGGED cut); the `ASSEMBLY_CUT` event autosave is withheld while it is true, and the interval save is soft-gated, bounded by `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` 7200. | Save/Load | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-016 | Weld apply is integer arithmetic only: no RNG and no wall-clock inside any Assembly apply or hook (replay and MIRROR identity). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-017 | Weld/cut channels live in `interaction.state` as IA-14 records with `owner_system = ASSEMBLY`; Assembly runs no clock of its own; durations are `WELD_CHANNEL_TICKS` 360 and `SECTION_CUT_CHANNEL_TICKS` 240 (data-driven, multiples of `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6, range 240-480). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-018 | No PLAYER-origin `validate` (`begin_weld`, `begin_seam_cut`, `install_module`) may read node/edge `hp`, `state`, `forced_mode`, `served_pm`, or call full `can_join` / `check_install_module`; only `can_join_static` is allowed (P4 truth-oracle lint); `weld_offer` additionally calls no `can_join*` or node accessor. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-019 | DEFERRED reasons (`PORT_ABSENT`, `SLOT_OCCUPIED`) are evaluated only in the SYSTEM-side apply (step A0 / `check_install_module`); on failure `apply` writes `BLOCKED`, clears `pending`, mutates nothing else, and is an Applied command, not a fault. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-020 | Weld quality `q = WELD_Q_DEFAULT` (850) is a data constant clamped to [`Q_MIN`, 990] (diagnostic `MSA_Q_CLAMPED`), is never an input and never reaches any player-visible string or UI path. | Data | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-021 | Cut mode (SEALED / RAGGED) is derived at apply from true seam positions via Graph `seam_view` (openings area 0, valves closed, breakers open), never chosen by payload, and passed identically to M3 and LM3. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-022 | `MSA.CUT_BLOCKED` rejects a CHANNEL cut (at begin, handoff, completion) when Body `actors_overlapping` over the seam's two hatch rectangles is non-empty, and a CASCADE cut defers/abandons; the cutter's stance must lie outside the rectangles. | Physics | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-023 | The cut offer exists only when the seat's foot-origin section lies in the component that keeps its `body_id`, computed via Layout `detach_preview`; the detached piece persists as its own body, translates without rotation (`heading_q` 0) and Assembly never calls M4/LM4 (discard). | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-024 | `weld_offer` is a pure, cached-static-half, O(1)-`docked` hot-path read (per-call budget `MSA_WELD_OFFER_BUDGET_US`, 5 us `[UNV]`) gated on Body `docked(a, b, frame_shift_px)`; host/mate selection puts the HOME-kind section first. | Performance | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-025 | Sockets are terminal: FREE -> WELDED -> CAPPED / CUT_OPEN, never back to FREE, changed only inside LM2/LM3; MVP supports exactly one weld-then-cut cycle. | Data | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-026 | Install is a TAP command whose apply order is Graph `check_install_module` -> M5 `install_module(slot_node_id, def_id, hp_start, hp_cap, patched_at_step)` -> Suit `release_carried` -> Controller `clear_carry_ref` -> `sync_section` -> state; it requires the Controller/Interaction carry carve-out for HARDPOINT subjects. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-027 | Assembly pushes nothing to the Attention web (Interaction pushes all TOOL/LOUD noise) and reads no `PERCEPTION_OUT` block (seam geometry, bead, cap plate, `CUT_BLOCKED` are truth channel). | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-028 | Post-channel non-DONE outcomes show one cue per verb family with no reason text; presenter plays a cue only for the local seat, deduped by `(PlayerId, T)` within `OUTCOME_CUE_WINDOW_TICKS` 60, `last_seen` initialised on join/restore. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-029 | Performance budgets (Release, spike-measured): weld apply <= 0.5 ms, cut apply <= 0.5 ms, install apply <= 0.2 ms, `assembly.step` idle <= 0.02 ms, weld-frame P5 subscribers <= 8 ms, no frame > 33 ms; weld/cut assets preloaded and warm-up drawn at scene load; measured on a release export. | Performance | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-030 | The PROJECTOR / Emitter rebuild / Lighting / Trail rebuilds run as P5 subscribers after commit with writes disabled and coalesce to the latest committed state across catch-up ticks (`MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME` 4). | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-031 | Live body count is bounded by construction (`N(t) <= N0`); `body_count <= MAX_BODIES` 8 is hard, `<= BODIES_LIVE_MAX` 4 is warn-only (`MSA_BODIES_LIVE_WARN`); MVP worst case is exactly 4 (zero margin). | Rendering | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-032 | Per-seat state is a map keyed by PlayerId (never transport peer id), iterated ascending; multi-seat contention rules (`BUSY`, second completion -> `NOT_TAKEN`) are `[VS-B]` and out of the MVP gate, but the data shape is built in MVP. | Networking | ADR-0032 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-033 | Each weld, cut and install apply marks explicit dirty keys within a fixed per-block footprint (graph at most 4 keys, body at most 3, assembly at most 1 per changed seat), stays below MAX_DIRTY_KEYS 256 and never collapses any block to ALL. | Core | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-034 | A weld or cut applied in P2 becomes visible to Graph hooks only at the next Graph hook after its topology refresh (propagate.power, atmo or failure by T mod 6), with no observable half-state. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0012 + ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-035 | A weld that pushes the loaded Interaction subject count above MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS 192 counts IA_SUBJECT_OVERFLOW and reports a content lint defect but stays applied and never faults the kernel. | Performance | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-036 | Divergence between the validate verdict and the apply-time re-check (MSA_VERDICT_DRIFT) or between predicted and returned frame_shift_px, moved sections or rects (MSA_SHIFT_DRIFT) is release-fatal and faults the kernel in every build. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-037 | After an Assembly fault, restore returns a world hash-equal to the last written save and writes no save while FAULTED; maximum lost play is AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS + AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS - 1 (17999 ticks). | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-038 | Load has two separate failure layers: a pre-check refuses an out-of-range assembly.state schema_version without touching the kernel, while a block restore() failure sends the kernel LOADING to FAULTED and makes no claim that pre-restore state survives. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-039 | begin_weld and begin_seam_cut apply calls Interaction open_channel once and Controller set_channel_ref(actor, ASSEMBLY, channel_id, face_octant) once, with face_octant derived from the anchor offset, and change no graph, layout or body hash. | Core | ADR-0020 + ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-040 | Weld and cut conserve every actor's integer absolute position, use frame shifts that are multiples of 24 px, and keep the canonical state_hash identical at every tick regardless of camera zoom level k, seat count or MIRROR role. | Core | ADR-0015 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-041 | A generated weld/cut property test runs 1,000 sequences from a committed fixed seed list with the verifier after every operation, asserting zero invariant violations, no id reuse, body count at most N0 and node/edge conservation; it is a gating nightly run. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-042 | Boot verifies the _process-driven TickDriver runs at TICK_RATE 60 with a catch-up bound of MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME 4 and refuses to start otherwise, because every Assembly duration assumes it. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-assembly-043 | A toggle-hold input setting lets one interact press run a weld or cut channel to DONE with identical duration, noise and abort set to the held form; a second press or movement cancels with the same cause as releasing the key. | Input | ADR-0019 + ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-044 | A single headless lint harness (tools/lint/msa_lint.gd with allow-list files) runs in CI before the gdUnit4 suite, emits one LINT-MSA-n line per finding, exits non-zero on any finding, and covers source and data under assets/data/. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-045 | The fresh weld bead, jambs and leaf appear in the first rendered frame whose committed tick is at least the apply tick with zero frames of delay, and no bead is drawn while welds[] has no row. | Rendering | ADR-0009 + ADR-0034 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-046 | The Perception Filter's wrapped-sprite registry must never contain the seam's own valve, breaker or lane sprite ids; those sprites render truthfully on every client even under a class (b) lie. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-047 | The release verifier subset plus status checks costs at most 0.05 ms p99 at MAX_SECTIONS 24 and MAX_ACTORS 8, and apply-budget thresholds are re-derived from the S0 GDScript micro-benchmark and frozen before the gating run is judged. | Performance | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-assembly-048 | Every precondition an apply step relies on is proved by a named read in validate or listed as total by contract; violating exactly one precondition rejects in validate with zero mutator calls. | Core | ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Derelict Authoring (Handmade Kits) (`derelict`)
GDD: `design/gdd/derelict-authoring-handmade-kits.md` | 41 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-derelict-001 | The system registers no state block, step hook, mutator or read_view and holds no runtime state; it ships only authored data files, a kit manifest and a lint. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-002 | Four template JSON files (`home_ship`, `derelict_a`, `derelict_b`, `pod`) live in `assets/data/sections/` and `assets/data/world/mvp_world.json` lists `{template_id, kind, world_slot}` for each. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-003 | Authoring adds only the content fields `def_id`, `hp`, `fail_since_step`, `forced_mode`, `switch_on`, `residual`, `count`, `condition_bp`, `visible_unknown`, `socket_tag` plus initial values of fields the Graph already persists; no new schema. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-004 | Every authored number is an integer (dJ, mGU, mL, mC, bp); the loader casts and bound-checks JSON floats and the lint rejects floats (`AUTHORED_NON_INTEGER`). | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-005 | World build order is `build_section` per template, Graph M1 `instantiate_section`, Emitter E1 `instantiate_section_emitters`, then Interaction `place_loose` and `sync_section`, with zero rejections. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-006 | Two world builds from the same file bytes and `world_seed` produce identical `state_hash`. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-007 | Each authored initial value diffs against `default_state()` exactly as listed in DA-35, and changing one value by 1 changes `template_hash` and `state_hash`. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-008 | A template edit changes `template_hash`; templates are never mutated at runtime (saves are restored from saved values, not re-templated, and no Authoring lint runs at restore). | Save/Load | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-009 | Negative authored `fail_since_step` (e.g. -14400) round-trips through save/load exactly; `since_steps` is recomputed from `step_no` so dried trails stay dried. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-010 | A derelict section not loaded is dormant: state frozen, no fast-forward, `can_join` returns `SECTION_NOT_LOADED`; its hp/stocks are identical after a 60-minute unload. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-011 | Endogenous hp loss on a loaded DERELICT section runs at rate_pm 2000 (`DERELICT_FAULT_COMPRESSION` 500); dead faults emit `intensity_pm` 0 until power/coolant reaches them. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-012 | Exactly eight live faults exist (4 home, 2 A, 2 B), each within +-15 % of Graph D.15 at trigger, with `MAX_FAULT_GLOW` 10 respected. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-013 | Dead faults are authored at hp <= `HP_FAIL` with back-dated `fail_since_step` and `forced_mode` so they present as dried trails; BOOT rule `DEAD_FAULT_NOT_DRIED` refuses otherwise. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-014 | The MVP is survivable with zero parts: a patch-only run of all eight faults ends none FAILED/DESTROYED (headless test `G0_ZERO_PART_SURVIVABLE`). | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-015 | The part ledger is finite and authored: 4 loose parts, 800 permille capped aggregate; salvage is the only faucet, via the authored node list and loose placements. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-016 | Loose placements `{def_id, count, condition_bp}` are expanded via Item DB `fresh_instance` and placed at Layout `loose_slots` through `place_loose`; an unknown `def_id` fails lint/BOOT (`PLACEMENT_DEF_UNKNOWN`), not runtime. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-017 | Exactly one BEACON (`bcn_main`, dedicated LOAD node, home `spine_corridor`) and one EMERGENCY self-powered lamp (`fx_cryo_lamp`, node_ref 0) exist; Attention only submits `set_beacon` and never modulates the EMERGENCY or residual lamps. | Rendering | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-018 | Authored emitters per reachable loaded set stay within `MAX_EMITTERS` 96, lanterns within `LANTERN_LIGHTS_MAX` 8, fixtures within cap 28, fixture counts exact per room (`FIXTURE_COUNT_ROOM`, `FIXTURE_COUNT_WORLD`). | Rendering | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-019 | Subject budgets are linted per reachable loaded set (S0-S4, S4 = home+B plus A, 145 interaction subjects, 175 total vs Emitter cap 192); Interaction must register subjects by adjacency/proximity (ruling D37) and a dormant section must unregister its subjects. | Performance | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-020 | Tile volume per reachable set stays within `TILES_WORLD_MVP` 600 (S4 449; pod adds 4) and authored nodes at most 120 (104 authored). | Performance | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-021 | Simulation at S4 with all eight faults stays at P99 <= `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 ms per tick on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (frame P99 16.6 / 33.3 ms, within 90 % of ceiling). | Performance | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-derelict-022 | World build (init_world stub, three build_section, M1, E1, place_loose) completes in <= 2000 ms; BOOT lint subset <= 100 ms; full CONTENT lint <= 60 s; each template file <= 256 KB (placeholders). | Performance | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-023 | At BOOT only six rules are re-checked (`PLACEMENT_DEF_UNKNOWN`, `SELF_POWERED_LAMP_COUNT`, `BEACON_COUNT`, `STORY_SLOT_LIST`, `RESIDUAL_LIGHT_COUNT`, `DEAD_FAULT_NOT_DRIED`); a violation refuses the template and names the record; CI-only violations never block boot. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-024 | The content lint is a pure, deterministic function of the files plus `content_lint_config` (rows `{name,value,owner,status LOCKED/PLACEHOLDER}`); output ordered by `(template_id, lint_id, record_key)`; waivers need a reason; no waiver may name `STORY_SLOT_FILLED_IN_MVP`. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-025 | The 90-row lint table is delivered in tranches T0 (BOOT six), T1 (CI-minimum ERRORs), T2, T3, and `TUNING_SOURCE_DRIFT` compares LOCKED rows to the registry. | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-026 | Content acceptance that needs the Graph runs as headless gdUnit4 integration tests under `tests/integration/derelict_authoring/` via the kernel headless path (H10); systems stay scene-tree free. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-027 | A plan-grammar converter turns `tools/asset-pipeline/plans/<template_id>.txt` into `tile_runs`, wall runs and the rect table deterministically (two runs identical); the converter ADR is a prerequisite of the first template story. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-028 | The kit manifest rows `{piece_id, category, footprint_cell, layer, flags, variant_tokens, layout_record_kind, count_bucket}` name no template, so procgen can reuse them; the projector selects the maintained or `_worn` atlas by `template_id` and no Layout field carries wear. | Rendering | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-029 | Authored data is truth only: no markers, labels, decoy/fake-trail records, entity/combat placements, loot rolls, or flesh/violet palette pixels (hard never-do rules; `PALETTE_SANITY_BAND`). | Core | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-030 | Spawn table (`spawn_cryo_1`, `arrive_home_airlock`, `arrive_a_dock`, `arrive_b_dock`, `seat_pod_1`) is section-local px data that must pass Body `can_place`; `SPAWN_BLOCKED` is an ERROR. | Physics | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-031 | The Godot 4.7.2 export must ship every `assets/data/**/*.json` template through an include filter, and the JSON loader must preserve each authored integer through cast-and-bound-check, as proven by a 30-minute spike against the export templates. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-032 | The headless content acceptance suite (about 50 integration tests) completes in at most 5 minutes in CI and gdUnit4 reports zero orphan nodes and zero leaked RefCounted handles after it. | Performance | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-033 | Weld-lane junction nodes (`j_main`, `j_b_main`) carry a `salvageable: false` flag so salvage never makes `can_join` return PORT_ABSENT, and socket offsets must lie on the 24 px lattice (a 12 px shift fails the lint). | Data | ADR-0013 + ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-034 | Layout must provide CONSOLE anchor records with a `console_role` (POD or DOCK); each dock room holds exactly one dock status glow on its docked-podcraft prop and the world holds exactly four CONSOLE anchors. | Data | ADR-0013 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |

| TR-derelict-036 | In a welded-body headless run, lantern, non-shadow, shadow-slot and fault-glow counts stay within their caps with the glow bound at most 9 and the demotion counter at 0, and contained light colours stay at or below `CONTAIN_COLOURS` 4. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-037 | Authored closed-valve staging relies on the Graph gating leak wear on `intensity_pm` > 0 (hp held constant at 0 intensity, falling only as flowing time), so a change to that Graph rule must fail the dependent tests. | Core | ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-038 | Content lints and tests must call the Emitter integer exposure query (`lit`, `c(d)`) and Body `can_place` headlessly against authored data, with no scene tree, to prove console, arrival and wake-room lighting. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-039 | Each visible-unknown residual lamp supplies an `exterior_anchor_px` and a non-occluding `wall_port` within 48 px so Web Map transit shows exactly one lit cluster there, and the Perception Filter neither adds nor removes it. | Rendering | ADR-0026 + ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-040 | Every reachable loaded set, including one with a pod loaded, holds at most `MAX_BODIES` 8 bodies, with a welded home plus B counted as one body. | Physics | ADR-0013 + ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-derelict-041 | Kit atlas VRAM (about 125 pieces, with `_worn` variants doubling) is measured and reported on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK until a technical-director budget converts it into a pass line. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Boarding & Salvage (`boarding`)
GDD: `design/gdd/boarding-and-salvage.md` | 44 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-boarding-001 | The system registers no state block, step hook, command type, mutator, read view, notification, registry constant or Debug Overlay diagnostics channel; no scene-tree code of its own (verifiable: registry delta is only referenced_by entries). | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-002 | Sortie step order (prepare, board, enter, listen, light, decide, position, cut, cap-or-leave, pick up, carry, hop, install, settle) is composed from existing owners and stores no sortie state; it reuses `loco_state`, Attention `phase` and `transit_state` by reference. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-003 | While carrying, exactly four interactions are admitted: `drop`, `install_module` on a compatible HARDPOINT, `pod_hop` on POD_CONSOLE, and V4 `set_opening` on a door face; all other targets stay prompt-less and `BUSY`. | Input | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-004 | A cut yield is never auto-carried; pickup is a lit TAP moving LOOSE_WORLD to CARRIED via `set_carry_ref` (Controller) and `set_carried` (Suit), one carried instance per seat. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-005 | `carry_ref` and the Suit carried instance are restored together and cross-checked on load (mismatch raises `SUIT_RESTORE_CARRY_MISMATCH`); Interaction `post_restore` never touches `carry_ref`; in-progress channels are dropped with a RESTORE abort. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-006 | A cut is lit-only: every channelled/object-addressed sortie verb obeys `act_gate(o) = in_reach(o) AND lit(o)`; no dark exemption exists for extraction or the console. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-007 | Carry speed is `V_CARRY` (no run) independent of module mass; mass never alters `V_CARRY` or carry noise, and no weight number, pack capacity or encumbrance meter exists (weight only via Suit `cue_load`). | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-008 | A stowed HEAVY item disables run (Controller rule PC-8); roster owned by Item DB. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-009 | The pod hop carries per seat one `carry_ref` plus STOWED stacks; capacity is seat count (one at MVP); one module per hop; `carry_ref` is intact on arrival; no refusal reason may read module-body state. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-010 | `pod_hop` must always be allowed during TELL/BREACH (escape verb); departure from non-home locus ends a BREACH and voids due-run breach when all recorded seats have left. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-011 | `drop` of the carried module is legal anywhere, free, exempt from the lit rule; downed actor drops the module within one tick (Controller clears `carry_ref`, Interaction hook at divisor 1 runs after). | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-012 | Abandoned modules persist in `loose[]` and a left derelict persists exactly as left (dormant, state stands still); salvage is salvage-once and a SALVAGED slot is a permanent tombstone. | Save/Load | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-013 | Hop-availability invariant: no sortie state exists where `pod_hop` is unavailable for want of light alone; docked pod carries a self-powered status glow (Emitter subkind DOCK_STATUS, set via `set_dock_status` E10) that lights POD_CONSOLE; BLOCKING until AC-BS-92 and AC-BS-51b pass. | Rendering | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-014 | Derelict dock rooms must have the status glow placed and linted so the POD_CONSOLE lies inside the glow light shape (build error otherwise). | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-015 | Carry burden `U_BURDEN` Attention intake is pushed per tick only while salvage is held aboard the non-home locus body where it was taken (not IN_POD/DOWNED/DEAD), moving or not; it stops in the pod and does not resume at home. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-016 | A completed module cut (V8 `salvage_node`) must be observable by Save/Load via Interaction's pure derived read `last_salvage_T()` polled under the SL-5 gate and `AUTOSAVE_MIN_GAP_TICKS`; Boarding mints no name or event. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-017 | Autosave is legal mid-carry (CARRY_WALK is not a gate state); channel, stagger and transit defer via `GATE_SOFT_STATES`; DOWNED/DEAD are hard gates; a tell defers via SL-5 soft row (b); CRITICAL vitals is a third soft input. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-018 | Threat draws (tell length) are re-keyed per restore epoch (death restore advances; newest Save & Quit Continue keeps; stored in per-slot `slot_epoch.bin`) while Graph physical failures and persisted counters replay exactly (determinism). | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-019 | Yield is deterministic (`salvage_yield`, no RNG) and `condition_bp` carries through install, so installing a FAILED-hp instance installs a FAILED module; no `remove_module` exists. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-020 | `install_module` prompt exists iff the carried def is in `compatible_defs(h)` of the focused HARDPOINT; chain M5 `install_module`, `release_carried`, `clear_carry_ref`, `sync_section`. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-021 | Cut completion registers one STUB per `open_end` fluid port; uncapped stubs hiss indefinitely with no forcing timer; `begin_cap` is hands-free-only (welder) and ledger-resumable; power-only modules leave no stub. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-022 | Cut is a CHANNEL of `SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` whose effect lands at completion only; abort leaves module in place and keeps the partial ledger for `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`; second seat on same node is `BUSY`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-023 | Price channels (noise, hiss, load breath, pace, time, air) are never altered by hallucination; only the prize may lie (class (b) lies only at MVP; class (d) deferred). | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-024 | No readouts of value/condition/Attention exist: no value tag, rating, condition bar, inventory screen, sortie timer, `listen` verb, or Attention meter (visual or audio). | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-025 | Content must satisfy Derelict Authoring lints across all five loaded sets S0-S4 and both sortie orders: carried round trip within `VACUUM_BUDGET_S` 27 s, hazard crossing at most 7 tiles lethal cold, `ESCAPE_ROUTE_BUDGET_PX` 1800 (600 carrying), 18/22 module nodes A/B, loose ledger of 9 placements. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-026 | Carried modules render as one shared plain shape on the tool layer (max 24x14 px, held overhead, figure plus module at most 71 px); stub/cap/kerf art is hue-less and matte (no flesh/violet palette). | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-027 | All Boarding values are integer tick/AU arithmetic over owners' registered constants (TICK_RATE, V_CARRY, TILE_PX etc.); no wall-clock performance criterion of its own; frame-time pending spike on RM-DESKTOP (P99 16.6 ms) / RM-DECK (P99 33.3 ms). | Performance | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-028 | No combat, progression, economy, construction freedom, forced-script drop/cap, or win-state is implemented (hard never-do list G-1..G-11). | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-029 | Berth is LOUD (one `U_LOUD` push at apply) and unberth QUIET; berth/unberth sit on DOCK_CONSOLE and are BUSY while carrying. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-030 | The simulation must be drivable headlessly by a scripted harness with a Web Map stub implementing pod_hop as a body transfer, and golden schedules (REF_ZERO_DWELL, REF_GOLDEN) must be generated by running the owners' real code and read back from owner constants, never typed or reimplemented in tests. | Core | ADR-0010 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-031 | Every hook, command, mutator, read-view and notification identifier must be published by its owner in a machine-readable list so a script can generate the allow-list the Boarding lints resolve against. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-032 | The partial-cut progress ledger expires on global ticks, not on the dormant derelict body's ticks, so a pod hop away always forfeits a partial once PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS elapses; a hop aborts any open channel with ABORT(BODY). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0026 + ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-033 | Suit vitals, lamp-cell drain and other statuses must keep running at the pod leg's per-run cadence while the seat is IN_POD, and a seat reaching DOWNED during the hop applies the Controller's DOWNED rule (carry_ref cleared, solo restore from the newest autosave). | Core | ADR-0026 + ADR-0027 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-034 | Autosave deferral is capped: a save deferred 7200 ticks is written flagged RISKY, and a death restore within DEATH_LOOP_WINDOW_TICKS of a RISKY generation, or with too little warning since the generation, steps back one generation; an absent Attention view defers the save and logs GATE_INPUT_ABSENT. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-035 | A loose item stores a section id and a section-local position, so a dropped module moves with its section when that section is welded to, or cut away from, another body. | Data | ADR-0013 + ADR-0021 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-036 | Interactable registration is by adjacency or proximity so the registered subject count never exceeds INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET (120) on the worst loaded set S4 (144 authored subjects), and Boarding's contribution to the loose-item pool is capped at PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE (24). | Performance | ADR-0020 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-037 | lit(ref) is false for a thrown glowstick on every tick up to and including its landing tick and reads true from the first exposure table committed after landing, with spawn_deployable called exactly once on the landing tick. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-038 | Spawning a deployable beyond MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE (8) is never rejected: the lowest-id deployable of the same kind enters a visible evict-wane and is removed, and an emitter_deployable_evictions diagnostics counter increments by exactly one. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-039 | A cut completing on a running machine is EXOGENOUS: the Graph re-solves the island on its next run, dependants go STARVE with no tell and no minimum-warning wait, and the cut's SEVERED descriptors open no Attention breach-window row. | Core | ADR-0012 + ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-040 | Same-tick ordering of cut completion, channel cancel or stagger, the Attention integrator run, and abort versus breach must be deterministic: the yield and an open tell can both exist after the run that crosses the threshold, and a cancel on a stagger tick writes the ledger once and plays no abort cue. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 + ADR-0022 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-boarding-041 | Cutting a store module routes its stock to discarded_* so the conservation invariant closes, and an uncapped stub leaks from the home network pool only while the seam valve is open, otherwise from the section's own stock only. | Data | ADR-0011 + ADR-0012 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-042 | The return pod_hop to home is never fuel-gated (accepted at any pool including 0); only the outbound hop is gated by HOP_FUEL_FLOOR_ML and debits HOP_FUEL_ML, and a hop out and back during a TELL does not reset the Attention level. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-043 | While the BURDEN push is active Attention's decay is suspended (level does not fall with the instance held), burden is zero for home-ship modules, own kit, IN_POD, DOWNED and DEAD, and no grams term enters the push. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-boarding-044 | The sanity ABOARD_EXPOSURE dwell is charged by locus from pod_settle to departure and not during the pod leg, preserving the ordering 300 s sortie, 315.8 s stage-1 point, 480 s, 1200 s across the Sanity, Web Map and Suit constants. | Core | ADR-0025 + ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Web Map & Transit (`webmap`)
GDD: `design/gdd/web-map-and-transit.md` | 46 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-webmap-001 | The system owns exactly six facts (roster records, `pod_at`, transit state + `leg_epoch`, berth state, leg/berth content data, ring reads) and is the only writer of them; it never writes fuel, dormancy mechanics, pose arithmetic, geometry, air, light or stimuli directly, only via owners' published mutators inside one atomic apply. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-002 | One persisted SIM block `web.state` exists (persist true, scope WORLD, SHARED, repl_hint SHARED_SUMMARY, schema_version 1) with `restore_after: [layout.state, graph.state, body.state]` only (not `controller.state`); it registers one `state_hash` row with records in `mvp_world.json` order, fixed field order and no dictionary iteration. | Save/Load | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-webmap-003 | One step hook `transit.step` (divisor 1, offset 0, after `body.integrate` and `graph.propagate.failure`) runs every tick, writes no state and calls no Class A mutator; its only outputs are idempotent SYSTEM command submissions and its inputs are reads. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-004 | The block persists per record {derelict_id, template_id, kind, ring (1-based), world_slot (0..7, distinct), seed (0 at MVP), status UNSEEN/SEEN/BOARDED/ABSORBED/ORPHAN, unknown_seen (monotone), first_boarded_T, section_id, body_id} plus machine fields (transit_state, leg_epoch, t0, arrive_T, t_phase, target_id, origin_id, leg_dir, leg_reversed, active leg params {n,vx,vy,p0x,p0y}, berth_target_id, berth_epoch, berth_phase, berth path, pod_body_id, pod_section_id, pod_at); about 756 B canonical, record ceiling 8. | Data | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-005 | `loaded(d)` and `berthed` are derived, never persisted: loaded = not `section(section_id).dormant` from the Graph, berthed = `berth_target_id != NONE` with machine IDLE, leg progress = `T - t0`; no second writer of dormancy exists. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-006 | The roster is read from content `assets/data/world/mvp_world.json` and legs from `assets/data/world/legs.json` (linted on load); star topology only (HOME to one derelict and back; no derelict-to-derelict edge, no steering, no route building). | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-007 | The pod is one persistent Layout body built once at `init_world` from the `pod` template with an always-dormant section; no other body is ever added at MVP (lint `BODY_COUNT_MVP`), at most two bodies are live in any reachable state against `BODIES_LIVE_MAX` 4, and `room_at` on the pod answers `pod_cabin`, never NONE. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-008 | Body pose is set only via BM7 `set_body_pose` (rejects `abs(coord)` above `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT` 2^22) inside atomic applies (`init_world`, departure, `pod_arrive`, `pod_settle`, `berth`/`unberth`, each `berth_step`); re-origins are pure translations of all live bodies (lint `REORIGIN_IS_TRANSLATION`) and dormant bodies' poses are never read by the simulation. | Physics | ADR-0015 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-009 | `init_world(world_seed)` runs in LOADING under one WriteScope in fixed order (LM1 `build_section` x4, Emitter E1, Interaction `place_loose`/`sync_section`, BM1 `place_actor`, BM7 poses, M19 dormancy for A/B/pod, E10 home dock glow, then records); any rejected call fails the build with `SL_NEW_GAME_NO_BASELINE`; nothing is built, discarded or re-instantiated after `init_world`. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-010 | Commands registered: `pod_hop{target_id}`, `berth{derelict_id}`, `unberth` (PLAYER, OWNED_BY_ISSUER, actor_source `body.state`, coalesce NONE, max_age 120) and SYSTEM-only `pod_arrive{leg_epoch}`, `pod_settle{leg_epoch}`, `berth_step{berth_epoch,phase}`, `web_reconcile{record_ids}` (actor_binding NONE); a PLAYER attempt at a SYSTEM command is `ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED`; stale epoch/state is rejected (`STALE_EPOCH`/`NOT_IN_LEG`/`NOT_ARRIVING`), counted at DEBUG and never FAULTED. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-011 | `pod_hop`, `berth` and `unberth` validate in the fixed reason orders given in WM-9 (first failing reason wins, none changes state); all reasons are closed sets and truth-only surfaces; `unberth` has no fuel path or floor. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-012 | The departure apply is one atomic Graph-then-Layout-then-Body apply (M16 fuel debit from HOME only, M19 dormancy of the origin derelict, BM5 transfer avatar to pod, BM7 poses, BM6 velocity, record update, E10 glow off, `POD_DEPARTED`); everything fallible is in validate (no rollback), HOME is never made dormant. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-013 | The leg is a deterministic script: pod velocity is whole pixels, `p1 = p0 + v*n` exactly, speed at most `VEL_MAX_PX_PER_TICK`, the same duration every time (`HOP_LEG_TICKS` 900 placeholder), with no input, hazard, random event or random delay. | Physics | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-014 | Arrival is two SYSTEM commands: `pod_arrive` (applies at `t0+n`, pose to p1, velocity 0, state ARRIVING, status UNSEEN to SEEN, `unknown_seen` set iff lamp predicate lit) then `pod_settle` a fixed `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` (45 placeholder) later (re-check cell, clear target dormancy via M19, BM5 avatar to arrive cell, park pod, IDLE, `pod_at`, E10 glow on, BOARDED + `first_boarded_T`); `ARRIVING` has a fixed simulation length independent of frame rate. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-015 | Phase-boundary commands are pose-carrying and idempotent: the pose is derived from persisted parameters (payload carries epochs/phase only), and persisted boundary values (`arrive_T`, `t_phase`, `first_boarded_T`) are nominal ticks, never the apply tick, so a restore-dropped or queue-delayed command rejoins the uninterrupted trajectory; `LEG_POSE_MISMATCH` is raised iff the offset differs from `v*(T_apply-(t0+n))`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-016 | `web.view.destination_ready` is a pure function of persisted state (`transit_state == IDLE`); the simulation never calls the Pipeline, never waits on the PROJECTOR (no submit path), and the Pipeline holds `TRANSIT_HOLD` until destination_ready and its own projector latch. | Rendering | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-017 | The arrival cell is the authored `arrive_*` cell, never searched; it is validated by Body `can_transfer` at departure (`ARRIVAL_BLOCKED`) and re-validated in `pod_settle`, where a failure takes a total recovery branch (one zero-fuel reversal with `leg_reversed`, then `ARRIVAL_FORCED` placement) and no `pod_settle` may reject. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-018 | Controller `IN_POD` is derived from body occupancy (avatar `body_of_actor` equals pod body), not from a hook call; `transit.step` only reconciles by read. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-019 | Fuel is the Graph's: hops pay `HOP_FUEL_ML` (400) from HOME-kind FUEL_TANK only (found by section `kind`, never by name `ft_main`) via M16 `withdraw_store` in mL, `berth` pays `BERTH_FUEL_ML` (800), both rejected `FUEL_LOW` when `pool - cost < HOP_FUEL_FLOOR_ML` (1500); the system keeps no fuel store, meter or counter, and a short grant still proceeds (never strand). | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-020 | `berth` is a PLAYER command only at HOME with pod at HOME and machine IDLE; it loads the derelict, then drives the home body through an authored piecewise-constant-velocity path (at most 3 phases, exact-sum lint, last phase slow crawl) via `berth_step` commands until `docked(home,B,frame_shift_px)` holds with `frame_shift_px` (696,384) and both velocities 0. | Physics | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-021 | Loaded set is HOME (plus welded pieces) plus at most one unwelded derelict, reachable sets exactly S0 to S4; a departed derelict goes dormant in the departure apply, an arrived one goes live in `pod_settle`; every dormancy flip is gated in validate (skip if already equal, HOME-kind guard `WEB_DORMANCY_HOME`, avatar-present guard `WEB_DORMANCY_AVATAR` excluding the issuing traveller, then M19 checks). | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-022 | Save/restore per state: the active leg params and active berth path are persisted; `post_restore` validates `p1 == p0 + v*n`, berth exact-sum, origin/target consistency, `leg_reversed`, epochs and loaded set, rejecting with named `WEB_RESTORE_*` reasons and never clamping or repairing; `post_restore` submits nothing and the first `transit.step` reconciles. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-023 | Events emitted in the P5 Notify batch: `POD_DEPARTED`, `POD_ARRIVED` (Save/Load autosave trigger), `DERELICT_LOADED`/`DERELICT_UNLOADED`, `BERTHED`/`UNBERTHED` (BERTHED is a Save/Load trigger), `FIRST_BOARDED`; the system owns no sound. | Core | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-024 | Dirty keys are explicit (`WEB/<derelict_id>`, `WEB/T`, `WEB/POD`), no write may rely on a packed-array setter to mark a key, and zero keys are marked per tick during a leg or berth (worst case 5 of 256 per apply). | Performance | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-025 | Each departure, arrival and berth-boundary apply completes within `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 including called mutators, and `transit.step` costs at most 0.1 ms per tick (placeholder) on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK. | Performance | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-026 | Restore-equivalence determinism: a run restored at every command boundary has an identical `state_hash` sequence to a second restored run, and equals the uninterrupted run exactly for web.state/body.state/layout.state at the first IDLE tick (other blocks 12 ticks later, three named bounded accruals excepted). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-027 | The system makes zero `push_stimulus` calls (lint `NO_PUSH_STIMULUS`); the single LOUD noise report per applied `berth` is Interaction's from the `berth` declaration; hops are always allowed during a TELL/BREACH. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-028 | Each dock room's DOCK_STATUS glow is switched via Emitter E10 `set_dock_status` inside the apply that writes `pod_at` (ON home at `init_world`, OFF at departure, ON at `pod_settle` including the reversed leg, no call at berth/unberth) so `switch_on(glow(d)) == (pod_at == record(d))` after every apply. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-029 | Truth-channel rule: `web.state`, `web.view`, `pod_at`, the leg exterior, `visible_unknown` and the residual-lamp point are never wrapped by the Perception Filter or Sanity; no meter, gauge, ETA, progress ring or percentage exists for the hop, berth or map; the lamp is lit iff lamp node state in {OK, DEGRADED} and battery stock > 0. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-030 | Death in the pod is the Controller's DEAD and rolls back with the last autosave (world block is WORLD scope); the roster is never destroyed (no destroy path at MVP); every mid-transit state is restorable. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-031 | Web Map & Transit scripts are integer-only and scene-tree-free: no float, random or wall-clock call, no Node or SceneTree reference, every division and modulo takes non-negative operands, the 8-byte seed is never stored as a GDScript int, and no rng_derive stream is registered. | Core | ADR-0005 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
