| TR-webmap-032 | The published `web.view` read model has a closed field list (nodes[], pod_at, transit_state, destination_ready, leg_target_id, leg_origin_id, leg_dir) with no time, position, progress, fuel, Attention or sanity datum; leg timing is a separate `web.leg_timing()` read, and the ring reads (`ring_of`, `body_is_home`, `locus_kind`) resolve without a hook. | Data | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-033 | `transit.step` skips the per-record `body_id` reconcile compare in IDLE unless Assembly's `last_shape_change_T()` has changed, and submits no `web_reconcile` when no weld or cut occurred. | Performance | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-034 | Timing constants derive from the kernel's published `queue_drain_ticks` (SLIP_MAX_TICKS = 2 x it - 1; IN_POD_SLACK_TICKS = 2 x SLIP_MAX_TICKS; ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS - 1 > SLIP_MAX_TICKS), recomputed by a blocking CI lint that fails the build when the kernel value changes without a GDD update. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0002 + ADR-0026 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-webmap-035 | Content and constant inequalities are enforced by blocking load-time lints on a tuple grid with a per-tuple oracle: slot pitch and coordinate budget against WORLD_COORD_LIMIT / 32, lamp view-ticks against field size and ladder step, settle ticks against tile build rate and MIN_FRAME_RATE, and leg and berth velocity and duration bounds. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-036 | The loaded set stays within fixed tile and subject ceilings (182/300/331/331/449 tiles for S0 to S4, 453 maximum with the pod, against TILES_WORLD_MVP 600 and INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET 120 per neighbourhood), asserted against a real Layout build at S4. | Performance | ADR-0013 + ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-037 | The PROJECTOR builds the largest destination scene (331 tiles) within ceil(331/256) frames, inside the ARRIVE_SETTLE window; a late projector never delays the simulation, the destination stays frozen until settle, and `ARRIVE_SCENE_LATE` is logged. | Rendering | ADR-0034 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-038 | The exterior layer renders pixel-identically at the largest canvas offset (WEB_COORD_BUDGET_PX plus field half-extent) as at the origin at every ladder step on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK, and Parallax2D behaviour under the integer-scale SubViewport is recorded; failure is cosmetic and never a simulation fault. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-039 | During a leg the pod-local camera keeps a constant camera-to-pod offset in every frame at every ladder step and raises no camera-cut event; the exterior composition (filaments, target lamp, astern derelict) is a pure function of persisted data and is identical after a restore at any leg tick, with the kernel LOADING hold preceding the cut. | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-040 | The exterior layer contains no Light2D node: the departed body's emitters are not live during the leg, and the residual lamp is a presentation point, not a light, with colour and alpha independent of Attention level. | Rendering | ADR-0028 + ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-041 | A dormant section is frozen with no fast-forward on reload (Graph partition hash, node hp, taken salvage and horror entity records unchanged); lamp battery drain and endogenous derelict fault compression accrue on loaded ticks only, and a Suit top-up shortens the lamp clock. | Core | ADR-0012 + ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-042 | Interaction subjects (pod console at every dock room, DOCK console at the home airlock) register or unregister in the same apply as the dormancy flip, eligible only when pod_at is that dock, the machine is IDLE and the subject is in reach and lit; no pick or selection value enters web.state, its hash or a save. | Core | ADR-0020 + ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-043 | While the seat is IN_POD no other system's clock is held or deferred: Suit breath reserve, thermal and cell drain, Sanity accrual and Attention TELL/BREACH clocks continue, the Graph reads no Web Map state and home faults may transition mid-leg, and Controller DOWNED/DEAD rules apply with no pod exemption. | Core | ADR-0026 + ADR-0027 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-044 | Audio consumes only `web.view.transit_state` state edges (the berth bed keys on BERTHING with no t0 or n), and the audio listener context switches to the target body in the same apply as `pod_settle`, with the Tell bed uncut by the hop. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-045 | Multi-seat configurations are rejected at MVP: a build with more than one seat fails lint `COOP_HOP_UNDEFINED`, and a missing pod-console anchor fails the content build `POD_CONSOLE_MISSING`. | Networking | ADR-0032 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-webmap-046 | No command or input action shortens a LEG, ARRIVING or BERTHING (only pause and menu), and HOP_LEG_TICKS is the sole pacing lever, enforced by a lint over the command vocabulary and input map. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Attention System (`attention`)
GDD: `design/gdd/attention-system.md` | 47 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-attention-001 | Attention is one scene-tree-free, integer-only (64-bit) SIM block `attention.state` (class SIM, persist, scope WORLD/SHARED, schema_version 1, `restore_after [graph.state, emitter.state]`) advanced only by hook `attention.step` (divisor 6, offset 0, `after [graph.propagate.power, emitter.place]`). | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-attention-002 | There is exactly one global attention level `L` in mAU (1 AU = one walking tick of noise), clamped to `[B, L_CEIL]` with `L_CEIL = ATT_THRESHOLD x L_CEIL_PM / 1000`; no per-section or per-player level exists. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-003 | Each run computes `L' = clamp(L + I_run - dec, B, L_CEIL)` with ceiling-step decay `dec = ceil(6(L-B)/TAU_ATT_TICKS)` so L reaches B exactly, and `dec = 0` on any run that drained a BURDEN push. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-004 | Noise enters only via Class B mutator `push_stimulus(kind, source_ref, class)` (class in QUIET/WALK/RUN/CARRY/TOOL/LOUD/BURDEN; kind NOISE only; unknown class is a counted no-op, clamp never reject), callable only inside a WriteScope by the Controller and Interaction (never Horror). | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-005 | A push adds `U[class] x 1000` mAU to `pending_noise_mAU` and, when the source resolves at push time to a loaded section (Ship Body `actor_section` / `section_of`), to `pending_ledger_mAU[section]`; the `source` PlayerId tag is transient and never stored. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-006 | BURDEN is pushed by the Controller every tick a salvage carry is held, only while the holder's `actor_section` is in a non-home locus body and `loco_state != IN_POD`, keyed on carry state and never on `mass_g`. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-007 | Light and power are ordered reads, not pushes: `emitters()` per loaded section (weights `W_L` per kind; BEACON always 0; FAULT_GLOW 0 in BREACH/SUBSIDING) and `power_use(section_id)` using non-lamp draw `nl_w = max(0, draw_w - lamps_lit_w)` plus Attention's own persisted five-run `nl_hist_w`; no `probe()` calls, no `lux_pm` reads, no double count of lamps. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-008 | Attention reads nothing from Lighting, Audio, Sanity, Horror, Perception Filter, HUD, Instruments, Failure & Trail, Camera or Save/Load, and no Graph/Controller/Suit/Interaction-data/camera/FT/ItemDB code may read an Attention symbol (lint both directions). | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-009 | Baseline `B = B_table[ring_B]` where `ring_B` is the max ring over sections holding a living player (`loco_state != DEAD`; DOWNED counts living), with `held_ring` held while IN_POD; `B_table` is non-decreasing. | Data | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-010 | A boot/data lint enforces the survivable-dark floor and abortability invariants (`abortability_invariant`, `abortability_play`, `survivable_dark_floor`, IR-1..IR-4, `event_defer_invariant`, theta upper bound) at every ring row, failing the data when violated. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-011 | Phase machine CALM -> TELL -> BREACH -> SUBSIDING -> CALM changes only inside `attention.step`; CALM->BREACH, TELL->SUBSIDING and BREACH->TELL do not exist; TELL entry requires `L >= theta`, `T >= refract_until_T`, `T >= rearm_until_T`. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-012 | Tell length is drawn once at TELL entry as `rng_int(TELL_DRAW_LO, TELL_DRAW_HI)` from `rng_derive(world_seed, "attention.tell", rng_counter + RESTORE_KEY_STRIDE x restore_epoch)`; `tell_len >= TELL_MIN_TICKS` (= ticks_for_seconds(W_MIN_WARNING_S)) on every path and a persisted tell_len is never rerolled on restore. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-013 | The tell draw is the only `rng_derive` use; beat jitter and all presentation randomness are a pure integer hash of `(tell_epoch, k, HASH_SEED)`; sim uses only tick `T`, never wall time; diagnostics are excluded from `state_hash`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-014 | Escalation keys on the behaviour-keyed escalation clock `esc_ticks` (advances 6 per run only while `L' >= theta_HOLD`, frozen otherwise, never keyed to `tell_len` or progress); every legal trajectory publishes >= `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` (4) beats in the first `TELL_MIN_TICKS`; one `tell_beat` counter drives both audio and visual. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-015 | A tell aborts to CALM when `below_hold_runs x 6 >= ABORT_HOLD_TICKS`, or at the due run with `L < theta_HOLD`, or on the departure void (`locus_vacated`); abort deposits no STRESS, sets `tell_outcome = ABORTED` and `rearm_until_T`, with an item-keyed rearm cancel via `tell_carry_items`. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-016 | No path reaches BREACH before `tell_start_T + tell_len`; there is no instant-breach path; `attention_arm_tell {tell_len}` (SYSTEM origin only, CALM only, clamped to `[TELL_MIN_TICKS, TELL_MAX_TICKS]`) is the only arming seam. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-017 | At BREACH entry Attention issues M17 `deposit_trace(STRESS, s, BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT)` for every section of `locus_body` in ascending section_id, at most `STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` (8) per run, re-armed in bursts (`STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS` every `STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS`, bounded by `STRESS_HOLD_STEPS`), and takes no first claim on `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP`. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-018 | Locus is the section with highest `ledger_mAU` at TELL entry (ties to lowest section_id), fixed for the epoch with `locus_body = sections_of(body_of(locus))` recorded; the ledger decays with TAU_ATT_TICKS; dormant sections hold no ledger entry. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-019 | BREACH ends at the first of BREACH_MAX_TICKS, `L < theta_HOLD` for `CLEAR_HOLD_TICKS` after `BREACH_MIN_TICKS`, last recorded occupant leaving a non-home locus, or X10 empty-locus case; SUBSIDING lasts `SUBSIDE_TICKS` then sets `refract_until_T`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-020 | Predicate `exogenous_permitted(section) = phase in {BREACH, SUBSIDING} AND section in locus_body` governs every web-originated exogenous harm (M17 RUPTURE/GOUGE/STRESS, M18 EXOGENOUS, horror lethal acts on crew); a `TellAudit` DIAGNOSTIC ledger and replay (`tell_window_contract`) must exist as a test fixture. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-021 | `attention.view` is a SIM read view of integers (never PERCEPTION_OUT) with the fixed field set (`phase`, `breach_phase`, `tell_active`, `tell_remaining`, `tell_elapsed`, `tell_beat`, `tell_beat_T`, `tier`, `tell_outcome`, `tell_epoch`, `phase_enter_T`, `breach_enter_T`, `level_pm`, `locus_section_id`, `exogenous_permitted`); `tell_progress_pm` is never published; `tell_remaining` never reaches a PRESENTATION subscriber. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-022 | The Perception Filter and Sanity can never remove, add, delay or alter any Attention publication or presentation (no phantom tell, no false calm); Attention reads no sanity or PERCEPTION_OUT value. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-023 | Persisted fields (level_mAU, phase, tell_epoch, tell_start_T, tell_len, breach_due_T, tell_beat, esc_ticks, timers, locus ids/body, occupant sets, occ_gone, tell_outcome, rng_counter, pending noise/burden/ledger, ledger, nl_hist_w, held_ring, stress cursor, beacon state) round-trip idempotently; section-keyed arrays stored sorted and bounded to loaded sections. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-024 | Restore is two-phase, returns `(ok, reason, detail)` with closed reasons `ATTENTION_RESTORE_{SCHEMA,RANGE,PHASE_INCOHERENT,SECTION_UNKNOWN,RNG_COUNTER}`, never clamps out-of-range values, and enforces `rng_counter <= tell_epoch`; `restore_epoch` is read from `kernel.clock` and never stored in the block. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-025 | `post_restore` rebuilds caches only (no phase change, no command submitted); the beacon is reconciled in the first post-restore `attention.step` run by comparing persisted `beacon_commanded` with emitter state; presentation cues fire on transitions only, so loading a save triggers no onset/breach cue. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-026 | Mutators track dirty keys explicitly (`ATT/L`, `ATT/P`) because a packed-array element write does not call the property setter in Godot 4.7; presentation polls `attention.view` every frame rather than treating dirty notifications as events. | Save/Load | ADR-0004 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-027 | Save/Load event-defer invariant holds: `TELL_MAX_TICKS + BREACH_MAX_TICKS + SUBSIDE_TICKS + AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS < AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS`, with `REFRACT_TICKS` and `TELL_REARM_TICKS` each >= `AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS`; `breach_phase` reports TELL as CALM so FAIR gate mode stays live. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-028 | The beacon is switched via `set_beacon {emitter_id, on}` as a SYSTEM command at T+1 (max_age_ticks 120) on at every BREACH entry and off at SUBSIDING entry, with rejected commands retried once per run for `BEACON_RETRY_RUNS`; BEACON `gates_actions` is false; exactly one BEACON exists in world data (`BEACON_COUNT`). | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-029 | A pod hop does not interrupt a TELL but voids the due-run breach (TELL -> CALM, ABORTED) when the locus body is non-home, `tell_occupants_at_entry` is non-empty and no living seat remains in it; the dormancy fallback is suppressed once `locus_vacated` holds; a home-ship locus is never voidable. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-030 | Pre-tell cues use a quantised ladder (`CUE_STEPS` 4 above `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM`, hysteresis `CUE_HYST_PM`) that runs in CALM only, forced to 0 otherwise; no continuous mapping of `level_pm` to any visible or audible parameter may exist (calibration lint F-24). | Rendering | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-031 | The Attention Presenter (PRESENTATION subscriber) draws rim filaments only inside the lit region of eligible player-controllable emitters (BEAM, FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN, DEPLOYABLE; excluding BEACON, EMERGENCY cryo lamp, FAULT_GLOW, DOCK_STATUS) in the Y-sort layer plus a dust-direction change, and never alters camera, beam, ambient light or controls in any phase. | Rendering | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-032 | Audio is the guaranteed tell channel: beat 0 is published in the TELL-entry run, audio never starts later than visual, and the non-positional bed reaches every living player; audio dedupes on `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` and keys on `phase`, never `breach_phase`. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-033 | Reduced-motion turns filament motion into a density change; reduced-flashing (default ON) makes the beacon a static mark; no captions or caption field exist for tell beats; an opt-in (default off) haptic pulse fires exactly once per `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` change on the same notification as the audio request. | Input | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-034 | `attention.step` has a p99 CPU budget (proposal 0.05 ms, unratified, OQ-AT-18 / AC-AT-54b, measured on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release exports) and `attention.state` snapshot size is bounded by at most one ledger/history entry per loaded section. | Performance | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-035 | Co-op seams: one machine, one host, `repl_hint SHARED_SUMMARY`; the transient `source` PlayerId keeps VS per-player attribution a data change plus one persisted array and schema bump. | Networking | ADR-0032 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-036 | Every F-1, F-3, F-4 and F-5 intermediate stays below 2^62 under worst-case inputs (96 emitters, weights at cap, 64 LOUD pushes in one run, 10,000 W), with `pending_noise_mAU` and each `pending_ledger_mAU[s]` clamped to L_CEIL before the drain, verified against an arbitrary-precision test oracle. | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-037 | Attention is testable headless without a scene tree: emitters(), power_use and the beat-jitter provider are injected dependencies (production uses the integer hash), so calibration traces and the floor proofs can run against stubs. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-038 | Attention's only outgoing calls are `deposit_trace(STRESS, ...)` and `set_beacon`; it never calls RUPTURE, GOUGE, apply_damage, or any lamp, Lighting or Graph-state write, enforced by source-lint with a negative fixture. | Core | ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-039 | Attention tuning lives in seven data files under `assets/data/attention/` (intake, integrator, weights, baseline, tell, breach, presentation), and noise-per-tick values are read from the registry, so no gameplay literal appears in Attention code. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-040 | Horror's hook is ordered after `attention.step` and `emitter.place`, and Horror's per-run footprint budget is at most `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP - STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN`, so Attention's STRESS deposits cannot be starved; a dropped deposit is re-armed by the next burst. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-041 | Stimulus classes come from Interaction's verb data: cut completion, seam-cut DONE, vent apply and throw landing each push exactly one LOUD, sustained tool use pushes TOOL per tick, breaker and door verbs push nothing, and weld completion or Assembly pushes no LOUD. | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-042 | The Suit contract exposes only `tool_charge(player_id, tool_suppressant)`, `consume_charge(player_id, def_id) -> ok` and `OUT_OF_CHARGES`; no suppressant effect verb ships at MVP and `tool_suppressant` is in no MVP start kit. | Core | ADR-0027 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-043 | Authoring lint `DERELICT_LOCUS_BODY_NO_FAULT` requires every body that can be a locus (each derelict alone and each welded to home) to contain at least one authored active Graph fault, so breach STRESS always has a subject; home and pod bodies are the only exemptions. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-044 | A source-lint token scan forbids `level_pm`, `level_mAU`, `tell_remaining` and `attention_level` in UI and presentation roots and assets outside an allow-list (Debug Overlay, diagnostics, tests), and the overlay is excluded from release exports by file. | UI | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-045 | Presenter visual lead-in compensates measured audio output latency (`VISUAL_LEAD_IN_TICKS >= ceil(L_out_ms x 60 / 1000)`) so no visual change for a beat appears before its audible onset; the value stays 0 and the AC deferred until the latency spike exists. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-046 | The Attention Presenter has a p99 frame cost of at most 0.25 ms (no frame over 1 ms) on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release exports with 16 resolved strands in four lit regions, with strand caps of 9 per lit region and 16 on screen. | Performance | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-attention-047 | Presentation of a restored or hitched state shows the end state with no transition animation, and a multi-beat stall plays at most one beat without replay; `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` is strictly increasing lexicographically across epochs. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Horror Entities (`horror`)
GDD: `design/gdd/horror-entities.md` | 43 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-horror-001 | Horror state lives in one scene-tree-free, integer-only SIM block `horror.state` (persist true, scope WORLD, SHARED, schema_version 1, restore_after [layout.state, graph.state, emitter.state, attention.state]) with at most `HORROR_MAX_MVP` 1 resident record of type `horror_t01`. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-horror-002 | `horror.step` is registered as one kernel hook (divisor 5, offset 0, after [attention.step, emitter.place]); an unknown hook id fails boot. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-003 | Every Horror duration is an integer count of runs published as runs x 5 ticks; a SIM-clock duration that is not a multiple of 5 fails a data-load lint (L3_DUR_LO/HI exempt); the rig pose is stepped per run and presentation interpolates pos_prev to pos_curr over 5 ticks. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-004 | Behaviour (LURK/STALK/REACH/STRIKE/RETREAT/TRANSIT) and all harm gates are functions of `attention.view` fields only (phase, tell_epoch, tell_outcome, locus_section_id, exogenous_permitted, breach_enter_T, phase_enter_T); no stored threat/aggro/awareness value exists, persisted or derived. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-005 | Every web-originated harm call (apply_suit_damage, M17 RUPTURE, M17 GOUGE) is made only if `exogenous_permitted(section)` holds at the call tick, checked at REACH begin, STRIKE entry and each M17 call; bodily scope uses Body `actor_section`, and the `tell_window_contract` TellAudit replay has zero violations. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-006 | Per-type tuning (speeds, ranges, strike_bp, cooldowns, unfold bounds, L3 constants, asset ids) is loaded from a data row keyed by type_id; the data lint rejects fields named damage, accuracy, armour, dps, xp, unlock, health, hp, kill, loot, threat, aggro, hate. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-007 | Horror writes the world only via Class B path-(a) mutators `deposit_trace` (CHILL, hull-only GOUGE, hull-only RUPTURE; never STRESS/BURN) and `apply_suit_damage(player_id, STRIKE_BP, source_ref)`, plus its own published state; it submits no SYSTEM-origin command and never calls push_stimulus, set_beacon or any Attention writer. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-008 | Horror issues at most `HE_FOOTPRINT_BUDGET` 8 deposit_trace calls per Graph window (w = floor(T/6)) and `MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` 4 per run, priority RUPTURE > GOUGE > CHILL; excess writes are not attempted and counted `horror_footprints_withheld`. | Performance | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-009 | A horror is a registered Emitter HORROR subject (E6 at arrival, E8 once per run, E7 at egress/unload; registered iff non-TRANSIT and body loaded, reconciled every run); it emits nothing, adds no LightOccluder2D or shadow, and never changes lux/lit/beam of any subject. | Rendering | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-010 | Target selection reads `lux(ref)` once per living candidate seat per run with a stateless `lux_pm >= LIT_ON_PM` (150) test (not the Emitter `lit` bit); highest lux_pm wins, ties lowest seat id; the target is never persisted and uses zero probe() calls. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-011 | Horror position is owned in `horror.state` (integer base px plus sub-pixel residue and last_octant), moved by Body's pure `LocomotionStep` bound to Layout `solid_in` with foot box 16x8; no CharacterBody2D, no Body AGENT row, no physics query. | Physics | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-012 | Speeds satisfy V_CREEP_MPX 1000 < V_HUNT_MPX 1500 < V_WALK x 1000 so a walking seat always outpaces the horror; there is no lunge travel; the STRIKE resolves at the locked point. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-013 | Pathing is integer breadth-first over the Layout room_adjacency graph (passable-leaf edges only, ties lowest id, at most `PATH_EXPANSIONS_MAX` expansions per run), cached per (goal_room, layout_hash, version(section_id) of every body section) and rebuilt on any restore; NavigationServer2D/NavigationAgent2D are not used. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-014 | REACH locks point P at begin (non-tracking), draws UNFOLD_TICKS = 5 x ceil_div(rng_int(UNFOLD_LO/5, UNFOLD_HI/5) x UNFOLD_SCALE_PCT, 100) once from `rng_derive_epoch(world_seed, "horror.tell", counter)`, and publishes state/locked_pt/reach_end_T; the strike resolves on the first run with T >= reach_end_T. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-015 | STRIKE calls `apply_suit_damage` once per living seat with dist < R_SNAP_PX of P in the same room as P (strict same-room test using Layout room_at), only if the predicate holds, and lays one STRUCK mark if any call was made; REACH begin requires `connected()` via Layout `door_passage` and `linked()` governs standoff rules. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-016 | All simulation randomness is the REACH length only, via the kernel's `rng_derive_epoch` (counter + RESTORE_KEY_STRIDE x restore_epoch, counter < 2^20); every other variation (mark seed, waypoint, L3 timing) uses the pure-integer `he_hash` with fixed salts and HE_HASH_SEED; no wall clock or global RNG; golden vectors pinned by AC-HE-139. | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-017 | `restore_epoch` is owned by Save/Load (u32 in slot_epoch.bin), exposed read-only in `kernel.clock`; Horror reads it only through rng_derive_epoch and never stores it in `horror.state`. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-018 | `horror.state` persists a closed manifest of fields (identity/place, timers, REACH/STRIKE, nav goal and counters, transit/ingress, travel accumulators, epoch ledgers, mark ring plus mark_id counter, "horror.tell" counter, fp_window/fp_count, disturbances and last_end_T map, lurk_dwell_index, unfold_scale_pct) and excludes target, path cache, restore_epoch and horror.view.debug. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-019 | Restore is two-phase with closed failure set HORROR_RESTORE_* (SCHEMA, RANGE, STATE_INCOHERENT, SECTION_UNKNOWN, RNG_COUNTER, MARK_RING); out-of-range values hard-fail and are never clamped; restore is idempotent, uses plain data only and explicit dirty keys, and a restore into REACH keeps the full remaining telegraph. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-020 | Same (save bytes, restore_epoch) pair replays identically (positions, marks, strike lengths, state_hash); the mark ring, counter and epoch ledgers are included in state_hash and `horror.view.debug` is non-hashed and never read by any SIM rule. | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-021 | The mark ring holds at most `MAX_MARKS` 32 persisted records {mark_id, kind, section_id, pos_px, seed, life_left_ticks} with per-kind quotas (RIME 7, HAIRLINES 3, STRUCK 3), oldest-of-kind eviction, at most `MARKS_PER_RUN_MAX` inserts per run, ageing only while the mark's section is loaded; marks are laid regardless of any viewer's hallucination state. | Data | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-022 | A body-unloaded record is dormant (no step, no ageing, no fast-forward) except the TELL departure into TRANSIT, which is evaluated first on every run before dormancy; TRANSIT records are unregistered, have no position, and wait if the destination body is unloaded. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-023 | On weld/cut the record re-bases all persisted body-frame points by delta = section_offset - offset_seen_px, re-derives body_id from body_of, and calls E8 in the same run; the path cache is recomputed on any layout.state change. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-024 | L3 light disturbances are published state `horror.view.disturbances[]` {emitter_id, strength_pm, start_T, dur_ticks} on FIXTURE and NODE_LANTERN emitters only (never BEAM, BEACON, FAULT_GLOW, DEPLOYABLE, EMERGENCY or DOCK_STATUS), bounded by L3_MAX_ACTIVE and L3_MIN_GAP_TICKS, persisted, never a call into Lighting. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-025 | `horror.view` publishes entity_id, type_id, state, pos_px, body_id, section_id, locked_pt, reach_end_T, strike_T and seats hit, transit_until_T, ingress/egress events, disturbances, mark ring and frozen `horror_*` diagnostic counters for Audio, Lighting, Sanity, Perception Filter and Debug Overlay; Horror owns no sound, mix or cue name. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-026 | Horror's truth-channel outputs (M17 writes, marks, disturbance state, suit damage, true horror.view positions) are bit-identical with and without a Perception Filter; Horror holds no sanity reference and reads no PERCEPTION_OUT block. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-027 | `horror.step` meets `HORROR_STEP_BUDGET_MS` 0.15 ms P99 per run with at most 4 lux reads, 5 solid_in queries, PATH_EXPANSIONS_MAX expansions on cache miss, 0 probes, and at most one apply_suit_damage per seat. | Performance | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-028 | HORROR subjects accept no player verb (forced commands return INVALID_TARGET); no HP, kill state, loot, XP, hit feedback, threat bar or proximity indicator exists; the suppressant `repel` interface is data-dormant at MVP. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-029 | Horror reads seat state only via Body `actors_on`/`pos_curr`/`actor_section` and Controller `controller.state.loco_state` (DOWNED, DEAD, IN_POD, STAGGERED); DOWNED/DEAD seats are never candidates or hit, and Horror never moves or staggers a seat. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-030 | The mark render subscriber draws within `HORROR_MARK_DRAW_GROUPS` of the shared `D_marks` budget in the floor-marks band, and the rig is Y-sorted at 12 fps with 15-degree segment rotation; presentation never writes the camera. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-031 | Horror reads Layout only through published queries with per-run read budgets (room_adjacency on cache miss only; room_at, anchor_geometry and solid_in at most 5 per run; hull_segments_near, version and emitters_near at most once per run), none per tick, and treats a NONE or UNKNOWN read as neither unlit nor open. | Performance | ADR-0013 + ADR-0014 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-032 | Door passability for pathing and REACH uses Layout's door_passage (open_extent_px, hinge-aware gap_rect) with one rule: passable iff open_extent_px >= 16 across an east-west run and >= 8 across a north-south run; standoff rules use linked, REACH begin uses connected. | Core | ADR-0013 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-033 | Seat commands issued during tick t are applied by body.integrate in that tick and horror.step reads positions after body.integrate; this tick-indexing convention is pinned by a fixture, and a measured input-to-display latency above INPUT_LATENCY_TICKS 3 on RM-DESKTOP/RM-DECK release exports forces UNFOLD_LO to 100. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 + ADR-0015 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-horror-034 | The serialised horror.state with a full ring (32 marks), 4 L3 entries and 16 last_end_T entries is at most 16 KiB, per-tick dirty keys stay at or below MAX_DIRTY_KEYS 256, and every mutation declares explicit dirty keys (a missing key fails the save-equivalence test). | Save/Load | ADR-0004 + ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-035 | Horror step runs seven ordered transition guards with fixed precedence: restore first, then layout change, then the tell edge before dormancy, then phase edge; the 4-phase by 6-state matrix is exhaustively tested and a new tell_epoch seen mid-REACH fizzles it to RETREAT in the same run. | Core | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-036 | Per-epoch counters (strikes, gouges, ruptures, chill totals, gouged_seg_ids) reset lazily at the first run that sees a new tell_epoch, identically for a record whose body was unloaded and for a record restored from an older save. | Save/Load | ADR-0023 + ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-037 | Cross-parameter relations among Horror tuning values (R_SNAP < REACH_RANGE < LURK_STANDOFF, ingress delay plus stalk floor plus mark lead <= TELL_MIN_TICKS, STRIKE_BP versus suit patch cap, L3 flash rate, MAX_MARKS quotas versus smear retention) are arithmetic assertions at data load, with negative fixtures that fail boot. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-038 | The source_ref passed to apply_suit_damage is classed EXOGENOUS once at record creation (boot or ingress arrival), persisted with the record, valid across restore independently of Emitter registration, and an unclassed ref is a build-failing programmer error. | Save/Load | ADR-0027 + ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-039 | Emitter registration is idempotent across restore and body load (post_restore E6 on an already-registered ref returns false and is not a defect; registered at stored pos_px before any other act on load), and HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX 2 plus A_max stays within MAX_MOVING_SUBJECTS 16, a failed first-registration E6 being a budget defect. | Core | ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-040 | Accessibility settings (reduced motion, reduced flashing) change presentation only: Lighting caps the read L3 strength at L3_STRENGTH_RM_CAP_PM, pose-tag sequence and stage lengths are identical to the setting-off run, and the SIM strength_pm and state_hash are unchanged by the setting. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-041 | Rig pose tag and mark rendering are pure functions of published horror.view state (state, state_enter_T, now) so the first frame after a restore needs no transition, sprites are drawn at alpha 0 or 255 only, and nothing is drawn at 0 percent ambient with no light in range. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-042 | The Horror test harness injects every neighbour read (attention.view, Layout, Emitter, Suit, Graph, Body) through stubs with a stub-or-real switch per read, and a replay runner returns state_hash every 600 ticks for a (world_seed, restore_epoch, script) triple; no Horror story starts before it. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-horror-043 | A headless 864000-tick soak of repeated breach cycles leaves horror.state structurally stable: key set equals the committed manifest, every list stays at its cap from the first full-ring cycle (at most cycle 20), mark_id strictly increases, and all withheld and wedged counters stay 0. | Performance | ADR-0023 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Sanity & Hallucination Rules (`sanity`)
GDD: `design/gdd/sanity-and-hallucination-rules.md` | 40 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-sanity-001 | Sanity registers exactly three state blocks (`sanity.state` SIM/WORLD/SHARED_SUMMARY, `sanity.ledger` SIM/WORLD/HOST_ONLY, `sanity.perception` PERCEPTION_OUT/non-persisted/OWNER_PRIVATE), all PER_PLAYER, each with schema_version 1, explicit dirty keys, restore-idempotence, default_state, post_restore and a two-phase restore returning (ok, reason, detail). | Save/Load | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-sanity-002 | Sanity is scene-tree-free and integer-only; level is an int mSU in [0, SAN_MAX=100000] per PlayerId (never peer_id), starting at SAN_MAX on first bind, and an unbound seat does not drift while keeping state. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-003 | A single hook `sanity.drift` (divisor 12, offset 0, 5 Hz) runs per bound seat in ascending PlayerId through the fixed 7-step order, with `after:` edges on graph.propagate.failure and suit.vitals (tolerates_stale), attention.step, emitter.place, body.integrate, interaction.step and horror.step. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-004 | The run clock is absolute (`run = T div 12` from kernel.clock); every stored stamp (start_run, active_run, end_run, last_onset_run, debounce, until_run) is an absolute run, while stage_dwell and harm_below_runs are executed-run counters that freeze when unbound. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-005 | Level changes only through D.1: net loss per run is clamp(E - R + draw, -REC_MAX, +DRIFT_MAX_PER_RUN=36); one-shots go via `pending` (PENDING_MAX 3000) and drain only into cap headroom; only debug force_level/force_stage may bypass. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-006 | Exposure is a closed list of four true SIM inputs (E1 ring/aboard, E2 Attention phase/tier, E3 horror distance only, E4 dormant); darkness, Suit vitals, player noise, any PERCEPTION_OUT/Perception/Lighting/Audio value and player action counts must never feed level (enforced by a source-scan lint of the D.1 path). | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-007 | Sanity reads Attention only via attention.view fields phase, tier, tell_outcome, tell_epoch (never level_pm) and never writes Attention. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-008 | Stage is a pure function stage_of(level, prev_stage, stage_dwell) with entry thresholds 70000/55000/25000, exit = entry + STAGE_HYST 2000, a 25-run dwell, and no stage 4; a stage change never ends a live lie. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-009 | harm_eligible is one SIM bit (sets after 150 consecutive runs below SAN_HARM_ENTER 15000, clears at once above SAN_HARM_EXIT 20000), exposed via `actor_sanity_below_harm_threshold(player_id) -> bool`, and the class-(c) render level is that bit with no PERCEPTION_OUT copy. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-010 | Boot assertions D.4 (i)-(v) lead invariants, ambient-floor invariants, THRESHOLD_ORDER, HOME_REFUGE and related SANITY_BOOT_* checks must refuse boot with a named reason on any retune that breaks them. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-011 | `record_falsification` and `record_harm_event(actor, victim, tick)` are Class B (clamp-never-reject) mutators called from Interaction's interaction.step hook; an unknown id or unresolvable ref is a no-op with one log line, never a fault or exception. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-012 | `record_harm_event` appends only {tick, actor, victim, attributed_to: WEB} to an `incidents` ring (INCIDENTS_MAX 16) and changes nothing else (no reward, no recovery, no level change). | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-013 | `sanity.ledger` is HOST_ONLY and PER_PLAYER and no symbol of it or of `sanity.perception` may be read outside `sanity/` except the Debug Overlay (DIAGNOSTIC) and the Perception Filter / replication adapter respectively (lint). | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-014 | Every lie must pass the shared guard rails SN-15 (corruptible module-body channel only, live consequence-channel witness, second instrument, leak always present, uniform immunity after a check), applied by effective direction (COMFORT/ALARM) and re-checked every run, with failure INVALIDATING the lie. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-015 | Onset is triggered only by a lit-entry event (first illumination of a subject per seat per visit, via cid(P) != persisted last_cid), with a 10-run batch window, a 20 s per-subject debounce stamp, and a fixed gate evaluation order. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-016 | RNG draws use only kernel `rng_derive(world_seed, "sanity.<stream>/<PlayerId>[/<stable_id>]", counter)` / `rng_int` with persisted counters; draw count is fixed at 0, 1 or 4 per event (0 when sum_W < 1), and counters advance only on gated player-driven events. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-017 | Determinism: replay is bit-exact for identical actions, no content depends on restore_reason or restore_epoch, snapshot/state_hash iterate sorted keys, and get_instance_id, hash(), shuffle, pick_random, unstable sort_custom and unsorted Dictionary iteration are lint-banned. | Save/Load | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-018 | `sanity.perception` is rebuilt in full every run and in post_restore as a closed-schema, slot-ordered list (int/bool/String fields only; no float/Vector/Node/Callable) listing every ACTIVE entry with an `away` flag; PENDING entries are never listed. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-019 | post_restore must not prune or mutate persisted state: a well-formed but dead subject_ref restores successfully, is projected as-is, and is INVALIDATED and pruned by the next hook run so state_hash matches the uninterrupted run at every tick. | Save/Load | ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-020 | Restore validates strictly (out-of-range or wrong-shape values are hard failures, never clamped); JSON-parsed integer-valued floats are accepted and re-cast to int, while fractional/NaN/inf values fail SHAPE; values stay below 2^53. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-021 | `last_onset_run` (init -1) and `last_cid`, `evented[]`, `batch_end_run`, `entry_pending`, `debounce[]`, `next_lie_id` and per-subject visit counters are persisted ledger state (not rebuilt caches) and per-subject visit counters are pruned at the first hook run after subject destruction. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-022 | Sanity must never touch body state/positions, geometry, trails, consequence-channel items, Attention outputs, prompts/durations, save/pause surfaces, camera/controller values, the human channel, emitters or Suit damage logic (identical input at stages 0-3 gives byte-identical pos/facing/aim_angle). | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-023 | Sanity registers no command, no INPUT_SURFACE and no player-origin anything, and submits no SYSTEM command. | Input | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-024 | Lie targeting is data-driven from `assets/data/sanity/lie_targets.json` (surface enum 1-5, witness descriptors, lethal_fault_modes, comfort-surface L-G guard rows); an invalid row fails boot with SANITY_BOOT_LIE_TARGET_DATA, and no lie is encoded in authored templates. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-025 | All gameplay values are `[PLACEHOLDER]` registry-backed knobs with a single fixture `tests/fixtures/sanity/safe_ranges.json` from which Tuning Knobs ranges are generated and CI-diffed. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-026 | Runtime data files under `assets/data/sanity/` must be listed in the export preset include_filter so the exported (template_release) build passes the same boot assertions as the editor. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-027 | Debug force_stage/force_level live under `src/debug/`, are excluded from Release by file, registered only via runtime `load()` guarded by `OS.has_feature(<gate tag>)` with no static reference from shipped code, and no such symbol/path may appear in the Release PCK (CI scan). | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-028 | Perf: on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release export with 4 seats and 14 eligible subjects per compartment, the Sanity step median is <= 0.5 ms and max <= 2 ms (ratcheting), with zero Performance.OBJECT_COUNT and static memory growth. | Performance | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-029 | Snapshot dirty keys per run stay under MAX_DIRTY_KEYS 256 and snapshot size is bounded over a 60-minute 4-seat soak (visit map bounded by the number of registered lie-capable subjects, at most 196 per seat in the MVP slice). | Performance | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-030 | CLASSES_ENABLED is a data bitmask (a=1, b=2, c=4, d=8) with MVP = {b}; classes a/c/d are implemented and testable with stubs but disabled, and turning class (b) off lets live lies expire normally with no snapshot format change. | Data | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-031 | Sanity reads upstream systems only through published views and queries, and the lit-entry lookup may call exactly actor_section, actor_local_px, room_at, compartment_of_room or compartment_at, nodes_in and lit; a write-spy on Attention, Graph and Emitter must record zero writes. | Core | ADR-0025 + ADR-0017 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-032 | Lie eligibility reads the Suit view (tools, carried_instance, scanner_equippable, five true_value keys) as read-only input; when the Suit view is unbound, a stub path is taken, a suit_read_stub_hits counter increments, and no single-instrument comfort lie is created. | Core | ADR-0027 + ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-033 | The SHARED_SUMMARY replication projection of sanity.state, and the sanity_view query, expose exactly {stage, tell_on, harm_eligible} with no raw level or counter; sanity.ledger and sanity.perception contribute no key to the replication adapter. | Networking | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-034 | Sanity publishes read-only accessors ledger_listed(player, lie_id) and reach_lie_active, which must always agree with sanity.perception membership (including the away flag and after restore) and must not alter any SHAPE key set or the state hash. | Core | ADR-0024 + ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-035 | A WriteScope audit hook must show every write made by the hook, record_falsification, record_harm_event and the debug controls targets a block id beginning with `sanity.`, and no other registry entry (command, input surface, PLAYER-origin) is written. | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-sanity-036 | A mutator called on a tick not aligned to the 5 Hz hook (T mod 12 not 0) stamps absolute runs as T div 12 + 1, writes its ledger effect immediately so state_hash matches a control at every tick, and updates sanity.perception only at the next hook run (at most 11 ticks later). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0025 + ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-sanity-037 | Restore, including the death-reload path, reinstates stored Sanity state verbatim: no recompute of stage from level, counters (harm_below_runs, stage_dwell) resume from stored values, and the autosave state fully replaces the dead seat's frozen state. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-038 | Sanity's candidate set must read the simulation lit(ref) value, and a debug counter lit_render_mismatch must count disagreements with the renderer's drawn-lit set, which may lag by at most LIT_RECHECK_TICKS (6) ticks. | Rendering | ADR-0017 + ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-039 | No UI, HUD, crew strip, pause, chart, menu or inspect-card source may reference a sanity symbol outside the Debug Overlay, enforced by a lint over the UI source root, and no sanity-bearing number, bar, icon or tint may exist on any screen. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-sanity-040 | Every Graph node hosting a lie-capable gauge in lie_targets.json must have an instrument placement record and an INSTRUMENT OBJECT subject registered through sync_section for which lit(ref) answers; a node without one fails with INSTRUMENT_NO_BINDING, and the per-seat visit map is bounded by those registered subjects. | Data | ADR-0025 + ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Perception Filter (`percfilter`)
GDD: `design/gdd/perception-filter.md` | 45 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-percfilter-001 | The Filter registers no kernel block, step hook or mutator and subscribes only as `PRESENTATION`; its perceived channel is never read by simulation, never persisted and never part of `state_hash`. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-002 | The Filter writes only `CanvasItem` members, `Material`/shader parameters, particle members and audio requests; it writes no real `Light2D` member, no `Camera2D`/`Viewport`/`SubViewport`/`SubViewportContainer` property, no simulation state; a grep lint enforces it. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-003 | The Filter holds no handle on `sanity.state` or `sanity.ledger`, imports no simulation module, never registers/moves an Interaction subject and never submits a command (lint). | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-004 | The Filter is the only non-diagnostic caller of Graph `true_value(kind, id)` and Suit `true_value(key, player_id)`; HUD and Instruments obtain values only via `perceived()`. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-005 | `perceived(ref: PerceptionRef, viewer: PlayerId) -> Perceived` is a pull query called from the render callback, memoised per (ref, viewer, frame); the no-lie path returns the true value with `leak_amplitude_pm` 0, `motion_mode` HONEST and allocates nothing. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-006 | The `Perceived` record (displayed_pm, displayed_value, frame_override, motion_mode {HONEST,GLIDE,TICK,STALE,BREATH,RETURN}, tick_phase_ms, leak_amplitude_pm, leak_modes bitmask {GLEAM=1,GLIDE=2,SOUND=4}, tremor_weight_pm, lie_id, lie_kind) never contains the true value while a lie is active. | Data | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-007 | `PerceptionRef` is a tagged union (GRAPH_NODE, GRAPH_COMPARTMENT, SUIT_KEY, SCANNER, HOUSING_GLOW, STATE_GLYPH, MACHINE_FRAME) with a stable integer `ref_key` (never an instance id); Sanity's `subject_ref {stable_id, surface 1..5}` maps to exactly one ref via one constructor (bijection). | Data | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-008 | `perceived` answers only for the local seat keyed by PlayerId (never peer_id); a remote PlayerId returns the honest record and `sanity.perception` is `OWNER_PRIVATE`. | Networking | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-009 | Every presentation node under the world layer is TRUTH by default; a `PerceptionTag` resource {class TRUTH_PX/TRUTH_ST/LEAK/CORRUPTIBLE, lie_surface_id} marks exceptions, resolved by a headless-testable resolver; CORRUPTIBLE requires a `lie_surface_id` in `lie_targets.json`. | Data | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-010 | Build-failing lints: no TRUTH_PX node has a non-rest leak parameter in any frame; TRUTH_ST allows only `edge_bleed_pm`; seam-lane valve/breaker ids absent from the wrapped registry; every module kind in `lie_targets.json` has a tagged CORRUPTIBLE surface; no `set_shader_parameter` on a material shared by more than one node. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-011 | The consequence channel (trails, acoustic classes, Suit body cues, lamp brightness from `served_pm`, AIRFLOW, SEAL_STRAIN), character sprites, crewmate beams, true reveal radius, camera framing, HUD frame, walls/door geometry and Attention tells are never removed, altered, added to or shifted; no lie adds scorch, mist, drip, frost, puddle or sparks. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-012 | Lie trajectories run on sim time: `lie_tau = (T_c - SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR * act_run + tick_alpha) / TICK_RATE` (divisor 12, rate 60), using Sanity's `active_run`; displayed value is a pure function of entry, live true value, lie_tau and per-lie constants, modulo one non-persisted slew state; pause freezes lies. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-013 | All presentation randomness is a pure 32-bit integer hash of (viewer, ref_key, lie_key=Sanity `lie_id`, epoch) plus use tag, with explicit masking; never `rng_derive`, global RNG or `RandomNumberGenerator`; per-lie draws use epoch 0 so they are restore-exact. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-014 | Presentation state (envelopes, slew, return tracks, breath phase, tick phase, tag registry) is never saved and is rebuilt from the formula on `hard_start`; the only persisted inputs are Sanity's ledger including `lie_id` and `next_lie_id`. | Save/Load | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-015 | The first RUNNING frame after LOADING, and after `force_stage`/`force_level`, is rendered already filtered with envelopes at target and ACTIVE lies at formula value with full leak (`hard_start`); `perceived` is answerable before the first RUNNING frame; counter `first_frame_filtered`. | Save/Load | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-016 | A lie ends only by its entry leaving `sanity.perception`; the three end types are presentation-identical (RETURN follower at `GLIDE_RETURN_RATE` until CLEAR); `away` entries cause no ONSET/RETURN/CLEAR/sound change and `away` is read only by the rim gate. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-017 | Every drawn ACTIVE lie carries GLEAM plus a hue-independent motion mode (`popcount(leak_modes)>=2`, GLEAM bit set); only SOUND is droppable; a lie that cannot render both is not drawn (honest path, `lie_unrenderable`); enforced by `lint_leak_palette.gd`. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-018 | Gleam is violet `#B07BE6`, edge-only, lit with its host, capped at `LEAK_MAX_CH` (= `DECOY_MAX_CH` 0.85, at most 0.9 x `GLOW_HDR_THRESHOLD`), and never a fill, vignette, blur, colour grade or sanity meter. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-019 | Leak geometry is defined in art px of the 640x360 base, drawn inside the world render (not as a container post-process) as exact integer multiples of zoom k at every k in [1, k_auto+2] under render pass A and B; widths re-derived on a zoom cut; marks are sprites/quads, never `draw_line`; nearest sampling only. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-020 | Filter luminance output obeys PIPE PR-13 (<=3 flashes/s per light per ninth-of-picture region and on frame mean) and HR-13 (HUD <=3 Hz); all sources are amplitude-continuous except the rise-only onset step; verified by a 60 s flash scan at stage 3 at the largest field. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-021 | Stage effects use linear envelopes slewed over `STAGE_RAMP_S` (default 8 s, min 6.5) of `present_time`, rate latched at each target change; stage steps are instant in SIM and never announced; rim fringe ships LIE_BOUND (`reach_lie_active`) and hue bleed ships default-off (`AMBIENT_FRINGES_ENABLED` false). | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-022 | Lying numeral readouts share a region scheduler: `T_tick = max(LIE_TICK_MS, N_r*334)`, floor 334 ms, with a region governor guaranteeing >=334 ms between Filter-owned lying ticks; honest numerals use the HUD-owned 500 ms `HONEST_TICK_MS` grid. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-023 | Wander (one sinusoid per lie, 0.40 to 0.45 Hz, `WANDER_AMP_PX` 2.2) guarantees a held lie's needle tip changes quantised pixel at least every 1.0 s; needles below `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` 16 are not lied on; reduced motion scales time axis only (`RM_GLIDE_SCALE` >=0.7) and never scales wander or removes a leak. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-024 | The Filter owns audio class ids `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES` (`FA_LIE_SIG_GAUGE`, `FA_LIE_SIG_SPRITE`; VS-A `FA_PHANTOM_MOVE`, `FA_PHANTOM_PRESENCE`) lint-disjoint from Graph witness, Attention and Horror classes, and sends requests `(viewer, ref/position, intent {ON, LEVEL(pm), OFF}, class)` with ON on the first ACTIVE frame carrying `(LEVEL, T_s, SOUND_BREATH_FLOOR_PM)` and OFF at CLEAR. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-025 | Frame order per render callback: Avatar Presenter, then the Filter (dirty-marks in notification, draws in render), then Lighting reads the per-viewer decoy list; SIM-to-pixel latency is up to one sanity run (12 ticks) plus one frame. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-026 | Gleam transport follows a ladder gated by spike S-1: (1) per-instance shader params, (2) `MultiMeshInstance2D` overlay per (depth band, atlas page, light mask), (3) otherwise the surface is not lied on; added effect draws stay within `D_FX_ADDED_MAX` 10; MVP container draws 0. | Performance | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-027 | Presentation CPU budget: median <= `CPU_BUDGET_MS` 0.5 ms, p99 <= 1.0 ms, max <= 2.0 ms per frame; GPU leak block <= 0.05/0.15 ms at rest (1080p/2160p) and <= 0.15/0.40 ms at stage 3 with four lies; no `STEP_BUDGET_MS` (no hook). | Performance | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-028 | Fail open to truth: any missing, malformed or unresolvable Filter datum yields the honest path with the matching counter incremented and never propagates to simulation or TRUTH nodes; one live lie per `subject_ref` (lowest `start_run`, then `lie_id`; `lie_collisions`). | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-029 | Dial/readout surfaces supply a calibration record via the tag (`arc_deg`, `needle_len_px`, zone bands, `lsb`; linear branch `travel_px`) plus a separate pivoted needle sprite, `_msk` edge slice and both honest state frames; absent record means honest path and a coverage-lint failure. | Data | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-030 | Container route at MVP is PER_ELEMENT with the `SubViewportContainer.material` slot null (assigned/nulled only by the pipeline root) and published as `filter_route`/`NOT_USED` (not an error); the Filter publishes the decoy list `{decoy_id, kind, anchor, shape, tint_leak, start, end}` for Lighting on mask bit 3 (VS-A). | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-031 | The Filter ships exactly three distinct ShaderMaterial source files (HullSurface leak block, Emissive leak block, fringe); all are code shaders, none reads TIME, none overrides the sprite's nearest filter, and no sixth material is added without a ratified Lighting decision. | Rendering | ADR-0024 + ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-032 | Every leak shader variant is instantiated once off-screen during LOADING under the black hold, so no variant compiles on first use in RUNNING; the registry counter `variant_first_use_after_loading` must stay 0 in a full 10-minute session. | Performance | ADR-0024 + ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-033 | Filter correctness depends on a windowed, rendering-capable test lane distinct from the headless CI lane; render-tagged checks must report SKIPPED, never PASS, when the baseline capture is blank or the draw count reads 0. | Rendering | ADR-0010 + ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-034 | Leak transport and the added-draw budget must hold on each renderer backend in the spike matrix (D3D12, Vulkan, Compatibility) on both reference machines, with draw-call counts read through the engine rendering-info monitor, whose 4.7.2 availability is unverified. | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-035 | Presentation slew and ramp advance with delta clamped to `DT_SLEW_MAX_S` (0.1 s) so a render hitch cannot snap a needle or produce a flash pair, while `lie_tau` keeps advancing by true simulation time. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0024 + ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-percfilter-036 | Lie presentation state is independent of visibility and distance: off-screen, unlit or other-compartment lies keep full leak modes, amplitude at or above floor and their sound request, evaluate from the formula at current `lie_tau` on re-entry, and play no onset animation. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-037 | The Filter's audio request stream re-sends LEVEL only when it moves by at least `AUDIO_LEVEL_STEP_PM` (20), re-issues ON on the first RUNNING frame after a restore, sends nothing while PAUSED, and the audible start must follow the first ACTIVE frame by at most `SOUND_LEAD_MAX_MS` (100 ms) on the real audio thread. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-038 | The only Filter settings exposed to Accessibility Settings are `reduced_motion_glide_pm`, `breath_depth_pm` and `lie_tick_min_ms`, each range-bound; reduced-motion scale is latched per lie at activation and at `hard_start`, and no setting may remove a leak mode. | UI | ADR-0030 + ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-039 | Filter tuning constants are validated at boot by named assertions (rate, arrival-bound, ramp-floor, flash-margin, dead-zone and similar inequalities) that fail boot with the assertion name, and a lint requires the shipped assertion list to equal the Tuning Knobs derived-constraints table. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-040 | The Filter publishes a fixed diagnostic counter set (including `lies_drawn`, `leak_repaired`, `lie_unrenderable`, `lie_collisions`, `filter_route`, `first_frame_filtered`, `render_cpu_us`) and a side-by-side `true_value`/`perceived` read for one ref, consumed only by the Debug Overlay. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-041 | The Debug Overlay, its Filter hooks and the `--filter-test-freeze-dither` flag are excluded from release builds by file or feature tag, and no accessibility option exposes them; a release-build lint asserts their absence. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-042 | Breath and sound-envelope periods are drawn per lie from the hash, kept outside registered dead zones fed by other systems' periodic signals (alarm sweep `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS`, Attention `tell_beat`, Audio fallback breath), so cross-correlation stays below `BREATH_LOCK_CEIL`. | Audio | ADR-0024 + ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-043 | The own-cone rim fringe reads the beam pose Lighting publishes for the same frame and must match it with zero art-pixel error; it adds one draw, casts no light and is absent from `emitter.state`. | Rendering | ADR-0028 + ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-044 | The Filter consumes `sanity.perception` entries of Sanity's closed SN-23 schema (including `direction`, `params.readout_mode`, `away`, `leak_modes`); a published value contradicting the kind mapping follows the published value and trips a debug assertion, and presentation never derives these from `lie_id` hash. | Data | ADR-0025 + ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-percfilter-045 | `ref_key` values at or above `0xFFFF0000` are reserved for non-subject effect vectors; the `(stable_id, surface)` to `PerceptionRef` join must reject WORN rows and surfaces outside 1 to 5, and a COMPARTMENT record without `quantity` fails closed at build time and at runtime. | Data | ADR-0024 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |

### HUD & Suit Layer (`hud`)
GDD: `design/gdd/hud-and-suit-layer.md` | 42 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-hud-001 | The HUD is a `PRESENTATION`-class kernel subscriber that owns no block, hook, command, mutator, reading key, RNG stream or `INPUT_SURFACE`, and writes only CanvasItem/Material/audio-request properties. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-002 | The HUD is a `CanvasLayer` (layer 2, group `hud`) in the root viewport, never under the 640x360 world `SubViewport`; a `Control` under the world SubViewport fails the scene-tree lint (build gate). | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-003 | HUD scale `H` is an integer in `{H_default..2*H_default}` with `H_default = max(k_auto,3)`, independent of world scale `k`; HUD code never reads `k` or assumes `H==k`; an `H` change alters no simulation value. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-004 | Frame-register nodes (plates, keycaps, prompts, bar, rail, notices) are bit-identical with the Perception Filter on or off; the only Filter-fed exception is an allow-list of mark-slot visibility properties (headless oracle: scene-state hash). | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-005 | Exactly six reading-register node families (`suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge`, scanner slot) are lie-capable and are read only via `perceived(SUIT_KEY(k), PlayerId)` / `perceived(SCANNER, PlayerId)`; HUD modules contain zero `true_value` call sites. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-006 | `perceived` reads fill a preallocated caller-owned `Perceived` record (`perceived(key, viewer, out)`); no record is cached across frames and steady state makes zero per-frame heap allocations. | Performance | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-007 | Non-reading suit data comes only from `Suit.view(PlayerId)` (+ `tool_in_hand`, `stowed_count`, `carried_instance`, `tool_charge`, `breath_src`); the four pre-wrap reading fields (`o2_pp_dkpa`, `reserve_pm`, `cell_pm`, `integrity_pm`) are never drawn. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-008 | The HUD's read interface is closed to the HS-6 table; it never reads Attention, Horror, Sanity state, Graph state at a distance, `peer_id`, `lit(ref)`, `loco_state`, `abort_cause`, `input_ignored_view`, or any `PERCEPTION_OUT` block. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-009 | Update model is hybrid pull: `perceived`, `focus_view`, `channel_view` and Controller reads are pulled every render frame; the discrete model rebuilds on dirty key `S/<PlayerId>` / `save_status`, and is force-rebuilt on first RUNNING frame, any restore, `ALL`-coalesced dirty (> `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256), exit from PAUSED, and any `H`/locale/glyph-device change. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-010 | Animation, settle, debounce, bar fill/drain and tick phases run on `present_time` (frozen while kernel PAUSED); the mark phase, honest-numeral grid and flash-arbiter window use committed tick `T` + `tick_alpha`; layer 3 uses process time; post-pause/hitch time steps clamp at `HUD_DT_CLAMP_MS` 50 and no integrator divides by dt. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-hud-011 | `HudLayout` outputs integer HUD-px rectangles via manual snapping (`anchor_snap`) at every `H`/display; `Control.scale` is (1,1), no node animates scale/rotation, `offset_transform_*` unused, and bars/arcs are rect/atlas fills, not `draw_line`/`draw_arc`; `gui_snap_controls_to_pixels` is never relied on. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-012 | Needle tips quantise to whole HUD px and the Filter never-rest rule (`NEVER_REST_MAX_S` 1.0 s) still holds (tip pixel changes at least once per 1.0 s at H 3 and 6; `L` 11 ineligible). | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-013 | The surface inventory is closed (`hud_strip`, `hud_suit_panel`, `hud_rail`, `hud_prompt`+`hud_bar`, `hud_give_up_plate`, `hud_notice`, conditional `hud_aim_indicator`, inspect-card frame, reserved anchors); forbidden surfaces (minimap, health bar, Attention/sanity/horror indicators, toast, death screen, etc.) are lint-banned under any setting. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-014 | The strip's breath dial shows `suit.o2` while `breath_src` is ROOM/GASP/NONE and `suit.reserve` (whole seconds) while RESERVE; `breath_src` also selects hue and mark eligibility and is never derived from displayed values. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-015 | Warning-mark phase is `((T mod BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS)+tick_alpha)/BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` (`BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` 128, duty `MARK_DUTY_PM` 500), leading edge at `T mod 128 == 0` within one render frame of the audio edge, one shared phase for all six mark slots. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-016 | While PAUSED an active mark is held ON solid, and on resume stays ON until the next leading edge (resume bridge); reduce-flashing/reduced-motion hold marks solid (reduce_flashing default ON). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-017 | A single flash arbiter keyed to `T` window `(T-TICK_RATE, T]` enforces `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` over all HUD luminance events: clock-locked events never delayed, hides never deferred, deferrable events wait at most `MAX_DEFER_TICKS` 60, grid boundaries are skip-only (`GRID_MAX_CONSEC_SKIPS` 1), overruns are recorded as `HUD_FLASH_OVERRUN`, never dropped, never altering the Filter's schedule. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-018 | The HUD creates no simulation state and persists nothing: no HUD state is saved, replicated or hashed; on restore all presentation-local counters (debounce, grid, bar fill/drain, tick phases, flash history, device latch) are cleared and readings draw directly at restored values with no settle. | Save/Load | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-019 | Save feedback is read-only: failed-write notice shows when Save/Load's `fail_escalated` (threshold `AUTOSAVE_FAIL_ESCALATE_COUNT` 3) is true and clears on `WRITTEN`; the HUD counts nothing; first-frame line shows for `UI7_DWELL_S` 8 after a fallback load or a `DEATH_STUB` restore (`restore_reason` read as request only). | Save/Load | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-020 | Interaction display is driven by `focus_view(seat)`, `channel_view(seat)` (local seat only), the result-event record, and Controller `confirm_view`/`glyph_view`; the bar fill is `fill_px = (hold_pm*L) div 1000` with the HUD doing no hold drain, and prompt show follows Interaction D.UI-2. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-021 | The HUD issues no commands and plays no sound (zero HUD-originated audio requests at MVP); sound-request ids exist only for dormant lie slots, and the mark clock is the shared AAV-9 grid, cited not reimplemented. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-022 | Per-frame presenter order is fixed (camera solver, Avatar Presenter, Filter, HUD, Lighting) so `world_to_hud` and `perceived` are read after their producers. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-023 | HUD performance budget on RM-DESKTOP (1920x1080) and RM-DECK (1280x800, `H` 3 over `k` 2) release export: <= 60 draw calls, <= `HUD_PRIMITIVE_MAX` 90 primitives, <= `HUD_FRAME_BUDGET_US` 400 us p95 over 600 frames, atlas+needle footprint <= `HUD_MEM_CEILING_MB` 8; provisional until spike S2. | Performance | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-024 | HUD maths (`strip_budget`, `hud_flash_budget`, `reserve_seconds`, `mark_phase`, `o2_dial_angle`, `bottom_row_fit`, `anchor_snap`, `cap_px`, `needle_frame_index`, etc.) are pure static functions assertable headlessly (e.g. `cap_px` H3..6 = 21,28,35,42; `side_avail` 149,102,73,53 at 1280). | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-025 | Dial faces are baked once per layout (not per frame) and instrument faces use bitmap/atlas assets with tabular numerals; the widget approach (Control tree vs custom draw) and Theme structure are an ADR decision. | Rendering | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-026 | Tuning data lives in external `hud_tuning` data validated at load; out-of-range knobs (e.g. `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` < 20, `HONEST_TICK_MS` != 500, `HUD_SETTLE_MS` outside 250-400, `BAR_LEN_*` not ordered S<M<L in 12-64) fail, and no gameplay-balance key may exist in it. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-027 | Nine diagnostic counters (`HUD_READ_MISSING`, `hud_perceived_oob`, `hud_unmapped_key_glyph`, `UI_MISSING_KEY`, `hud_layout_overlap`, `UI_PLACEMENT_WITHHELD`, `HUD_FLASH_OVERRUN`, `hud_plate_overflow`, `hud_unknown_override`) exist, read 0 on a clean run, and are exposed via `hud_debug_snapshot()`. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-028 | `HudSettings` hooks (opacity, reduce_flashing/flash_mode, aim_indicator, pattern strength, etc.) are consumed by the HUD but never read or written by game state; Alpha settings screens are owned elsewhere. | Data | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-029 | Prompt plates and glyphs follow the Controller's device latch and closed `keycap_token` enumeration; unknown tokens fall back to the `?` keycap and count `hud_unmapped_key_glyph`; gamepad-only play of the core loop is fully supported. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-030 | Missing upstream data degrades safely: a missing `perceived` record draws a blank numeral with arc at rest and increments `HUD_READ_MISSING`; the HUD never falls back to `view()` readings or invents a rule for an unowned *when*. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-031 | The HUD reaches Suit, Filter, Interaction, Controller, Save/Load and the kernel clock only through injectable interfaces, accepts a scripted (T, tick_alpha) pinner, and exposes a scene-state hash (ordered visible nodes with atlas region, rect, modulate, visibility) so a headless lane asserts what the render lane checks as pixels. | Core | ADR-0030 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-032 | The needle settle is a closed-form critically damped step that is frame-rate independent (6x50 ms, 18x16.7 ms and 1x300 ms agree), keeps position and velocity continuous on retarget, never overshoots, yields no NaN for any dt, and receives its dt already clamped by the caller. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-033 | Suit publishes per-axis rung states and a one-bit TRUTH-class `suit.alarm` key (lie stage pinned never, read via `perceived`); the HUD derives mark_active from them and never compares displayed values to thresholds. | Core | ADR-0027 + ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-034 | The `Perceived` record schema carries displayed value and permille, motion_mode (HONEST/GLIDE/TICK/STALE/RETURN), tick phase, leak amplitude and lie_tick_min_ms; the Filter owns the lie schedule, the HUD clamps out-of-range fields and never reschedules substitutions. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-035 | Zone and band constants (O2 band edges, HYPOXIA_PP, SUIT_HP thresholds, warmth zone edges, RESERVE_S, CELL_*) are read by name from their owners' published data with no HUD literals; a missing or non-increasing edge draws no notch, counts hud_perceived_oob and fails the data-load lint. | Data | ADR-0007 + ADR-0027 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-036 | Interaction and Controller views expose monotonic transition_seq/result_seq, ended_by, length_class, give_up_live and press_blocked_moving; the HUD edge-detects them so terminal transitions are never skipped across multi-tick render frames, and never recomputes length_class or drains hold itself. | Core | ADR-0020 + ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-037 | Plate placement uses `world_to_hud(anchor)` on the unjolted camera pose with floor snapping; a zoom step re-projects plates and recomputes bar fill but recomputes no layout, while an `H` change recomputes layout as a cut; plates are withheld, never clamped, when no placement clears. | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-038 | `HudLayout` is a pure function of (picture rect, H) over a closed `offered()` display table (720p to 2160p, ultrawide, Deck keep/expand) with per-side fit and 5% safe margin; an out-of-set pair raises a defect flag, counts hud_layout_overlap and falls back to `H_default`. | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-039 | Every HUD string resolves through `tr()` with a StringName key (a missing key renders the key and counts once per distinct key per show); plates wrap at 72 px under a +40% pseudo-locale without truncation; digit changes are atlas-region swaps with no text reshape. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-040 | The HUD is unmounted until the first committed tick, draws nothing and calls neither `view()` nor `perceived` before it or during kernel LOADING, holds its last model through DEAD and LOADING, and adds no scene node on death or restore except the record line. | Core | ADR-0001 + ADR-0030 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-hud-041 | In `gameplay` context every HUD node has `mouse_filter` IGNORE and `focus_mode` NONE, gamepad focus is never assigned to a HUD node, and the OS cursor visibility follows the active input context. | Input | ADR-0019 + ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-hud-042 | All HUD Controls use the single `ui_theme_main` Theme with no per-node colour, font or stylebox override, and shared components (keycap, plate, bezel, bracket, bar, tabular-numeral label) each exist once as a reusable scene or resource. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |

### In-World Instruments & Inspect View (`instr`)
GDD: `design/gdd/in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view.md` | 41 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-instr-001 | Instruments register zero sim blocks, hooks, mutators, commands, dirty keys or persisted fields; they are `PRESENTATION` subscribers that write only CanvasItem, Material, Light2D, particle and audio-request state. | Core | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-002 | Instruments persist and replicate nothing; all settle, tremor, dwell, numeral-throttle and cull-history scalars are discarded on restore, cull, camera cut and `hard_start`. | Save/Load | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-003 | Each on-screen instrument in a loaded section makes exactly one `perceived(ref, viewer)` call per frame, via one `InstrumentPresenter` per instrument; wall node and card are views of the frame index and digits it publishes once per frame. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-004 | Instruments never call `true_value`, `summary()`, `power_use()`, `can_join()` or `block_hash()`, never cache a `Perceived` record across frames, never address the Filter by `peer_id`, and never derive a displayed value themselves. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-005 | Non-value `read_view` inputs (tremor predicate, module frame selection, WORN, patch-plate hp, alarm eligibility, binding validity) are refreshed once per Graph step (`GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6), never per frame, and never feed a displayed value. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-006 | An instrument that was culled or whose section was unloaded snaps to `displayed_pm` on its first pull with no settle or interpolation from stale history. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-007 | Every instrument is one data record in `assets/data/instruments/` (`instrument_id`, `gauge_kind`, `size_class`, `quantity`, `value_lo/hi`, `lsb`, `unit_key`, `name_key`, `zones[]` as LS-7 band names, `lie_surface_id`s, `beyond_witness`) rendered by one scene definition at two scales (wall `k` and card `H`); a missing or malformed record yields the honest path plus `lie_unrenderable`, which is a build failure for any kind in `lie_targets.json`. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-008 | Build-time lints fail the build on: `BEYOND_WITNESS_MISMATCH`, `LIE_SURFACE_ENUM_MISMATCH`, `INSTR_QUANTITY_COLLISION` (duplicate `(subject, quantity)`), `ALARM_MARK_ON_REMOTE`, `INSTRUMENT_NO_BINDING`, `PANEL_GAUGE_VERB_CONFLICT`, a zone arc narrower than `ZONE_MIN_ARC_PX` (6), and `lsb` visibility (D.12); `NO_BEAT_C_FIXTURE` is advisory. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-009 | Zone thresholds are derived at build time from LS-7 band names and never typed into instrument data; zone bands, ticks and bezel arcs are baked sprites, never drawn at runtime. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-010 | The needle is a separate pivoted element placed on whole art pixels with NEAREST sampling, 2 art px thick; baseline is pre-rendered needle frames indexed by rounded tip pixel (about 100 on DIAL_L, 76 on DIAL_S), with the wall and card reading the same frame index; no antialiased drawn lines. | Rendering | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-011 | A needle's motion is selected solely by the Filter's `motion_mode` (HONEST, GLIDE, TICK, STALE, BREATH, RETURN); an honest gauge never glides and a lie never settles or starts a tremor; the instrument never infers a lie. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-012 | Honest motion is "step, then rest": a new honest sample ramps from `prev_honest_pm` to `target_pm` over one Graph step (100 ms) through the PR-8 rule-5 interpolator (`tick_alpha`); rest means no sample for `INSTR_REST_STEPS` (3) steps; a closed-form underdamped settle (D.4, overshoot at most 5 %, 250-400 ms) fires only on `settle_eligible` quantities (`load` at MVP) when the change accumulated in the 4-step window reaches `INSTR_SETTLE_JUMP_PM` (40). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-013 | A reversal of at most one quantum is held `INSTR_REVERSAL_DWELL_STEPS` (4) Graph steps before acceptance, so a 10 Hz dither across a quantum edge yields at most one needle move. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
