| TR-instr-014 | Honest tremor (positional, +-`TREMOR_AMP_PX`, 6-10 Hz, raised to a 2 screen px minimum at `k` 1) starts only when `motion_mode` is HONEST and a physical-stress predicate holds, and is never driven by Attention, sanity, a timer or a lie; a tremor running at lie onset fades by the Filter's `tremor_weight_pm` over `TREMOR_BLEND_S` (latched `env_hold`), while the settle triple and the reduced-motion notch clear at once. | Core | ADR-0009 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-015 | Under `reduced_motion` honest settle becomes a step and tremor (wall and card) is replaced by a held stress notch pair shown only while HONEST and the predicate holds; the alarm mark holds solid. | UI | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-016 | The card numeral is formatted integer-only from `displayed_value` per the quantity table and `lsb`; honest numerals update only on the HUD's shared `HONEST_TICK_MS` (500 ms) grid with at most `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` (3) transitions per second, showing the then-current step; lying numerals are drawn from Filter-supplied digits under `T_tick`. | UI | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-017 | The inspect card opens only from the `gameplay` context on a press while the target is lit (`lit(ref)`, Emitter State) and resolvable; it uses the same `PerceptionRef` and `Perceived` as its wall gauge, so it lies identically (no card ref, no card lie), and opening it calls no `record_falsification`. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-018 | The card closes in the same render frame, with no animation, on `lit(ref)` false, `cancel`/B, a second `inspect`, restore/`hard_start`, binding loss, leaving reach, section transit, any context change (pause included, with no restore on resume), STAGGERED/DOWNED/DEAD/IN_POD, or movement past the dead-zone (avatar moves in the same frame); the card runs in real time on `present_time` and does not pause the sim. | UI | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-019 | The card is a seat-local layer-2 node subtree on an opaque Ground plate, never replicated and not an `INPUT_SURFACE`; all card/instrument Controls have `mouse_filter` IGNORE and `focus_mode` NONE; it is placed via `world_to_hud(anchor_px)` with side chosen at open (right, left, above, below), latched, and follows its anchor per frame on the HUD grid. | UI | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-020 | The card contains only label (`name_key`), the same dial/meniscus and zones, and one numeral with `unit_key`; it never shows a state/condition word, rating, bar, reason string, lie indicator, wear or alarm mark; all text goes through `tr()` StringName keys, and a missing glyph falls back per element to the default-locale string then the `instrument_id` stencil. | UI | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-021 | Instruments are `INSTRUMENT`-class OBJECT subjects registered via Interaction's `sync_section` from Layout's `subject_anchors(section_id)`, counted inside `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET`; `N_loaded` must not exceed `INSTRUMENT_SHARE_MAX` (24 placeholder); `inspect` acts on primary focus if it is an INSTRUMENT, else on a secondary INSTRUMENT slot (provisional pending Controller/Interaction). | Core | ADR-0020 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-022 | The Module-Body Presenter selects one honest sheet frame from Graph `state`/`served_pm`, with `frame_override` swapping only to the other honest frame of the pair; SPRITE_SWAP eligibility requires an Item DB `has_both_frames(def_id, pair)` query, and both frames of every lie-capable `mach_*` sheet sit on one sheet page. | Data | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-023 | The in-world 16x16 Alarm Red mark is an untagged TRUTH node drawn only on housings physically at an `alarm_eligible` subject per the IW-19 decision table (never on DIAL_S, GLYPH, FRAME, the card or REMOTE instruments), uses the descriptor's `alarm_eligible` flag and never re-derives it from `sev`, and pulses no faster than 3 Hz. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-024 | The gleam is drawn from `leak_amplitude_pm` as a runtime material parameter on glass/needle/housing edges only (never baked, never a fill, peak channel at most `LEAK_MAX_CH` 0.85, hue fixed under coloured light), at zero added ShaderMaterials (LO-13 budget of five); fallback is a child edge sprite per lying instrument if per-instance shader parameters are unavailable. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-025 | World gauge faces are not gated in code on `lit(ref)` (the lit renderer/HullSurface makes unlit faces dark); only the inspect prompt and card are gated on `lit(ref)` (hysteresis `LIT_ON_PM` 150 / `LIT_OFF_PM` 120); the housing pip is on the emissive layer (mask bit 2), exists only while `served_pm` > 0, casts no light and is corruptible via `HOUSING_GLOW(ref)`. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-026 | Performance budget: for 20 on-screen instruments (4 lying) the presenter pass costs median at most 0.25 ms and p99 at most 0.5 ms on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release builds (proposal, pending technical-director acceptance); per-frame work is O(1) per instrument; draw calls are at most 2 per instrument plus 1 per lying instrument on the gleam fallback; `perceived()` calls equal the on-screen instrument count. | Performance | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-027 | The `load` reading must be a Graph-published `load_pm` `true_value` kind (0 when `supply` is 0, else `min(1250, floor(1000*demand/supply))`, quantum `LOAD_QUANTUM_PERMILLE` 20, separate dead/idle/overload cases); Instruments never derive it, and until granted `inst_engine_bus_load` is not buildable as a lie target. | Data | ADR-0011 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-028 | Lie-eligibility floors are lints over data and placements: L-2I per seat and direction (fixed same-loop gauge, scanner, or suit for ALARM-direction only), at least 3 lie-eligible surfaces per site (counted as a range by scanner availability), a consequence-channel witness within `WITNESS_HOPS_MAX` 2 sharing no node, and no decorative gauge (`beyond_witness` non-empty). | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-029 | Hard never-do list: no schematic/overlay/minimap/ship-status/loop-summary screen, no numerals or text on a world gauge, no closed progress ring, no condition word/bar, no Attention/threat/proximity indicator on any instrument, no sanity/lie counter, and a scene lint asserts no node outside an instrument or the card renders Graph state at a distance. | UI | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-030 | A binding to a DESTROYED, SALVAGED, ABSENT or unresolvable node shows a truth-graded DEAD frame (hairline crack, snapped 4-px needle stub) with no prompt, card, gleam, tremor or pip; a missing Filter datum falls back to the honest path. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-031 | In development builds only, each instrument exposes read-only `drawn_value(ref)` and `instrument_diag(ref)`, publishes a stable integer `ref_key`, keeps counters `diag.instr_pm_clamped`, `diag.inspect_unresolved`, `diag.instr_quantity_collision`, `diag.gleam_over_cap`, and registers one Debug Overlay channel (DO-12); all stripped from release builds and never exposing `true_value`. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-032 | `inspect` is not a kernel command; the `inspect_card` input context sends `set_move_intent {NONE}` on entry, and movement past `MOVE_DEADZONE` (plus `CARD_PAD_DEADZONE_MARGIN` on a pad) closes the card and submits the first `set_move_intent` in that same frame. A direction key held at open is ignored until released, `set_aim` stays live, and the cursor stays hidden and inert. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-033 | Card open latency: a press on a lit target draws the card within 2 render frames (next frame nominal) at any tick after focus or close, with no dropped press. The prompt appears after the HUD-owned `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS`, read by name and never hard-coded, and the debounce delays only the plate. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-034 | Tremor frequencies and phases derive from a hash of the stable integer `ref_key`, never from `rng_derive` or any wall-clock or unseeded source, so a given instrument shows the same offset sequence every run and across a restore, and different instruments differ. | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-035 | The `Perceived` record carries the native-unit `displayed_value` alongside the permille triple. The card numeral is built from the native value, never rebuilt from the lossy pm triple, and uses an explicit `floor_div` helper because native GDScript integer division truncates toward zero. | Data | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-036 | Instruments request no sound directly; they publish only `card_opened` and `card_closed` once per transition, plus a single `UI_INSTRUMENT_CARD_UNLATCH` request on light-loss close. Lying-gauge sound comes only from the Filter, and Audio's hum-pitch sign must track the needle, machine frame and lamp values. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-037 | Instrument and overlay sheets (`mach_*`, `ui_*`, `ovl_*`) are lossless palette-locked PNG with binary alpha and NEAREST filtering. They carry the required named slices (`glass`, `needle_edge`, `needle`) and state-token sidecar vocabulary, and a build lint scans reserved hue bands. | Rendering | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-038 | Each instrument placement record carries a valid `instrument_id`, `node_ref` or `room_key` plus `quantity`, `mount` in {WALL_FACE, MACHINE_PANEL} and `host_face`. Lints forbid DIAL_L on pipe runs, restrict DIAL_S to terminal fittings or panels, and make a panel-mounted gauge beside a verb target an error while the secondary-slot question is open. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-039 | Card, alarm mark, housing/glyph gleam, notch placement and panel-only dials are each independently switchable by a data flag; disabling one leaves the inspect binding dormant and changes only a pre-declared set of other criteria, verified by a test-owned per-flag matrix. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-040 | Only presentation nodes with a `lie_surface_id` in the corruptible set are bound to a `PerceptionRef`; anchor and TRUTH nodes (housing, bands, ticks, alarm mark, DEAD frame, notch, card frame, label) carry no ref and no tag, enforced by a scene lint. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-instr-041 | Card plate size and placement are a pure function of display size, `k` and `H`: `H_card` is an integer HUD scale capped by the display, plates snap to the HUD art-pixel grid, candidate rects are rejected on overlap, and a resize or zoom re-projects without closing the card, with side changes as cuts. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Chart Table (Web Map & Menus) (`charttable`)
GDD: `design/gdd/chart-table-web-map-and-menus.md` | 38 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-charttable-001 | The chart table is one layer-3 `CanvasLayer` in the root viewport, opened only by the `chart_table` action from the `gameplay` context, and is never wrapped by the Perception Filter or Sanity (pixel-identical at sanity stage 0 and forced stage 3 apart from the world beneath). | Rendering | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-002 | The layer registers no state block, hook, command or mutator, submits zero commands at MVP, and holds only host-local state (`ChartPick`, `PauseGate`, `SettingsStore`, authored layout) that is never in a snapshot. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-003 | `PauseGate` is the single adapter to the kernel control plane (`request_pause`, `request_resume`, `request_shutdown`): SOLO pauses on open and resumes on close; OVERLAY (VS-B) makes no kernel call; no chart/pick/panel/menu rule may read `kernel.state == PAUSED` except `PauseGate.can_save_quit`. | Core | ADR-0030 + ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0030) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-charttable-004 | `PauseGate` re-issues the latest desired pause state until the kernel matches, so a same-boundary `request_pause`/`request_resume` pair leaves no wedged half-open state. | Core | ADR-0030 + ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0030) | ⚠️ Partial (Proposed) |
| TR-charttable-005 | Layer 3 handles input and animates on its own UI clock (render delta, deltas clamped to `UI_DT_MAX_MS`) independent of kernel state, `present_time` and `SceneTree.paused`; hold timers and filament drift accumulators persist across opens. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-charttable-006 | A layer raise (panel open, console-press chart open, Chart from panel, message plates) arriving less than `CHART_REOPEN_MIN_MS` (334 ms; 1000 ms under reduce flashing) after the last lower is queued in a single latched cancellable slot and applied together with its `request_pause`; lowers/closes are never delayed. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-007 | `ChartPick` is NONE or one `derelict_id`, host-local, never persisted, never a field of `web.state`/`web.view`, reset on any `request_restore`, on arrival, or when the picked record leaves the legal-target mirror, and frozen while `transit_state != IDLE`. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-008 | The legal-target mirror is computed purely from `web.view` for selectability only (empty while any record is `berthed` and not ABSORBED; never reflects fuel/range/busy refusals) and is not a second copy of WM-9's reason order. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-009 | The pod console press is the only simulation write; the Input Adapter's seam injects `ChartPick` into `pod_hop{target_id}` / `berth{derelict_id}` per the R1-R8 verb-resolution table, routes a no-pick press to "open chart", and `unberth{}` never needs a pick. | Core | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-010 | The layer reads no input device: all input arrives as the Adapter-owned MenuInput event stream (NAV_DOWN/UP, ACCEPT_DOWN/UP, BACK, CLOSE, FOCUS_OUT/IN, DEVICE_CHANGED, POINTER), delivered on render frames at frame start; mouse buttons never produce ACCEPT. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-011 | Every layer-3 `Control` is created with `focus_mode = FOCUS_NONE` and `mouse_filter = MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE`; the layer tracks a single retained focus id itself and the bracket is drawn from keyboard/gamepad focus only. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-012 | The Adapter latches the closing activation, key echo and the open press's release/repeat so no `interact` edge reaches gameplay or the layer until a fresh press, protecting the irreversible `pod_hop`; focus loss aborts any hold fill. | Input | ADR-0019 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-013 | Chart layout is authored data (`assets/data/ui/chart_layout.json`, path provisional): per-`derelict_id` art-px positions in a fixed 288x162 `CHART_FRAME`, `home_attach` offsets, ring radii, filament refs and ordinals; nothing is derived from `world_slot`, leg data, ticks or distance; a missing position is a content-lint failure with a deterministic runtime fallback. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-014 | The chart draws `web.view` only (nodes, `pod_at`, `transit_state`, `leg_target_id`/`leg_origin_id`/`leg_dir`) and never calls `web.leg_timing()`; while `transit_state != IDLE` it is view-only and the in-flight route is drawn only from `leg_*` fields, never from `ChartPick`. | Core | ADR-0026 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-015 | Hard never-show rules: no Attention, horror, sanity, loot value, salvage count, fuel, time, ETA, danger, "recommended", distance or name label on any chart/panel/menu surface under any setting, and no save decoration (autosave mark, thumbnail, spinner, "progress saved"). | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-016 | Every layer-3 rectangle is positioned by anchors 0/1 plus integer HUD-px offsets (panel width `floor(canvas_w/3)`), valid at every HUD canvas `picture/H` with `H` in `[H_default, 2*H_default]`, `H_default = max(k,3)`; chart multiplier `m < 1` switches to the compact vertical list. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-017 | Routes, seams and rule lines are 1-art-px quads aligned to whole art px (no width-1 line draws on fractional transforms); filament drift moves in whole-art-px steps from an authored irregular interval table (>= 1 s) and stops under reduced motion. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-018 | The `SettingsStore` is a host-local per-install store of four comfort values (display mode, reduced motion, reduce flashing, master volume) plus `notice_seen`, not a kernel block and not WORLD/PROFILE scope; the safety toggles and `notice_seen` are written synchronously, display mode and volume debounced (`SETTINGS_WRITE_DEBOUNCE_MS`) and flushed on close/quit/exit; first-launch default is reduce flashing ON. | Save/Load | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-019 | The `SettingsStore` exposes a read interface and presentation-only change signal to consumers (Lighting, Filter, Audio, Camera); a missing/unreadable store is treated as first launch and re-shows the photosensitivity notice; a write failure shows one ship-log line once per process. | Save/Load | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-020 | Save & Quit disables items and presents one frame, then calls Save/Load `save_and_quit()` which must return promptly (`REFUSED_DOWNED`/`STARTED`) and report via `save_status`/`quit_held`/`quit_write_active` without blocking the frame loop; the layer holds no write timer and calls `cancel_held_close()` once on return to play. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-021 | Boot-flow calls into Save/Load (`new_game(world_seed)`, `continue_campaign()`, `retire_campaign`, `delete_campaign`, `campaign_info()`, `list_saves()`) follow a load sequence: items disabled and plate removed, layer-1 black presented (>= `t_hold_pad` 120 ms), then the blocking call, then a camera CUT on the first RUNNING frame; nothing is drawn between. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-022 | Destructive actions (UI-3 quit-without-saving, UI-5 Retire/Delete) use a linear hold-to-confirm gesture on the UI clock reading the action's effective duration (`CONFIRM_HOLD_S`, `DELETE_HOLD_S` = 2x), firing on the first frame the threshold is reached and aborting on release/cancel/chart_table/pointer exit/focus loss. | Input | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-023 | FAULTED in release returns to the boot-flow main menu with Continue focused (the kernel never overwrites the existing save). | Core | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 | 🟡 Covered (Proposed) |
| TR-charttable-024 | All user-visible strings are keys in a closed `ct.*` table via `tr()` with fit classes (`LABEL_1`/`LABEL_2`/`PARAGRAPH`), lint-measured with the real faces at every canvas row, never truncated; instrument face (bitmap, integer H) versus reading face (vector, continuous scale). | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-025 | The layer plays no sound itself; it requests UI-bus cues (focus-move, confirm, `UI_CHART_REOPEN_QUEUED`) from Adaptive Audio and publishes the master-volume step through the `SettingsStore`. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-026 | Every focusable chart node is a `Control` carrying an accessible name equal to its neutral ordinal (nothing drawn), and every other control's accessible name is its visible `ct.*` label; the compact list uses a stable authored order with whole-key `ct.compact.slot_<n>` ordinals. | UI | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-027 | Layer-3 draw calls stay within `LAYER3_DRAW_CALL_BUDGET` (default 40; estimate ~26 on the MVP chart, max 33), asserted at load and measured via `Viewport.get_render_info`; layer 2 (HUD) is hidden under a full-bleed plate. | Performance | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-028 | The chart table layer is an `INPUT_SURFACE` subscriber class, reads only `web.view`, `SaveInfo`, `save_status`, `restore_reason`, `campaign_info()`/`list_saves()` and its own host-local state, and rebuilds its state machine from CLOSED on any `request_restore`. | Core | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-029 | Rate of large-plate raises is limited so the registry `flash_count` stays `F <= 3` (<= 2 under reduce flashing) on the layer-3 rectangle; verified by a per-frame linear-luminance capture. | Rendering | ADR-0028 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-030 | Display & Comfort changes apply immediately (no Apply/revert timer), read back the applied display mode, and recompute `k`, `H_default`, canvas and panel width live while retaining focus by item id. | UI | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-031 | The layer state machine is data-driven from `assets/data/ui/chart_table_transitions.json`, diffed at build against a fixture of every authored (from, trigger, to) triple; every kernel `request_*` and Save/Load API call is bound to exactly the edges that name it, and every state has a path to CLOSED or a boot exit. | Data | ADR-0030 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-032 | Every layer-owned tuning knob is read from a config resource with a registry entry of identical name, never a script literal; out-of-range values and violated coupled constraints (C-1..C-10) are clamped with a log line or refused at load, and derived knobs are computed, not settable. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-033 | Layer-3 script time per frame stays within a measured budget (placeholder 0.5 ms, p95 over 600 frames on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release exports); opening a surface costs one presented frame, the layout file is parsed at boot, and a `SettingsStore` flush completes inside one frame. | Performance | ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-034 | An OS window-close request is handled by Save/Load, not the layer; the layer reacts to `quit_write_active` and `save_status`/`quit_held` from any state including CLOSED: it enters `SAVE_QUIT_WAIT` with nothing drawn from CLOSED, raises the panel with one `request_pause` on FAILED, and shows `UI3_PLATE` on `SL_QUIT_NO_WRITE_DOWNED`. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0030 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-035 | Boot-flow screens (notice, main menu, UI-5, UI-7/8/9, load hold) run while the kernel is not RUNNING with no session: they make no `request_pause`, reference no Sanity, Filter or Attention symbol, are not children of the Filter viewport, and Quit from any of them calls only `request_shutdown`. | Core | ADR-0030 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-charttable-036 | Adapter `POINTER` events carry a position in a defined unit (canvas or window px) mapped to HUD and art px under the `canvas_items` stretch, with picture top-left mapping to HUD (0,0); the layer publishes a cursor-hint request on hover; the unit is verified by a 4.7.2 spike. | Input | ADR-0019 + ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-037 | On fractional HUD canvases (e.g. 426.67 x 266.67 at `H` 3) every bitmap glyph, bezel, seam and route edge lies on a whole screen-pixel boundary, 1-art-px strokes are exactly `H` screen px on every side, and the panel left edge is an integer screen px, verified on the real display path. | Rendering | ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-charttable-038 | The layer takes all neighbours (view, kernel control plane, Save/Load, clock, store, window, audio, MenuInput) by injection and exposes a per-frame draw list, forced-`H`, frame-capture and `force_stage` hooks as data, with contract tests keeping doubles schema-equal to the real interfaces; render-capture checks run on a pinned non-headless CI runner. | Core | ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Adaptive Audio & Tells (`audio`)
GDD: `design/gdd/adaptive-audio-and-tells.md` | 43 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-audio-001 | Audio registers no hook, state block, mutator, command or `push_stimulus`; it is a `PRESENTATION` subscriber that reads `SIM` and `PERCEPTION_OUT` views and writes audio nodes only, and muting any bus leaves `state_hash` unchanged. | Core | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-002 | Audio persists nothing and is not hashed or replicated; its presentation memory (`(tell_epoch, tell_beat)`, `phase`, `transition_seq`, `result_seq`, `last_result.T`, shared `last_alarm_T`) is rebuilt from published state after any restore. | Save/Load | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-003 | Every cue is the output of one pure, headless-testable function `cue_requests(published_state, presentation_memory) -> [cue_request]`; the `cue_request` record carries cue_id, family, tier, kind, anchor, transmission, gain/pitch/rate, start_T, seek, listener_scope, leak_flags. | Core | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-004 | P5 notification handlers only record into a mailbox (idempotent, no cue creation or `play()`); one frame poll per render frame, ordered after the last sim step and before the presenter draw, classifies net change and creates all requests. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-005 | Cue dedupe uses last-seen counters set to the observed value (never seen+1): at most one beat per poll, no catch-up burst after hitch, pause or restore. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-006 | No timer exists in Audio: no `Tween`, `Timer`, `create_tween` or wall-clock call drives a world voice; all world-voice envelopes (`volume_db`, `pitch_scale`) are pure functions of sim `T` (+ render alpha) written in the frame poll, clamped at -80 dB. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-007 | Pause sets `stream_paused` on all world voices while the kernel is PAUSED (UI and Voice voices continue, `SceneTree.paused` not used); audio never causes a tick and never pauses the sim. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-008 | On restore, one-shot cues never replay and last-seen values are primed from restored state; sustained cues resume at steady level with `DECLICK_TICKS`, the tell bed fades in over `RESTORE_FADE_TICKS`, an active lie's leak resumes at full level on frame one, REACH lead-in resumes with `seek`. | Save/Load | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-009 | Death and save play no sound; `restore_reason` is never read by Audio. | Save/Load | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-010 | Exactly one audio family (`hal`) is PERCEIVED; all other families are true at every sanity stage, never suppressed, delayed or synthesised by the Filter or Sanity; audio never reads `sanity.perception`. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-011 | A master identity table binds each asset (name `sfx_<token>_<context>_<name>_<variant>`) to exactly one family token and one bus; a build-time lint over `assets/data/audio/tell_audio.json` and the asset dir proves families disjoint and `hal` ids equal to the Filter's `FILTER_AUDIO_CLASSES`. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-012 | All levels, radii, intervals, loudness and class tables live in `assets/data/audio/tell_audio.json` (and sibling files), never in code; assets 48 kHz, mono for positional, OGG for loops/beds and sounds over `PCM_MAX_TICKS`, 16-bit PCM for short one-shots, all preloaded at boot. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-013 | Tell onset carries beat 0 on one reserved impulse voice, and each strictly-greater `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` yields exactly one unpitched beat with no suppression before `breach_due_T`; audio starts no later than the visual twin by call order, not absolute latency. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-014 | During `phase == TELL` a non-positional, pool-exempt structure-borne bed plays for every living player, never attenuated by distance, transmission, zoom, camera or room, at least `TELL_AUDIBILITY_MARGIN_DB` above the loudest concurrent non-tell bed per band. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-015 | Tell tier layering is discrete (three tiers, `TIER_XFADE_TICKS` crossfade), no audible parameter varies continuously with `tell_elapsed` or `level_pm`, and `level_pm` is used only as the argument of `ambient_cue_step`; Audio reads `phase` never `breach_phase`. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-016 | Alarm clock: breach entry is pulse 0 (always emitted); later pulses one per `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` on ALIGNED or FALLBACK grid, at most one `attn` and one `ann` start per tick, gap >= `ALARM_MIN_GAP_TICKS` against `last_alarm_T`, branch re-evaluated only at pulse boundaries, duty capped by `ALARM_DUTY_MAX_PM`. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-017 | Pre-tell creaks are a stateless predicate over the shared presentation hash (`HASH_SEED`, never `rng_derive`), scan capped at `CREAK_SCAN_MAX_TICKS`, primed to current `T` on restore/resume, positioned in the listener's own section, four discrete density steps only in CALM. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-018 | Abort plays a release cue and decays the bed over `RELEASE_TICKS`; `release_cue_relation` (`RELEASE_TICKS < ABORT_HOLD_TICKS` and `STRAND_RELEASE_TICKS == RELEASE_TICKS`) is a boot error if false; CALM and subside never map to an all-clear. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-019 | Witness (`wit`) cues exist iff a live Graph descriptor has `acoustic != NONE` and `medium != NONE`, positioned at the descriptor anchor with level/rate from `intensity_pm`/`sev`; eight classes only; the annunciator (`ann`) is reachable only from `alarm_eligible` descriptors. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-020 | Running loops (`run`) are read from `node()`/`loop_component()` and woken by `N/`, `L/`, `C/`, `B/`, `F/`, `E/` dirty keys; smooth values (`served_pm`, `intensity_pm`) are polled at render rate. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-021 | Listener is the avatar position (never the camera, lead or zoom); gain is the manual D.9 value written per voice (built-in 2D attenuation disabled); transmission attenuates but never culls a `sev >= 1` witness; an unknown door state is treated as open. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-022 | Fixed voice pool (`VOICE_POOL_SIZE` 32 placeholder) with `RESERVED_TELL_IMPULSE_VOICES` (3) never lent out, pool-exempt tell bed and INTEGRITY room tone, deterministic stealing order (tier, quietest, oldest), tell and `hal` voices protected, named DIAGNOSTIC on any tier-1 steal. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-023 | Mix priority is five tiers; ducking is applied per voice (not by bus effect/sidechain) only to tier >= 4 beds; no compressor, limiter or sidechain may attenuate a tier-1 cue; `hal` cues never duck, delay or mask a true cue. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-024 | `hal` requests are `OWNER_PRIVATE` per seat, driven only by the Filter audio request `(viewer, ref/position, intent {ON, LEVEL(pm), OFF}, class)`, carry an audible leak with restore-exact `(T_s, floor)` breathing, and are never stolen while lies <= `LIES_MAX`. | Audio | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-025 | Hull anchor of `locus_section_id` is derived by Audio from Layout's `plate_positions` composed with `section_offset` and the body transform (D.16); sources on moving bodies stay attached to ship-local anchors. | Physics | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-026 | Cue delivery for Horror consumes `horror.view` (`strike_T`, `reach_end_T`, ingress/egress events); the REACH resolve is a sustained-with-seek cue evaluated as a function of `T`, and the strike cue never replays on restore. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-027 | Settings: one World slider covers all in-world families; no setting lowers the tell bed below its margin, strips a `hal` leak, sonifies Attention level, or is affected by reduce-flashing/reduce-motion; the only tactile exceptions are Suit own-body cues and the opt-in off-by-default `haptic_beat_pulse` (fixed duration/strength, same edge rules as the beat). | Input | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-028 | Captions policy: `wit`, `ann`, `run`, `suit`, foley, three interaction terminals and UI are captionable; tell, breach/alarm, pre-tell creaks, Horror, `hal` and the P1 cue are never captioned. | UI | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-029 | Audio main-thread cost (poll, `cue_requests`, node updates) is at most `AUDIO_FRAME_BUDGET_US` (500 placeholder) at p95 on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release export, with flat `OBJECT_COUNT` and `MEMORY_STATIC` after a 300-frame warm-up. | Performance | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-030 | Audio never reads Attention internals beyond the published `attention.view`; nothing in Attention or the Graph references Audio symbols (no `AudioServer`/`AudioStreamPlayer*`/bus in Attention source). | Core | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-031 | A Sprint-0 request-capture harness runs `cue_requests` headless on the dummy audio driver and returns the request list per poll, and a development-build-only `AudioEffectCapture` test tap bus fed from the shipped Master pre-fader supports loopback-free level measurement; neither exists in release exports. | Audio | ADR-0029 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-032 | Every cross-knob data relation (D.6 bed margin, D.8 radius and floor budget, D.10 pool sizes, D.11 reach pre-roll, D.13 creak density, D.19 audibility and peak relations, D.9 log-law radius) is checked at data load; hard relations refuse boot, soft relations log a DIAGNOSTIC, and a DIAGNOSTIC failing at shipped defaults counts as a failure. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-033 | Output latency is unobservable from the engine, so audio-versus-screen delta and footfall-to-contact-frame offset are measured by loopback on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK release exports, and `AUDIO_LEAD_MS` is set from the measured p95 only if negative; the rules claim call-order, never absolute latency. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-034 | Positional audio uses `AudioStreamPlayer2D` with a current `AudioListener2D` on the avatar inside the 640x360 world SubViewport, built-in attenuation disabled, `max_distance` at least 1218 px, pan matching the D.9 world-pixel model within 0.05 at every integer zoom, else a manual-pan fallback arm; 2-tick de-click is verified at 60 Hz tick and 144 Hz render. | Audio | ADR-0029 + ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-035 | When one poll holds several simultaneous facts, requests are created in a fixed order (annunciator, strike, BREACH_ENTER), each on a free or reserved voice with none dropped, and every audio request frame is at or before the first visual draw frame of the same event. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0029 + ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-audio-036 | Every dirty key, view field and event name that Audio consumes must match the owning system's published name exactly, and an unknown name fails boot; Audio registers no subscription to a name absent from the owner's inventory. | Core | ADR-0004 + ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-037 | The alarm clock mode (ALIGNED or FALLBACK) is chosen at `breach_enter_T` + 14 from a debounced `beacon_live_f` (Emitter BEACON on the home-ship body with `output_pm` > 0 held `BEACON_LIVE_HOLD_TICKS`, listener on that body), changes only at pulse boundaries, and is immune to read toggling. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0017 + ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-038 | Footfalls are driven only by published odometer, gait, octant and facing, never by a Presenter `anim_counter`; `FOOT_CONTACT_FRAMES` is exported from the character sidecar, not coded; restore primes the counter and a catch-up poll fires at most one footfall. | Core | ADR-0019 + ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-039 | An output-device loss and return emits one `audio_output_lost` DIAGNOSTIC per loss, replays no one-shot that fell inside the loss, resumes sustained cues at steady level with de-click, and leaves `state_hash` unchanged. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-040 | The Master bus peak stays below -3 dBFS in the worst-case scene over a 10,000-frame soak (`master_peak_warn` is 0), and the total shipped audio asset size is under `AUDIO_ASSET_BUDGET_MB` with the `wit` asset count capped at `WIT_MVP_ASSET_CAP`. | Performance | ADR-0029 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-041 | The bus tree maps each family to exactly one bus, the `Voice` bus carries only `vox` and has no DSP, no sanity, stage or Filter input and no duck from a `hal` cue, and no `Area2D` `audio_bus_override` is used for routing. | Audio | ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-042 | No presenter or shader symbol reads Audio, and no visual parameter references an audio level, bus meter, cue start or voice state, so the visual twin never depends on audio and audio-driven flashes, vignettes or camera nudges cannot exist. | Rendering | ADR-0009 + ADR-0029 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-audio-043 | In co-op, each client derives its own cues from replicated published state: peer-seat P1 cues use `listener_scope` ALL_EXCEPT_SOURCE and trigger on `tell_on`, no bed request is addressed to a dead seat, and a source seat never hears its own cue; the contract is dormant at MVP. | Networking | ADR-0032 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Save/Load & Versioned Format (`saveload`)
GDD: `design/gdd/save-load-and-versioned-format.md` | 42 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-saveload-001 | A save file is one generation laid out as 8-byte magic (7-byte ASCII prefix + container-layout id byte), u32 LE `header_len`, UTF-8 JSON header, concatenated block payloads, and a 32-byte SHA-256 trailer over all preceding bytes. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-002 | `header_len` is validated (`0 < header_len <= MAX_HEADER_BYTES` and `12 + header_len + 32 <= file size`) before any allocation, and file/block size caps (`MAX_SAVE_BYTES` 64 MiB, `MAX_BLOCK_BYTES` 32 MiB) are enforced before decode. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-003 | Block payloads are encoded with `var_to_bytes(dict)` (never `var_to_bytes_with_objects`/`bytes_to_var_with_objects`) or the owner's canonical byte form, and decode is bounded by `MAX_DECODE_DEPTH` 16, `MAX_DECODE_NODES` 250 000 and `MAX_JSON_DEPTH` 8. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-004 | Persisted state serialises data models only, never scene trees or Node references. | Data | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-005 | Saves are kept as a rolling ring of `AUTOSAVE_GENERATIONS` (3) complete self-verifying files whose order is the header `generation` number, never file name, mtime or tick `T`. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-006 | A write goes to a `.tmp`, is flushed, re-read and re-hashed, then the oldest position is renamed to `.bak`, `.tmp` renamed in, verified again, `.bak` deleted and `campaign.json` rewritten; an existing generation is never overwritten in place nor deleted before its successor verified. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-007 | The file write runs on a worker thread that receives only immutable per-block `PackedByteArray`s, paths and integers (no kernel, block, Node or scene-touching Callable), and the main thread adds only encode plus hand-off bounded by `SAVE_ENCODE_BUDGET_MS` 4 ms. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-008 | Every `store_*` return value is checked; any false return, open error, hash mismatch or rename failure leaves all previous generations untouched, sets `save_status` FAILED and retries with bounded exponential backoff (`AUTOSAVE_FAIL_BACKOFF_MAX_TICKS` 4 800), escalating after `AUTOSAVE_FAIL_ESCALATE_COUNT` 3 failures. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-009 | Capture is synchronous inside the kernel `SAVING` state at a tick boundary and no write ever begins while the kernel is `FAULTED`; a `FAULTED` capture leaves existing files untouched. | Core | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-010 | Autosave fires on a sim-time interval (`AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS` 10 800 measured from `kernel.clock.T`) and on `EVENT_TRIGGERS` (`ASSEMBLY_WELD`, `ASSEMBLY_CUT`, `SALVAGE_CUT`, plus `QUIT` and `NEW_GAME`), with a minimum gap `AUTOSAVE_MIN_GAP_TICKS` 1 800 and an event whose owner read is absent never firing. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-011 | A due autosave is held by the gate (re-evaluated every `AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS` 300, capped at `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` 7 200 then tagged RISKY) while any seat is `DOWNED`, within the post-DOWNED refractory, a suit is CRITICAL or worse, or an owed warning is open; the gate shows no per-write glyph or sound. | Core | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-012 | Save & Quit takes a `QUIT` snapshot while `PAUSED` (`PAUSED -> SAVING -> PAUSED`, `T` unchanged), calls `request_shutdown` only after the write verifies, is never deferred (bypasses soft gates only), and times out at `SAVE_QUIT_TIMEOUT_MS` 3 000. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-013 | The death stub (crew death = every bound seat `DEAD`) restarts through the kernel `request_restore` from the newest non-demoted generation passing the pre-check, with fallback newest-first within `LOAD_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, a fairness step-back, and `restore_reason = DEATH_STUB`. | Core | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-014 | The load pre-check is a pure function of (bytes, registry view, build constants) with no clock or file handle, runs entirely before the kernel is touched so a bad file never produces `FAULTED`, and is unit-testable over in-memory byte arrays. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-015 | Verification has a file stage (trailer SHA-256, every `payload_sha256`, structure) before the kernel is touched and a reproduction stage (kernel K-4 `state_hash` compare) after the last `post_restore()` and before the first `RUNNING` tick; a reproduction mismatch is never a fallback reason. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-016 | Versioning has two levels: container `format_version` migrated by Save/Load, and per-block `schema_version` migrated only by the owner inside `restore(data, stored_version)` as a chain of pure functions; Save/Load never rewrites block data. | Data | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-017 | Every persisted block meets the authoring contract: registers with the kernel (never calls Save/Load directly), declares `supported_versions`, `persist_scope`, `restore_after`, returns plain data with no object refs, and uses numeric values below 2^53 (S-1). | Data | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-018 | Save/Load registers zero state blocks and zero step hooks; its ring bookkeeping lives in `campaign.json` so it cannot roll back with the world. | Core | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-019 | The kernel snapshot is split by `persist_scope` at write time: WORLD blocks go to the generation, PROFILE blocks go to `profile.sav` (same container, `file_kind = PROFILE`, no `state_hash`), and PROFILE data survives an in-session world restore. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-020 | Per-slot `restore_epoch` is stored in `slot_epoch.bin` (u32 + CRC32), made durable before `request_restore`, and is the carrier by which threat draws re-key per restore while physical counters and seeds replay exactly. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-021 | Save/Load never reads, derives, stores or varies by perception: no `PERCEPTION_OUT` block is written (boot assertion and pre-check refuse one), and no thumbnail or "what the player saw" field exists. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-022 | `new_game(world_seed)` creates `user://saves/<campaign_id>/`, a random `campaign_id` and `slot_epoch.bin` = 0, then calls kernel `request_new_game(world_seed, restore_epoch = 0)` and writes the initial `NEW_GAME` generation. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-023 | Save/Load publishes only host-local reads (`save_status` in IDLE/WRITING/WRITTEN/FAILED, `fail_escalated`, `SaveInfo`, `restore_reason`, `quit_held`, `quit_write_active`) and calls (`cancel_held_close()`); it registers no commands and no `save_now` or `load(generation)` outside dev builds. | Core | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-024 | Boot asserts the tuning-knob invariants against the data file (e.g. retry <= max_defer/4, min_gap < interval, `LOAD_MAX_ATTEMPTS` >= generations), and gameplay/timing values are data-driven. | Data | ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-025 | Loading completes within `LOADING_TARGET_MS` (1 000 ms provisional) and a save stays below `SAVE_SIZE_ALARM_BYTES` 2 MiB, with the main-thread hitch bounded by `SAVE_HITCH_BUDGET_MS` / `DEAD_TICK_HITCH_MS` placeholders. | Performance | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-026 | Bad generations are demoted (`.demoted`) or quarantined after `QUARANTINE_AFTER_FAILURES` 2 restore failures, rename/read failures retry (`RENAME_RETRY_COUNT` 3, `READ_RETRY_COUNT` 3), and abandoned worker jobs are capped at `MAX_LIVE_ABANDONED_JOBS` 2. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-027 | The first `RUNNING` frame after a load is presented through the Perception pipeline (no clean frame) and the camera `CUT`s on every `LOADING -> RUNNING` transition; camera state is never saved. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-028 | A load that hits an INCOMPATIBLE reason (newer format, unknown block, unsupported schema, tick-rate mismatch, unknown layout) stops without loading or moving any older generation, puts the campaign in READ_ONLY so no generation is written, and a second process finding the campaign lock held also opens READ_ONLY. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-029 | Crash recovery on campaign open resolves leftover `.tmp` and `.bak` files by generation tiebreak: a complete `.tmp` newer than every ring generation is rolled forward, an older one is deleted, and a file that cannot be proven obsolete is never deleted. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-030 | Each write job carries a `write_epoch` in its `.tmp` name and is abandoned on timeout (`WRITE_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS` 30 000, `DEATH_STUB_WRITE_WAIT_MS` 5 000) or when a restore advances the epoch; a late finisher is quarantined as STALE_EPOCH, and the death stub never blocks or joins on the writer. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-031 | The newest verified generation is kept as a decoded hot RAM copy, and the likely step-back candidate is pre-staged on the worker when a seat goes DOWNED, so the DEAD-tick boundary performs zero file reads and zero main-thread decode and stays within `DEAD_TICK_HITCH_MS`. | Performance | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-032 | Death-stub restore advances `restore_epoch` and makes it durable before `request_restore`, whereas Continue of the newest QUIT generation leaves it unchanged; a missing or bad-CRC `slot_epoch.bin` is rebuilt from the newest verified header copy, and the value saturates at 2^32 - 1. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-033 | Every owner `restore()` returns `(ok, reason, detail)` with `reason` from its declared closed `<BLOCK>_RESTORE_*` set, never clamps out-of-range data, and leaves the live block byte-identical on failure; a non-conforming return or an owner failure during LOADING sends the kernel to FAULTED with no in-line retry and a persisted per-generation failure counter. | Core | ADR-0003 + ADR-0006 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-saveload-034 | Every persisted block satisfies restore idempotence (`snapshot -> encode -> decode -> restore -> snapshot` yields equal canonical bytes) and supplies a fixture factory and `default_state()`; blocks that cannot yet meet it sit on an interim `REBASE_BLOCKS` list that must be empty at the first outside build. | Data | ADR-0006 + ADR-0016 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-035 | Post-restore verification runs in EXACT mode, comparing the K-4 `state_hash`, or in REBASED mode (migration, stripped or filled block, unknown `hash_algo`, `REBASE_BLOCKS` member), which records a baseline hash in `campaign.json` keyed by generation, build and registry epoch and compares later restores against it, without rewriting the save file. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 + ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-036 | An `ItemInstance` is persisted only in the Item DB canonical `to_array()` form (a 6-element Array: String then five ints), never as a Dictionary, and a holder block whose restore sees a non-canonical instance fails with its named reason. | Data | ADR-0006 + ADR-0007 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-037 | Saves identify players only by PlayerId keys in a header `seats` list (non-empty, unique, in range, covering every PER_PLAYER key); no peer id and no binding or controlled-entity table is stored, and the kernel rebuilds bindings after restore so the first RUNNING tick passes authority. | Core | ADR-0002 + ADR-0016 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-saveload-038 | Save/Load reads only its granted observation set (clock, per-seat loco and vitals state, Graph failures, optional Attention view, Assembly breach and shape-change reads, change notifications), never calls block mutators or `restore()`, uses an injected monotonic source only for I/O timeouts, and capture plus writer leave `state_hash(ALL_SIM)` unchanged. | Core | ADR-0006 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-saveload-039 | Save/Load intercepts the window-close request (`auto_accept_quit` false) and routes it by kernel state: RUNNING and PAUSED write a QUIT generation then quit, LOADING defers, SAVING settles then dedupes or writes, DOWNED, FAULTED and boot screens write nothing, a failed or timed-out write holds the close, and the handler never blocks a frame. | Input | ADR-0006 + ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-saveload-040 | On the frame whose boundary service enters RUNNING from LOADING, PAUSED or SAVING, the tick driver discards the accumulated frame delta so `T` does not advance, and advances by at most one tick on the next frame, so no catch-up burst follows a restore or autosave. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-saveload-041 | The `campaign.json` manifest is a non-authoritative hint: if missing, corrupt or disagreeing with the files it is rebuilt from generation headers, demoted and quarantined generations stay excluded, and `gen_next` is computed from ring, quarantine, demoted files and manifest together. | Save/Load | ADR-0006 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-saveload-042 | In dev builds Save/Load registers exactly one `save_load` diagnostics channel with the Debug Overlay during BOOTING, whose `pull()` is read-only and adds no kernel or block reads beyond the observation set, and no channel is registered in release builds. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |

### Debug Overlay (True Values) (`debug`)
GDD: `design/gdd/debug-overlay-true-values.md` | 45 requirements

| Requirement ID | Requirement | Domain | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TR-debug-001 | The Overlay is exactly one kernel subscriber of class `DIAGNOSTIC`; it submits no command, calls no mutator or owner debug-write function, and owns no block, hook, read view or RNG stream. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-002 | The single `DIAGNOSTIC` handler only copies the P5 batch `(block_id, tick, change_keys)` (merged per block per catch-up frame, stamped with the frame's last tick) and marks the model dirty; it does no formatting, classification or I/O. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-003 | All Overlay code lives under two excluded source roots (observer root `src/observer/`, dev root `src/debug/`); the public release export excludes both by resource filter, verified by a CI file-list check, and the playtest preset excludes only the dev root. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-004 | The playtest preset is the release template plus the custom feature tag `playtest`; the composition root forces `HIDDEN` mode at launch and registers only the `debug_overlay_mark` action. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-005 | `OverlayModel` is a pure, scene-tree-free class built from (diagnostics snapshot, read views, channels), holds copies not live block references, and performs zero `FileAccess` I/O (observer lines go to an injected `ObserverSink`); it is unit-testable headless. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-006 | All Overlay state (mode, page, cursor, viewer, pins, marks, defect ring, history windows) is dev-session only: never a block, never in a snapshot, never in `state_hash`, never saved across launches in MVP. | Save/Load | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-007 | With the Overlay `FULL` versus absent over the same scripted run, the `state_hash` sequence is identical, the Filter draw path is unchanged, and honest-path `perceived` calls allocate nothing. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-008 | The Overlay never writes, rewinds, replays, sonifies, uploads telemetry, appears in accessibility/rebinding/AccessKit enumerations, or exists in a public release build. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-009 | The view is a root-viewport `CanvasLayer` at pipeline layer 4 (above HUD layer 2 and chart table/menus layer 3), integer-scaled at `H_default = max(k,3)`, receiving no Filter pass, lighting, camera jolt or grade; non-interactive panels use `MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE` and never take gameplay focus. | Rendering | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-010 | The Overlay root and model driver run with `PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS` so it stays drawable and refreshing in all six kernel states (including `PAUSED` and `FAULTED`, where it shows the FAULT record). | UI | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-011 | The Overlay reads other systems only through their published read views or registered diagnostics channels (e.g. Graph `true_value(kind,id)`, Suit `true_value(key,player_id)`, `sanity_view`, `sanity.ledger`, `attention.state`, `horror.view`, Filter `perceived`), never into a block it has no view of. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-012 | The Overlay is the sole sanctioned non-owner caller of `true_value` and reader of the Sanity ledger (via `ledger_listed(player_id, lie_id)`); on a `MIRROR` the ledger is unreadable so affected refs classify `UNAVAILABLE` reason `NO_LEDGER`. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-013 | `register_channel(declaration: Dictionary) -> Dictionary {ok, reason}` is valid only while the kernel is `BOOTING`; later calls return `LATE_REGISTRATION` and the channel is `REJECTED`; registrations are bounded by `DIAG_CHANNELS_MAX` 64 and sourced from a data-driven `expected_channels` list (14 siblings; Boarding registers none). | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-014 | Every channel `pull()` returns `{channel_id, rows, complete}` validated against the closed enum `DIAG_ERR` (NULL_RESULT, NOT_A_DICTIONARY, SCHEMA_MISMATCH, LIMIT_EXCEEDED, INVALID_CALLABLE, INCOMPLETE); failures yield a `DEFECT` row and suspend the channel after `DIAG_PANEL_ERR_LATCH_N` 3 consecutive failures (no exceptions in GDScript). | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-015 | `desync_classifier` is a pure first-match-wins function with fixed order (`UNAVAILABLE` first, `DEFECT_UNCAUSED` catch-all) using exact integer equality `p_int == tv` for HONEST, run at class-fold cadence `CLASS_FOLD_HZ = TICK_RATE / SANITY_DRIFT_DIVISOR = 5` over the classified set (cap `DIAG_FOLD_REFS_MAX` 48). | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-016 | Grace and window timers (`DIAG_SILENT_LIE_GRACE_TICKS`, `DIAG_SKEW_GRACE_TICKS`, `DIAG_NO_LEAK_GRACE_TICKS`, `DIAG_RETURN_WINDOW_TICKS`) are denominated in kernel ticks and advance only when `T` changes, so refresh rate, throttling or `Engine.time_scale` never alter verdicts. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-017 | Numeral refresh is driven by accumulated process-delta (never a wall-clock read) at `DIAG_REFRESH_HZ` 10 (ladder {10,5,2}); markers and debug draws update per frame; the Overlay reads no wall clock (`launch_id` supplied by composition root). | Threading/Timing | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-018 | A restore/new world/transit is detected by the `LOADING`->`RUNNING` edge: all history windows clear, the classifier reports `UNAVAILABLE` reason `SETTLING` for `DIAG_POST_RESTORE_SETTLE_TICKS` 12 kernel ticks, while the defect ring, marks and log survive. | Save/Load | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-019 | The Overlay never hashes anything itself; it displays the kernel `diagnostics` accessor's `state_hash`, `hash_T`, `hash_algo` (H64 = first 8 bytes of SHA-256) and age `T - hash_T`, with the kernel hash run outside P3 every `DIAG_HASH_CADENCE_TICKS` 60 (phased off the class fold). | Core | ADR-0005 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-020 | Dirty-key accounting records per delivered batch the key count (or `ALL` collapse) against `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256 with peak-hold, warning at `DIAG_DIRTY_WARN_PM` 500 and alerting at `DIAG_DIRTY_ALERT_PM` 750 per mille. | Core | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-021 | `P-HOOKS` displays the kernel's resolved hook order and per-hook `t_hook` (displayed, not recomputed) plus derived `graph_hook_tick` summed over Graph hooks at stagger offsets 0, 2, 4, with rolling P99 over `DIAG_HOOK_WINDOW_RUNS` 600 against `GRAPH_HOOK_BUDGET_MS`; breaches are `WARN`, never `DEFECT`, as the figure is dev-template-indicative. | Performance | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-022 | The Overlay's own cost stays at or below `OVERLAY_CPU_BUDGET_US` 1000 us/frame mean and `OVERLAY_FRAME_CEILING_US` 2000 us worst frame (budget owned by the technical director, OQ-DO-13), dev builds only, with dirty-driven view updates and a refresh-rate degradation ladder. | Performance | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-023 | The Overlay owns a non-screen observer sink writing `user://debug/observer-<launch_id>.log` (kinds LAUNCH, MODE, KERNEL_STATE, ATTN_PHASE, SANITY_STAGE, VIEW, HASH, CHANNEL, MARK, DEFECT), in every mode but `OFF` (MARK always), capped by `DIAG_LOG_MAX_LINES` 20000 and `DIAG_LOG_MAX_FILES` 4, plus a latched defect ring of `DIAG_DEFECT_RING_MAX` 64. | Save/Load | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-024 | `dump` writes a plain-text snapshot of every page to `user://debug/` (retained count capped by `DIAG_DUMP_MAX_FILES` 20) and `mark` stamps up to `DIAG_MARK_MAX` 8 observer marks, refusing further marks rather than dropping older ones. | Save/Load | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-025 | Four modes (`OFF`, `FULL`, `OBSERVER_STRIP`, `HIDDEN`) switch within one rendered frame without altering `T`; `FULL` and the strip must never appear on a tester's screen, and the strip shows only numbers (no ref, pair or ledger field). | UI | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-026 | Dev-only input actions (`debug_overlay_toggle`, `_page_next`, `_page_prev`, `_mode`, `_dump`, `_reveal`, `_mark`, `_cursor_next`, `_cursor_prev`) are registered by the Input Adapter only under the dev predicate and forwarded by a host-local call, never as commands or visible rebinding entries. | Input | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-027 | The Overlay is HOST-authoritative: it addresses viewers by `PlayerId` never `peer_id`, and on a `MIRROR` shows only the own-seat pair classified `UNAVAILABLE`; `HOST_MIRROR_MISMATCH` is defined but deferred (Vertical Slice B). | Networking | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-028 | A dev-only render probe (`drawn_value(ref)` as integer pre-snap millidegrees, `drawn_leak(ref)` per mille) from In-World Instruments detects Filter-bypass (`DEFECT_BYPASS`, tolerance `LEAK_EPS_MDEG` 250 inclusive, integer compare); without it, bypass is undetectable. | Rendering | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-029 | World debug draws use `draw_line` width >= `OVERLAY_DEBUG_DRAW_WIDTH_PX` 1 base pixel, and severity is conveyed by text tag first, with the UI palette only (Alarm Red `#E0102A` only beside `DEFECT` marks and for `NO_MASK`). | Rendering | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-030 | Seven MVP pages (`P-KERNEL`, `P-HOOKS`, `P-STATE`, `P-TRUTH`, `P-SANITY`, `P-ATTENTION`, `P-FRAME`) are data tables, plus generic `P-CHANNELS`; sanity seats set by `force_*` carry a `FORCED` tag. | UI | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-031 | The kernel `diagnostics` accessor must publish, allocation-free per tick in release, T, state, boot progress and hook-cycle error, per-issuer queue depth, command-log tail, reject counters, lag, the six-field FAULT record, resolved hook order with per-hook t_hook and over-budget flag, save_status, and a forced hash at restore. | Core | ADR-0003 + ADR-0005 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-debug-032 | Per-hook t_hook samples are delivered once per (T, restore epoch) and a sample straddling a restore epoch or delivered twice is dropped and counted rather than sampled. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-debug-033 | Every state-block owner optionally publishes `block_hash()` and a `schema_version`; the Overlay shows owner values, shows n/a where none is published, and never hashes itself, so HASH_DRIFT names a block only from an owner-published hash. | Data | ADR-0005 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-debug-034 | The Perception Filter must publish a read surface for the Overlay: per-(ref, viewer) Perceived record, lie_intent, lie_targets(ref), t_return_max, q_display calibration, memo-hit counter, the 15 PF-41 counters and the ref-to-subject map; unpublished items degrade to UNVERIFIED or NO_RESOLVER, never to a defect. | Core | ADR-0024 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-035 | Sanity must publish `sanity_view`, `sanity.state` with an explicit `forced` flag, and the ledger `lies[]` schema including away rows via `ledger_listed`; the Overlay reads the forced flag directly and never infers forcing from level. | Core | ADR-0025 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-036 | The Overlay refresh runs with a process_priority after the tick driver and reads every source once at refresh start, so all reads carry the same T; a mismatched read raises a read_straddle counter and a DEFECT that also works in release-template builds where assert() is stripped. | Threading/Timing | ADR-0001 + ADR-0031 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-debug-037 | The composition root loads the observer and dev roots only by a guarded runtime load() after ResourceLoader.exists(); no preload, scene, resource or class_name reference to the dev root exists elsewhere, the dev root declares no class_name, and a release process contains no global-class or script entry from either root. | Core | ADR-0031 + ADR-0010 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-038 | CI lints enforce reader allow-lists: sanity.ledger readable only by Sanity and the observer root, raw attention level and tell_remaining only by named readers, true_value only by owners, Filter and the Overlay, and none routed to a PRESENTATION-class subscriber. | Core | ADR-0010 + ADR-0022 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-039 | The composition root and kernel must offer testability seams: injectable dev-tag provider, subscriber id exposed at mutator and WriteScope entry, injectable frame delta and clock, stubbable Filter, Sanity and Graph/Suit true_value sources, a harness hook forcing state_hash every tick, call-spy seams, and an injected ObserverSink. | Core | ADR-0010 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | ⚠️ Partial |
| TR-debug-040 | The observer sink writes only at fixed triggers (a class fold with pending lines, a MARK, entry to PAUSED or FAULTED, WM_CLOSE_REQUEST), at most one append per frame; an unwritable user://debug/ drops to the in-memory ring with a NOTE and never stops the Overlay. | Save/Load | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-041 | In HIDDEN and OBSERVER_STRIP the Overlay collects only the observer set (no channel pull, hook-window or P-FRAME reads), calls the classifier exactly n_refs times per fold, never skips collection when throttled, and places a fold and a refresh due on one frame on consecutive frames. | Performance | ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-042 | Engine capabilities on 4.7.2 must be proven by spike before the debug-gate ADR: export resource-filter exclusion, OS.has_feature for dev and playtest tags, Viewport.get_render_info per-viewport draw counts, AccessKit per-node opt-out, and GDScript H64 hash cost over 100-300 KiB against STEP_BUDGET_MS. | Rendering | ADR-0031 + ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-043 | World debug draws render from a node in the world viewport unshaded and unaffected by lighting, camera jolt or sanity stage, with a layer-4 projection fallback; they exist only in FULL, and leaving FULL yields a zero frame diff against a never-toggled frame. | Rendering | ADR-0008 + ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-044 | All DIAG_* and OVERLAY_* knobs load from a data file with a startup assertion for ranges and cross-knob constraints (e.g. P99 min samples within window, alert at or above warn, ladder strictly decreasing), clamping and logging a NOTE on violation. | Data | ADR-0007 + ADR-0031 | — | ❌ Gap |
| TR-debug-045 | Rendered-pixel tests run only in a named windowed CI job with a real window and GPU; the headless job reports them SKIPPED, and each asserts a nonzero draw or pixel count so it cannot pass vacuously. | Rendering | ADR-0010 + ADR-0008 | — | ❌ Gap |
