## Partial Coverage

| Requirement ID | Planned ADR | ADR Coverage | Requirement |
|---|---|---|---|
| TR-cmd-013 | ADR-0016 + ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0016) | The kernel rebuilds binding-table half (b) (PlayerId -> {(source block_id, entity_id)}) itself as the last step of every LOADING pass from registered binding sources in ascending block_id order; half (b) is never saved and conflicts fault LOADING. |
| TR-cmd-027 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0005 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0005) | Commands apply in ascending (queued_tick, host_order) with apply_tick >= queued_tick + 1 while running; from an initial snapshot, same PLAYER command stream plus control-plane request log and same (save bytes, restore_epoch) yields identical canonical hash at tick N on the same build and platform. |
| TR-cmd-034 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0032 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0032) | Mode HOST ticks and applies authoritatively while MIRROR applies nothing, with an identical public API; blocks declare `repl_hint` (HOST_ONLY/SHARED_SUMMARY/OWNER_PRIVATE) now though unused until VS-B. |
| TR-cmd-037 | ADR-0016 | ADR-0003 (needs ADR-0016) | A new game runs, inside LOADING and a WriteScope, every `default_state()`, then each owner's `init_world(world_seed)` in `restore_after` order, then `post_restore()`, then the binding rebuild; `init_world` never runs on a load from save. |
| TR-cmd-043 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0005 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0005) | Sequence numbers are kernel-stamped per (issuer_peer, origin) space with `host_order` global and monotonic; only a test or remote ingress supplies `seq` (DUPLICATE_SEQ on repeat); `correlation` is echoed in results but excluded from canonical bytes and hashes; T, seq and host_order are 64-bit and never wrap, asserted at boot. |
| TR-cmd-044 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0032 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0032) | Admission evaluates terms in a fixed order and reports the first failing reason deterministically; remote issuers are rate-limited by a tick-based window (REMOTE_RATE_LIMIT per RATE_WINDOW_TICKS) that does not slide while PAUSED, the local issuer is never limited, and SYSTEM_RESERVE slots are reserved for SYSTEM commands. |
| TR-cmd-045 | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | Binding-table changes requested during tick T take effect at T+1 (every authority check in T uses the pre-change table), an unbound PlayerId's pending OWNED_BY_ISSUER commands are Rejected ACTOR_NOT_OWNED rather than dropped, and recreating a binding resets that issuer's seq counters while host_order continues. |
| TR-cmd-046 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0009 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0003 (needs ADR-0009) | Blocks flagged `interpolated` expose previous and current values for `tick_alpha` lerp; `commit_baseline` rolls only a derived cache outside snapshot and hash; resume, load and new-game call `snap_baseline` so previous equals current and tick_alpha reads 1.0 while PAUSED. |
| TR-lsg-034 | ADR-0003 + ADR-0021 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | Every structural mutator (M1, weld_join, cut_detach) emits exactly one P5 dirty-key batch of its declared keys; can_join and read_view emit none; and a GraphFault mid-mutator emits no P5 notification and Drops the tick's commands with FAULTED. |
| TR-emitter-035 | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | Every submitted emitter command reaches exactly one terminal result; a same-state SYSTEM `set_switch`/`set_beacon` is Applied with no state change, no dirty key and no `last_toggle_tick` write; a mutator called outside a WriteScope is rejected with no state change in Release. |
| TR-emitter-038 | ADR-0003 + ADR-0005 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | emitter.place and all mutators do nothing while the kernel is RESTORING or FAULTED; an unsupported `schema_version` on restore is a reported failure; the block exposes `canonical_bytes()` byte-identical across round trips and `block_hash()` once kernel amendment K-1 lands. |
| TR-light-033 | ADR-0017 + ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | The Emitter State commit path must itself enforce flash spacing (`SWITCH_MIN_SPACING_TICKS` 12) and count a defect when committed switches would breach the 3 flashes per second limit, because a command-level spacing guard alone is proven insufficient under arming-latency jitter. |
| TR-pc-034 | ADR-0020 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | Every channel-owner hook registers `after: [controller.step]`; an interrupt (move intent, cap change, stagger, target invalidation, body transfer, restore) reaches the owner only as the Controller clearing `channel_ref` through state, never as a command result, and the owner aborts before accruing progress on that tick. |
| TR-pc-037 | ADR-0006 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | The registered `controller.state` schema, its published read view and its snapshot payload must agree field by field (incl. `cap_seen`, `ramp_ticks`, `walk_ticks`, `last_octant`, `decel_k`, `stagger_end_tick`, `death_tick`, `channel_body`); `cap_seen` persists so a restore never re-fires the cap falling-edge stagger. |
| TR-pc-038 | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | Render-time confirm-bar and tap-window timers advance by frame delta clamped to `CONFIRM_DT_MAX_S`, are frozen (effective delta zero) while the kernel is PAUSED, and resume where they stopped; idle animation frames advance from sim tick `T`, not wall time. |
| TR-pc-039 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0009 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | Controller and Presenter outputs (sim blocks, `set_move_intent`/`set_aim` submission logs, Presenter frame/position/light logs) are byte-identical across render cadences of 30, 60, 120 and 144 fps, across calm/tell/breach/sanity-stage fixtures, and with `aim_indicator` on or off; a recorded input-trace replay format is required. |
| TR-pc-044 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0003 (not claimed) | The Adapter submits no intents or commands while the kernel is PAUSED and holds timers and channel progress unchanged; solo `chart_table` issues `request_pause` through the menu path while co-op opens an overlay with the kernel RUNNING. |
| TR-interact-031 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0020 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | The fifteen Interaction command types are registered at boot with coalesce NONE, actor_binding OWNED_BY_ISSUER, actor_source body.state, max_age_ticks 120 and not allowed_when_paused; none is named interact or use_item, and the Interaction public surface is exactly sync_section, place_loose and open_channel plus focus_view, channel_view and last_salvage_T (boot-registry test). |
| TR-interact-032 | ADR-0003 + ADR-0020 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | interaction.step is registered with divisor 1, offset 0 and after [controller.step, emitter.place] using verbatim hook ids; it runs Class A mutators M6, M7, M11, M12 in P3 inside a kernel WriteScope, M13 only in P2 from set_opening, and the Graph reads a Class A change at the next multiple of 6 (lag 1 to 6 ticks). |
| TR-interact-043 | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | Two commands applied on one tick (two throws, two seats on one subject) are processed in seq order with the second validate seeing the first apply; a press during a channel or a rejected press queues nothing (ACTION_BUFFER_TICKS 0), and a cancel_channel wins over a same-tick abort cause. |
| TR-ftrail-039 | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | When the kernel is PAUSED, extent, animation frame index and all trail animation stay unchanged because every animated quantity derives from present_time, which must hold still while paused. |
| TR-suit-003 | ADR-0027 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0027) | Per-seat data is keyed only by PlayerId (int) and item `def_id`; no peer_id, node path or engine object appears in the block. |
| TR-suit-034 | ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | The `equip_tool` descriptor is origin PLAYER, `actor_binding` OWNED_BY_ISSUER, `actor_source` `body.state`, `coalesce` NONE, `max_age_ticks` 120, and over every enumerated state combination a command that passes `validate` never fails `apply`. |
| TR-suit-038 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0027 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | `suit.vitals` reads only committed state after all same-tick commands have landed, so multiple damage and patch commands in one tick produce one final `integrity_bp`, and a hit committed in run n shows its cue in run n with the first debit in run n+1. |
| TR-section-036 | ADR-0003 + ADR-0021 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | A failing `can_*` changes no state, marks no dirty key and consumes no id; reason sets are closed per validator (including `CAPACITY` for build and detach), surface as `VALIDATION_FAILED(layout.<REASON>)`, never include door-close tokens, and a fault inside an apply sends the kernel to `FAULTED`. |
| TR-shipbody-042 | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | A SIM hook may read Body's `moved` or `pos_prev` only if registered `after: [body.integrate]`; a lint rejects any other SIM reader, so the Controller's `controller.noise` sees the same tick's `moved`. |
| TR-assembly-042 | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | Boot verifies the _process-driven TickDriver runs at TICK_RATE 60 with a catch-up bound of MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME 4 and refuses to start otherwise, because every Assembly duration assumes it. |
| TR-boarding-040 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0022 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | Same-tick ordering of cut completion, channel cancel or stagger, the Attention integrator run, and abort versus breach must be deterministic: the yield and an open tell can both exist after the run that crosses the threshold, and a cancel on a stagger tick writes the ledger once and plays no abort cue. |
| TR-webmap-034 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0026 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | Timing constants derive from the kernel's published `queue_drain_ticks` (SLIP_MAX_TICKS = 2 x it - 1; IN_POD_SLACK_TICKS = 2 x SLIP_MAX_TICKS; ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS - 1 > SLIP_MAX_TICKS), recomputed by a blocking CI lint that fails the build when the kernel value changes without a GDD update. |
| TR-horror-033 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0015 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | Seat commands issued during tick t are applied by body.integrate in that tick and horror.step reads positions after body.integrate; this tick-indexing convention is pinned by a fixture, and a measured input-to-display latency above INPUT_LATENCY_TICKS 3 on RM-DESKTOP/RM-DECK release exports forces UNFOLD_LO to 100. |
| TR-sanity-035 | ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | A WriteScope audit hook must show every write made by the hook, record_falsification, record_harm_event and the debug controls targets a block id beginning with `sanity.`, and no other registry entry (command, input surface, PLAYER-origin) is written. |
| TR-sanity-036 | ADR-0025 + ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | A mutator called on a tick not aligned to the 5 Hz hook (T mod 12 not 0) stamps absolute runs as T div 12 + 1, writes its ledger effect immediately so state_hash matches a control at every tick, and updates sanity.perception only at the next hook run (at most 11 ticks later). |
| TR-percfilter-035 | ADR-0024 + ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | Presentation slew and ramp advance with delta clamped to `DT_SLEW_MAX_S` (0.1 s) so a render hitch cannot snap a needle or produce a flash pair, while `lie_tau` keeps advancing by true simulation time. |
| TR-hud-040 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0030 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | The HUD is unmounted until the first committed tick, draws nothing and calls neither `view()` nor `perceived` before it or during kernel LOADING, holds its last model through DEAD and LOADING, and adds no scene node on death or restore except the record line. |
| TR-charttable-003 | ADR-0030 + ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0030) | `PauseGate` is the single adapter to the kernel control plane (`request_pause`, `request_resume`, `request_shutdown`): SOLO pauses on open and resumes on close; OVERLAY (VS-B) makes no kernel call; no chart/pick/panel/menu rule may read `kernel.state == PAUSED` except `PauseGate.can_save_quit`. |
| TR-charttable-004 | ADR-0030 + ADR-0002 | ADR-0002 (needs ADR-0030) | `PauseGate` re-issues the latest desired pause state until the kernel matches, so a same-boundary `request_pause`/`request_resume` pair leaves no wedged half-open state. |
| TR-charttable-035 | ADR-0030 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | Boot-flow screens (notice, main menu, UI-5, UI-7/8/9, load hold) run while the kernel is not RUNNING with no session: they make no `request_pause`, reference no Sanity, Filter or Attention symbol, are not children of the Filter viewport, and Quit from any of them calls only `request_shutdown`. |
| TR-audio-035 | ADR-0029 + ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | When one poll holds several simultaneous facts, requests are created in a fixed order (annunciator, strike, BREACH_ENTER), each on a free or reserved voice with none dropped, and every audio request frame is at or before the first visual draw frame of the same event. |
| TR-saveload-033 | ADR-0003 + ADR-0006 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | Every owner `restore()` returns `(ok, reason, detail)` with `reason` from its declared closed `<BLOCK>_RESTORE_*` set, never clamps out-of-range data, and leaves the live block byte-identical on failure; a non-conforming return or an owner failure during LOADING sends the kernel to FAULTED with no in-line retry and a persisted per-generation failure counter. |
| TR-saveload-037 | ADR-0002 + ADR-0016 | ADR-0002 (not claimed) | Saves identify players only by PlayerId keys in a header `seats` list (non-empty, unique, in range, covering every PER_PLAYER key); no peer id and no binding or controlled-entity table is stored, and the kernel rebuilds bindings after restore so the first RUNNING tick passes authority. |
| TR-saveload-038 | ADR-0006 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | Save/Load reads only its granted observation set (clock, per-seat loco and vitals state, Graph failures, optional Attention view, Assembly breach and shape-change reads, change notifications), never calls block mutators or `restore()`, uses an injected monotonic source only for I/O timeouts, and capture plus writer leave `state_hash(ALL_SIM)` unchanged. |
| TR-saveload-039 | ADR-0006 + ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | Save/Load intercepts the window-close request (`auto_accept_quit` false) and routes it by kernel state: RUNNING and PAUSED write a QUIT generation then quit, LOADING defers, SAVING settles then dedupes or writes, DOWNED, FAULTED and boot screens write nothing, a failed or timed-out write holds the close, and the handler never blocks a frame. |
| TR-saveload-040 | ADR-0001 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | On the frame whose boundary service enters RUNNING from LOADING, PAUSED or SAVING, the tick driver discards the accumulated frame delta so `T` does not advance, and advances by at most one tick on the next frame, so no catch-up burst follows a restore or autosave. |
| TR-debug-031 | ADR-0003 + ADR-0005 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | The kernel `diagnostics` accessor must publish, allocation-free per tick in release, T, state, boot progress and hook-cycle error, per-issuer queue depth, command-log tail, reject counters, lag, the six-field FAULT record, resolved hook order with per-hook t_hook and over-budget flag, save_status, and a forced hash at restore. |
| TR-debug-032 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0003 (not claimed) | Per-hook t_hook samples are delivered once per (T, restore epoch) and a sample straddling a restore epoch or delivered twice is dropped and counted rather than sampled. |
| TR-debug-033 | ADR-0005 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | Every state-block owner optionally publishes `block_hash()` and a `schema_version`; the Overlay shows owner values, shows n/a where none is published, and never hashes itself, so HASH_DRIFT names a block only from an owner-published hash. |
| TR-debug-036 | ADR-0001 + ADR-0031 | ADR-0001 (not claimed) | The Overlay refresh runs with a process_priority after the tick driver and reads every source once at refresh start, so all reads carry the same T; a mismatched read raises a read_straddle counter and a DEFECT that also works in release-template builds where assert() is stripped. |
| TR-debug-039 | ADR-0010 + ADR-0003 | ADR-0003 (not claimed) | The composition root and kernel must offer testability seams: injectable dev-tag provider, subscriber id exposed at mutator and WriteScope entry, injectable frame delta and clock, stubbable Filter, Sanity and Graph/Suit true_value sources, a harness hook forcing state_hash every tick, call-spy seams, and an injected ObserverSink. |

## Superseded Requirements

None. This is the first registration; no GDD changed after an ADR claimed a requirement.
